<meta>This page continues the cosmological and mystical exploration of the world's structure, transitioning from the celestial zodiac on the previous page to the terrestrial and subterranean realms. It focuses on the judgment of creatures, the seven levels of the earth, the four directions, and the relationship between Torah study and protection from Hell (Gehinnom).</meta>

# The Book of Raziel the Great

...as it is said, "The mountains quaked and all the hills melted" <note>original: Nahum 1:5</note>. And there are 665 types of creatures upon it. Among human beings, there is one who resembles a lion in his body but has a human face; some have a lion's head and a human body; some have the head of a serpent; some have the head of an ox. There are those with two heads, or three heads, with four hands, one body, one belly, and twelve legs. 

In the future, the Holy One, Blessed be He, shall judge the world <note>original: "Tevel"—one of the seven names for the earth</note>, as it is said, "He shall judge the world in righteousness" <note>original: Psalm 9:9</note>. He shall revive those human beings from the dust who made themselves as if they were dwellers in the dust <note>meaning: those who were humble during their lives</note>. He shall apportion a good reward to them and grant them an inheritance even at His throne, as it is said, "He raises the poor from the dust... to make them inherit a throne of glory" <note>original: 1 Samuel 2:8</note>.

Above the world:
Abyss upon Abyss, 
Chaos upon Chaos <note>original: "Tohu"</note>, 
Void upon Void <note>original: "Bohu"</note>, 
Sea upon Sea, 
Water upon Water, 
The World <note>term: "Cheled" (another name for the earth/the temporal world)</note>.

**The World** (*Cheled*) is suspended in the storm-wind and bound in the palm <note>original: "Kaph," perhaps referring to the hollow of God's hand</note>. The angel appointed over the world is named **Chaldiel**. Why is his name called Chaldiel? Because human beings tarnish <note>original: "machlidim," a pun on the name Chaldiel and the word for rust or tarnish</note> the world with their evil deeds. *Cheled* refers specifically to the body. Upon the world are humans, domestic animals, wild beasts, birds, fish, crawling things, swarming things, the wicked, the righteous, the Torah, good deeds, and the fear of Heaven, as it is said, "Hear this, all you peoples; give ear, all you inhabitants of the world <note>original: "Cheled"</note>" <note>original: Psalm 49:2</note>.

Thus we have counted the seven earths and the seven abysses between them. And there are four winds <note>or directions</note> of the world:

**East:** From the East, light comes forth to the world.
**South:** From the South, dews of blessing come forth.
**West:** From the West, storehouses of snow, storehouses of hail, cold, and rains come forth.
**North:** This direction was not completed. It is the wind of damagers, terrors, spirits, and demons, as it is said, "From the North, evil shall break forth" <note>original: Jeremiah 1:14</note>. Why was it not completed? So that if a person should say he is a god, they can say to him: "Complete this direction which was left open." For the Divine Presence <note>term: "Shekhinah"</note> is in the West. 

Every turn you make should be toward the right. See the world before Him: East, South, West. This corresponds to the seven "precedences" written in the Torah, to inform you that through the order of the **Mishnah** <note>the primary collection of Jewish oral law</note>, He took counsel and created His world in the six days of creation. 

He created His world to stand for six thousand years. The righteous who occupy themselves with the Torah—which is described by six terms: (1) The Torah of the Lord is perfect, (2) the testimony of the Lord, (3) the precepts of the Lord, (4) the commandment of the Lord, (5) the fear of the Lord, (6) the ordinances of the Lord <note>original: Psalm 19:8-10</note>—they shall be blessed with the seven blessings of the Priestly Blessing: (1) May He bless you, (2) and keep you, (3) may He shine, (4) and be gracious to you, (5) may He lift up, (6) and give you (7) peace. And they are protected by the seven mentions of "guarding" <note>original: "shmira"</note>: (1) The Guardian of Israel, (2) the Lord is your guardian, (3) the Lord shall guard you, (4) He shall guard your soul, (5) He shall guard your going out, (6) and your coming in, (7) and the seventh is "Behold, He who guards..." 

In the merit of those who guard the Torah, they are empowered; for there are 53 <note>the text says "Gan," which is gematria for 53, though the modern count is 54</note> portions in the Five Books of the Torah. The measurement of the Tablets was six handbreadths by six. By studying the six orders of the Mishnah, they are saved from **Gehinnom** <note>Hell</note>, where it is written, "A land of thick darkness... without any order" <note>original: Job 10:22</note>. 

The Tablet was six handbreadths by six strips. If the length of each is six, and you divide each handbreadth into six parts, you have 36 handbreadths. Three times 36 is 108, which is the numerical value <note>term: "Gematria"</note> of **Gehinnom** <note>G=3, Y=10, H=5, N=50, M=40; Total = 108</note>, from which they are saved.

Know that **Gehinnom** and the **Garden of Eden** are adjacent, and only a single wall separates them. The Garden of Eden is on the Northeast side, as it is said, "And Your hidden treasure <note>original: "tzafun"—also means North</note> fills their belly." The sun is red in the West because of the brilliance of the flowers and the stones of fire in Eden. On the Northwest side is Gehinnom, with rivers of heat. In the East, the sun is red as blood because it passes by the **River of Fire** <note>term: "Nehar Dinur"</note>. 

**Alexandros** <note>note: Likely Alexander the Great, referring to the medieval legend of his journey to the gates of Paradise</note> provides proof, as he reached the gate of the Garden of Eden which is beneath the firmament. But there is another, more weightier Garden that enters the world, while the rest is outside the firmament. Their "shells" <note>term: "Kelipot" (husks/shells)</note> contain the River of Fire. Above these shells, the Garden of Eden surrounds them, for there are two shells for Eden and Hell. Above the shells is the Throne of Glory and the Living Creatures <note>term: "Chayot" (angels)</note>. 

From the intensity of the boiling heat, the sun, moon, and constellations roll like a boiling pot. Because of this heat, they descend to the other side; therefore they go from East to West and return in a circuit. The firmament above us is a journey of 500 years. It is called "World" <note>original: "Olam"</note>, which has the numerical value of 146... <note>unclear gematria calculation</note>. 

At the edge of this earth is a very high mountain; from there and beyond is the Garden of Eden. The mountain separates the world from the Garden of Eden. Through that mountain, the angels brought the **first fruits** <note>original: "bikkurim"</note>. The mountains are placed upon the thickness of the firmament beneath the earth. 

There are 22 letters of the Torah... <note>The text here performs a complex "triangular" summation of the Hebrew alphabet: 1, 1+2=3, 1+2+3=6, etc.</note>. The sum reaches 6,000, which corresponds to the six thousand years the world exists. In the merit of the 3 Patriarchs, 4 Matriarchs, the 7 Heavens were created, and the 7 Earths, and the 12 Tribes corresponding to the 12 Constellations. 

**Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob** in gematria = 638.
**Sarah, Rebecca, Rachel, and Leah** in gematria = 1,086.
**Reuben, Simeon, Levi, Judah, Issachar, Zebulun, Benjamin, Dan, Joseph, Naphtali, Gad, and Asher** in gematria = 3,166.
**Israel and Jeshurun** = 1,107.
**Torah, Prophets, and Writings** = 3.
All these together equal the 6,000 years that the world stands. 

**Ephraim, Manasseh, and Israel** in *Atbash* <note>term: "Atbash" (a cipher where the first letter is replaced by the last, etc.)</note> = 1,038. This brings the total to 7,000, representing the 6,000 years of the world's existence and the 7th millennium of chaos <note>original: "Tohu"</note>. Therefore, there were 7,000 in the days of Ahab who did not bow to Baal, and 7,000 in the days of Jehoahaz. 

Behold, we have counted the seven earths and the seven abysses between them, and the seven heavens. It is written: "The Lord by wisdom founded the earth" <note>original: Proverbs 3:19</note>, for there are creatures full of wisdom.

<summary>This page details the mystical geography of the seven earths and abysses, the locations of Eden and Gehinnom (Hell), and the symbolic mathematics linking the Hebrew alphabet and biblical figures to the 7,000-year lifespan of the universe.</summary>
<keywords>Chaldiel, Cheled, Gehinnom, Garden of Eden, River of Fire, Nehar Dinur, Gematria, Alexander the Great, Four Directions, Priestly Blessing, Seven Earths</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the cosmological hierarchy established on the previous page. # The Book of Raziel the Great

A Song of Ascents <note>original: "Shir HaMa'alot"</note>. Aside from every Song of Ascents, whether for a generation or for three, that one is not to be counted. And so it is for the Lord, regarding the Name that is written upon the pottery shard with nineteen letters, and the name Y-H-W <note>a three-letter variant of the Tetragrammaton</note> and Y-H, which makes five letters. By these, the Abyss was calmed from its cry. The Holy One, Blessed be He, looked upon his humility and signaled to the wind, revealing all the dust that was upon the Rock <note>The Foundation Stone or "Even Shetiyah"</note>. The Holy One, Blessed be He, said to David <note>original: "Dor," likely a shorthand for David (D-V-D)</note>: "Gird your loins and build the foundation of the Temple." 

David looked at the Rock, upon which were written nineteen letters of the Explicit Name <note>term: "Shem HaMephorash" — the specific, hidden name of God used in miracles</note>, and around it was written: *“May the glory of the Lord endure forever; let the Lord rejoice in His works”* <note>original: Psalm 104:31</note>. It was further written: "David and Solomon shall build the Temple." Immediately, he reached out his hand to take the Rock. The Rock said to him: "Do not touch me, for the name of my Creator is engraved upon me, and I am sealed by the Holy One, Blessed be He." And do not wonder that a rock spoke, for the lot of Joshua cried out: "I am the lot of so-and-so," as it is said, *"According to the lot..."* <note>original: Numbers 26:56</note>. 

David replied: "My name is like the name of my Creator—David <note>spelled here with an extra 'Yod' (D-V-Y-D)</note>, 'My beloved is mine and I am his' <note>original: Song of Songs 2:16</note>. Let me touch it." He took the Rock with the power of the Explicit Name upon it, and the waters of the Abyss floated upward. David said to the Holy One, Blessed be He: "Did You intend to kill me?" He replied: "I did not intend to kill you, but rather to show you the foundation of the Temple." Immediately, David mentioned the Name and halted the waters, and they rose up to the sides. David took the Rock with the Explicit Name engraved on it and threw it upon the face of the Abyss, and the waters retreated.

**And now,** I shall enlighten you in the secrets. Let it not be your business to ask: "How many tens of thousands of years is the depth below?" If your heart runs toward this, return to the place of the Blessed Name, as it is said: *“The living creatures ran and returned”* <note>original: Ezekiel 1:14; a key concept in Jewish mysticism regarding the limits of human meditation</note>. For if your heart runs to this thought, restrain your heart and return to the Unity of the Blessed Name, to His service and His awe. Just as the living creatures <note>original: "Chayot" — high-ranking angels</note> wish to look but immediately bow their stature and run back and prostrate themselves, so too should it not enter your heart to think of the depth below. For His glory is below in the depths just as it is in the heavens, and the majesty of His kingship and His glory are there, as it is written: *“And underneath are the everlasting arms”* <note>original: Deuteronomy 33:27</note>.

Know that the entire world is round, for the heavens surround it like a basin over the whole world. The entire world is five hundred by five hundred <note>a traditional measure of distance in rabbinic cosmology</note>. And everything great returns to the world, like you, to the foundation of the world. And that dome, and the Great Sea, and the entire world stands upon one pillar, and its name is **Tzaddik** (The Righteous One), as it is said: *“The righteous is the foundation of the world”* <note>original: Proverbs 10:25</note>. 

That pillar and the entire world stand upon one fin of the **Leviathan** <note>a primordial sea monster in biblical tradition</note>. Leviathan dwells within the Lower Waters like a small fish in the sea. The Lower Waters are upon the Waters of Creation <note>original: "Mayim Bereshit"</note> like a small spring on the seashore. They are called "Weeping Waters" <note>original: "Mayim Bochim"</note> because at the time He divided the waters and took the Upper Waters, the Lower Waters wept a great weeping because they did not merit to go up. They tried to rise, but the Holy One, Blessed be He, trod them down. These Weeping Waters are suspended and stand above the lowermost earth. 

The earth is spread out over the "raging waters," as it is written: *“To Him who spread out the earth above the waters”* <note>original: Psalm 136:6</note>. The waters are upon the pillars of the **Chashmal** <note>term: "Chashmal" — a mystical amber glow or celestial energy seen in Ezekiel's vision</note>, and the pillars of Chashmal are upon the treasuries of snow, and the treasuries of snow are upon the mountains of Bether <note>referring to "mountains of separation"</note>, and the mountains of Bether are upon the Abyss. To what are they compared? To three heads of an ox, as it is said: *“Deep calls unto deep”* <note>original: Psalm 42:8</note>. 

And the Abyss is upon the **Tohu** (Chaos). Tohu is the green line from which darkness emerges. And Tohu is upon **Bohu** (Void), which is like marble stones from which water emerges. And Bohu stands upon the sea, and the sea is upon the water, and the water is upon the mountains, and the mountains are upon the wind, and the wind is suspended by a hair and bound to the body of the lowermost earth. 

The angel appointed over it is **Artzi-Tziau**. It is called **Eretz** (Earth) because it wanted <note>a wordplay on "Ratzta," meaning "wanted"</note> to do the bidding of the King. 

The dominion above the lowermost earth:
Abyss upon Abyss, 
Tohu upon Tohu, 
Bohu upon Bohu, 
Sea upon Sea, 
Water upon Water. 

The dust from the lowermost earth is bound by a hair to the dome of **Adamah** (Ground). Upon *Adamah* are His greatness, His might, His majesty, His strength, His beauty, and His power—the Creator of Genesis—as it is said: *“Be glad, O Adamah, and rejoice, for the Lord has done great things”* <note>original: Joel 2:21</note>. Why is it called *Adamah*? Because from it Man (*Adam*) was created, as it is said: *“And the Lord God formed man of the dust of the ground (Adamah)”*. The angel appointed over *Adamah* is named **Adamiel**. 

Above *Adamah*: Abyss upon Abyss, Tohu upon Tohu, Bohu upon Bohu, Sea upon Sea, Water upon Water. 

**Charevah** (Arid Land) is suspended by a hair and bound to the dome of *Charevah*. The angel appointed over it is named **Charevah**. Why is it called *Charevah*? Because it draws and dries up (*macharvet*) all the waters in the world, as it is said: *“And behold, he saw Charevah upon the face of the ground.”* Rivers are turned to dry land, as it is said: *“I will make the rivers dry (Charevah).”* 

Above *Charevah*: Abyss upon Abyss, Tohu upon Tohu, Bohu upon Bohu, Sea upon Sea, Water upon Water. 

**Yabashah** (Dry Land) is suspended by a storm-wind and bound to the dome of *Yabashah*. The angel appointed over it is named **Yovash-Yahel**. Why is it called *Yabashah*? Because it dries up all the waters in the world, as it is said: *“And the children of Israel walked on dry land (Yabashah) in the midst of the sea.”* *Yabashah* refers to seas, rivers, and every gathering of water, as it is said: *“To Him belongs the sea, and He made it, and His hands formed the dry land (Yabeshet).”* 

Above *Yabashah*: Abyss upon Abyss, Tohu upon Tohu, Bohu upon Bohu, Sea upon Sea, Water. 

**Arka** (Aramaic for Earth) is suspended by a storm-wind and bound to the dome of *Arka*. The angel appointed over it is named **Arka-el**. Why is it called *Arka*? Because in it is **Gehinnom** (Hell), and the wicked are judged there in fire, as it is said in Aramaic: *“Thus shall you say to them: The gods that have not made the heavens and the earth (Arka)...”* <note>original: Jeremiah 10:11</note>. 

In *Arka* are: Sheol (The Pit), Abaddon (Destruction), Beer Shachat (The Well of Corruption), Tit HaYavon (The Miry Clay), Shaarei Mavet (The Gates of Death), Shaarei Tzalmavet (The Gates of the Shadow of Death), and Gehinnom (Hell). The wicked are in them, and forty Angels of Destruction are appointed over them. The thickness of the darkness in them is like a wall, as it is said: *“The wicked shall be silent in darkness.”* 

Half of Sheol is fire and half is hail, yet they are not consumed, as it is said: *“And their form shall wear away in Sheol”* <note>original: Psalm 49:15</note>, but their souls are not consumed, for *“their worm shall not die.”* 

How many rivers of fire are in it? Seven great compartments in Hell, and each compartment has seven rivers of fire and seven rivers of hail. The fire and hail of the upper realms are sixty times stronger than our fire. Each compartment is forty times larger than the one next to it. Each compartment has 7,000 holes, and each hole has 7,000 cracks, and each crack has 7,000 scorpions, and each scorpion has 300 segments, and in each segment are 7,000 venom-sacs <note>original: "Kedad"</note>. There are seven rivers of the blood of death, which is like fire; when a person touches it, he bursts, but the Angels of Destruction revive him and judge him every moment. He spends half a year in fire and half a year in hail and snow, and the cold is harder for him than the fire. This is the portion of the wicked man. 

Above *Arka*: Abyss upon Abyss, etc., suspended by a storm-wind and bound to the dome of **Tevel** (The World). The angel appointed over it is named **Tebel**. Why is it called *Tevel*? Because they season <note>original: "metavlin," a wordplay on Tevel</note> it with their good deeds. Upon *Tevel* are mountains and hills and all their inhabitants, as it is said...

<summary>This page details the mystical account of King David securing the Abyss with the Explicit Name and provides a complex cosmography of the seven layers of the earth, focusing heavily on the terrifying geography and punishments of Hell (Arka/Gehinnom).</summary>

--- Page 37 ---
<meta>This page transitions from the physical foundation of the Temple to the celestial geography of the Divine Throne. It continues the theme of the "Explicit Name" by describing the vast, angelic realms and the complex structure of the Seven Heavens, specifically moving from the legend of King David to the mystical teachings attributed to Rabbi Akiva.</meta>

# The Book of Raziel the Great

He is magnificent in the holiness of His glory above the Living Creatures <note>term: "Chayot" — the highest order of angels who carry the Divine Throne</note> beneath the Throne, Throne facing His Throne of Glory; below as it is above, for there His Divine Presence <note>original: "Shekhinah"</note> dwells. As it is written: *"The heavens are My throne, and the earth is My footstool"* <note>Isaiah 66:1</note>, and it is written: *"and His praise fills the earth"* <note>Habakkuk 3:3</note>. And that place of the "feet" of the Divine Presence is surrounded by eighteen thousand worlds. There are four thousand and four hundred for the Northern wind; four thousand and four hundred for the Southern wind; four thousand and four hundred for the Western wind; and four thousand and four hundred for the Eastern wind—as it is said: *"It shall be eighteen thousand [cubits] round about"* <note>Ezekiel 48:35</note>. Fourteen thousand and four hundred for the Northern wind—full of praise, glory, power, acclaim, song, majesty, rejoicing, pride, and excellence. Four thousand and four hundred for the Southern wind—full of holiness, purity, righteousness, and the Great Name. Four thousand and four hundred for the Western wind—full of splendor, adornment, crowns, kingship, and a still, small voice <note>original: "kol demamah dakah"</note>. Four thousand and four hundred for the Eastern side—full of majesty and beauty, splendor, joy, singing, song, and renewal. And there is that which is written of it, that which is in the beauty of the north: full of grace, righteousness, strength, greatness, eternity <note>original: "netzach"</note>, and power. And that which is on high: full of majesty, beauty, splendor, adornment, pride, and excellence. And that which is in the East: full of a new song, the crown of kingship, and a still, small voice. And that which is in the West: full of praise, beauty, strength, pride, joy, and singing. And between all these Seraphim <note>term: "Seraphim" — "burning" angels associated with purification and praise</note> are those of the "Chariot of God," twice ten thousand, thousands of angels <note>original: "Shinan"</note>; the Lord is among them, and eighteen thousand remain who minister before Him. And beyond these eighteen thousand worlds, they are surrounded by rivers of water; and after the rivers of water, they are surrounded by rivers of fire; and after the rivers of fire, they are surrounded by walls of fire and water; and after the walls of fire and water, they are surrounded by mountains of hail and hills of snow; and after them, walls of tempest; and after them, walls of storm; and after them, they are surrounded by earthquake and trembling; and after them, they are surrounded by dread and fear, thunders and lightnings; and after them, flying Cherubim; and after them, wings of wind; and after them, thousands upon thousands of camps; and after them, myriads of "sweeteners of pleasantness" <note>likely referring to angelic choirs</note>; and after them, those who sing songs; and after them, those who blow the Shofar; and after them, those who trumpet with horns; and after them, myriads of sweeteners of pleasantness; and after them, like the baskets of His praise; and after them, those who say "Holy"; and after them, those who say "Blessed"—as it is said: *"and His praise fills the earth."* After all these was Metatron <note>original: "ט'ט" — an abbreviation for the primary archangel and celestial scribe</note>; and after Metatron, there was nothing. From here onward, all is chaos, void, and darkness, beyond searching and without end, without measure and without limit, as it is said: *"He made darkness His hiding place"* <note>Psalm 18:11</note>. And every single day, the Holy One, Blessed be He, rides upon a Cherub and is present in everything; when He goes, He rides upon a swift Cherub, and when He sees, He rides upon the wings of the wind, as it is said: *"And He rode upon a cherub, and did fly; and He was seen upon the wings of the wind"* <note>Psalm 18:10</note>. And He fills everything. And when He rides upon the Cherubim that are on high, they go toward the South, and so it is for all the Cherubim and for every single direction. And when He turns to this side, the angels of that side grow strong, and the princes below grow strong.

<margin>And the radiance of the Glory on this side is not like the other side when He looks upon it; therefore Solomon said in his prayer, *"May You hear in heaven, Your dwelling place"* <note>1 Kings 8:30</note>.</margin>

Against the Glory that comes in radiance, the angels offered blessing. And those eighteen worlds are a place of testimony for the righteous, and they are at the end... <note>original: "v'dak'al" — meaning unclear</note>. One should not say that there are small worlds included in these, for those are others; "the lovers of 'Yesh' <note>meaning "Substance" or "Being"</note> possess 310 worlds" <note>A common mystical reference to the 310 worlds reserved for the righteous, based on the numerical value of the word "Yesh"</note>, and there is another world for Him there... worlds. And in the radiance of the Glory that is seen from the place of His Throne of Glory—and the radiance is like sparks—we understand this is: *"Blessed be the glory of the Lord from His place"* <note>Ezekiel 3:12</note>; and He is everything and fills everything, and His Presence is in everything.

<margin>An exposition</margin>

In the *Alphabet of Rabbi Akiva* <note>term: "Otiot de-Rabbi Akiva" — a midrashic work on the mystical meanings of the Hebrew letters</note>: *"The earth is full of the kindness of the Lord"* <note>Psalm 33:5</note>. The Holy One, Blessed be He, gave great kindness to the Son to know the Torah, which is called "Kindness," as it is written: *"and the Torah of kindness is on her tongue"* <note>Proverbs 31:26</note>. What is above? Eighteen [worlds] of grace in the Five Books [of the Torah]; and eight of mercy. And whoever possesses kindness and mercy has everything within him, to teach about every single firmament and to show us what is inside it. The first is **Vilon** <note>term: "Vilon" (Curtain) — the first and lowest of the Seven Heavens</note>. And we said above: a void, a cause, and a curtain. *Vilon* is like a drape spread over the entrance so that those in the house cannot be seen; thus He *"stretches out the heavens as a curtain"* <note>Isaiah 40:22</note>. In the morning, it is spread over the face of the firmament of the heavens to protect against the face of the sun, and at night He removes it because of the stars and constellations. In the morning, it returns to its place and covers the firmament. From the West comes its sheath, and in the morning it comes to build and renew the work of creation every day. *Vilon* and there... are joined together. The "Heaven" <note>original: "Shamayim"</note> is beneath the firmament, separating, as it is written: *"The heavens declare the glory of God"* and afterward *"and the firmament shows His handiwork."* Again, above it is *Vilon*, which is like a fine veil and is seen by day. For the Holy One, Blessed be He, created three hundred and sixty firmaments, according to the number of days, and they are doubled together... and every firmament has a name and the sons of punctuation upon it, and each one has ministers between the Throne of Glory. But the Seven Heavens are the main ones, and by their names all the firmaments are included and mixed. *Vilon*, *Raki'a* <note>Firmament</note>... and now the Heavens are a journey of five hundred years, and a ladder is set on the earth for five hundred years, and its top reaches the "Shmichah" <note>covering</note> as the measure of the ladder’s journey of five hundred years. And honorable princes are appointed; seven Seraphim are directed over the lands in their ascending and descending; they must bring them from *Matshion* <note>unclear place-name</note>. And the heavens were created from fire and water; by the word of the Lord the heavens were made. How? When the Holy One, Blessed be He, spoke, the power of the fire was the heat of the word and the nature of the moisture... and He created the heavens with it. And "My Heaven" which is beneath the firmament... there is water there. The name of the firmament is "H' Ashan" <note>literally "The Fire-Smoke"</note>. And so there are camps of angels of fury. And seven thrones are prepared in the heavens, not to sit upon them, but to show their ministers that they rule over them; and their company dwells in their blessings, and they strengthen themselves to take vengeance according to the will of the Place <note>term: "HaMakom" — a name for God implying His omnipresence</note>, Blessed be He. And they harm the person who occupies himself with the angels or who adjures the angels [who are] ready to act. And the seven princes, the officers, are created from fire, and their appearance is of fire, and fire blazes [from them], for from fire they emerged. And their camps shall do nothing except by the counsel of the seven officers and by their permission; and they are sent whether for good or for evil, whether for abundance or for lack, whether for war or for peace. Under the first watcher among them are serving princes before them, who are heard for every matter of healing, to do and to call to all of them by the name of *Tzim* and by the name of Purity. Under the second watcher are those who minister before the princes and angels, and they are [made] of "restrained" anger and fury to cast down enemies in the midst of the angels of war, and to make peace from... to all of them He shall call by name in purity. Under the third watcher, there minister before him [number unclear] princes and angels, and they are those who inform a person whether there will be plenty or hunger, and every matter that shall come, whether good or evil; to all of them a name shall be called in purity. Under the fourth watcher are forty-two [ministers], and they are those appointed over the heart of the king and the princes and over every person; to all of them a name shall be called in purity. Under the fifth watcher are twenty-two, and they are those who are heard at night

# The Book of Raziel the Angel

It is written: *"But where can wisdom be found?"* <note>Job 28:12</note> and it is written: *"The fear of the Lord is wisdom."* <note>Proverbs 9:10</note> But in the heavens, which are full of understanding, from where is one thing derived from another? It says understanding is in the heavens and in the depths, for they have the knowledge to bring forth two drops corresponding to every single language. It says knowledge is in the depths, and He recorded his lot and a portion of ten for His glory.

## Seven

*"Seven times a day I praise You"* <note>Psalm 119:164</note> and so on. The Lord loves all "sevenths." He created seven lands and gave His Torah upon the seventh land <note>Refers to the idea that the Land of Israel is the "seventh" or most central land</note>; He chose *Arabot* <note>term: Arabot — the highest of the seven heavens</note> as the seventh firmament; He chose the seventh day, the Sabbath. They are many. And as one circles the lower earth behind them, they are surrounded by fire and water. Behind the fire and water, they are surrounded by clamor and storm; behind them, lightning and thunder; behind them, sparks and quakings; behind them, the likeness of the Living Creatures <note>term: "Chayot" — the highest order of angels</note>; behind them, they are surrounded by those who "rush and return" <note>Refers to Ezekiel 1:14, describing the movement of the angels</note>; behind them, they are surrounded by those who say "a great noise"; behind that, a still, small voice; behind them, they say "Holy"; behind her, they say "Blessed"; behind her, they say "He shall reign"; behind them are all these Holy Living Creatures, and the Wheels <note>term: "Ophanim" — an order of angels associated with the Chariot</note>, and the Throne of Glory, and the "feet" of the Divine Presence <note>original: Shekhinah</note> which are surrounded above their heads, as it is said: *"And I saw the Living Creatures, and behold, one wheel was on the earth."* <note>Ezekiel 1:15</note> Just as the Presence is above, so His Presence is below, as it is said: *"and His praise fills the earth."* <note>Habakkuk 3:3</note> Furthermore: *"The heaven is My throne, and the earth is My footstool"* <note>Isaiah 66:1</note>; and thousands upon thousands and myriads upon myriads of ministering angels guard and stand beneath the feet of the Divine Presence and praise Him with every kind of praise and every kind of acclaim, as it is said: *"and His praise fills the earth."* Beneath all the lands, His Presence is upon the Throne of Glory; this is the meaning of *"And underneath are the everlasting arms."* <note>Deuteronomy 33:27</note> Why two arms? One to support the lower beings and one to support the upper beings. His glory is in the heavens above and below, and the connection is in all the air and in the thickness, and He fills every place. For if He had not created firmaments within it, then there would be one glory above and one below; if so, it would be difficult to understand the verse *"The Lord is One."* <note>The Shema: Deuteronomy 6:4</note> Rather, it is truth: He is One and there is no second to His pair; there is no Creator who fills all besides Him, as we shall explain at the end of the book, if the Creator of all grants us merit. And His glory fills the heavens above, for the Glory dwells above corresponding to the Throne, and there are the Seraphim, the Chashmalim <note>term: "Chashmalim" — a high rank of angels often described as "speaking fire"</note>, the Cherubim, the Wheels, and the Living Creatures. Thus, beneath the lowest earth, it is to inform us that the Glory which is above is praised by those with wings, and I heard the sound of their wings like the "wing of the earth," and songs we heard from above: "Holy," and from below: "Blessed." 

At the hour when the Holy One, Blessed be He, said, *"Let them make Me a sanctuary, that I may dwell [among them],"* <note>Exodus 25:8</note> all the princes of the heavenly hosts gathered and came before the Holy One, Blessed be He. They said: "Master of the Universe! Why should You dwell in a place of impurity and settle there? It is better for You to dwell among us, for we are pure and we are in a place of purity and permanence. Dwell among us and do not dwell among them!" The Holy One, Blessed be He, said to them: "I have a man below who is equal to all of you. You sing before Me with song," as it says, *"Sing, O heavens, for the Lord has done it; shout, you lower parts of the earth."* <note>Isaiah 44:23</note> "Sing, O heavens"—these are the angels. "Shout, you lower parts of the earth"—these are Israel, who sing below. And not only that, but they sing before you, as it is written: *"When the morning stars sang together"* <note>Job 38:7</note>—these are Israel—and afterward: *"and all the sons of God shouted for joy."* Furthermore, he is more important to Him than the Throne of Glory, for your prayers do not ascend unless they sing. Just as Aaron... "one"... ascends... *"Sing to Jacob with gladness,"* <note>Jeremiah 31:7</note> and regarding him it is written: *"and he shall see you and be glad in his heart."* <note>Exodus 4:14</note> When they heard this, they all fell silent, and He commanded Moses: *"And let them make Me a sanctuary, that I may dwell in their midst."* 

And corresponding to the letters of Abraham, Isaac, Jacob, Reuben, Simeon, Levi, Judah, Issachar, Zebulun, Dan, Naphtali, Gad, Asher, Joseph, Benjamin, and the tribes of Jeshurun—behold, there are 72 letters, just as in His Name <note>term: "Name of 72" — a mystical 72-letter name of God derived from Exodus 14:19-21</note>. And the letters of the Mothers are like the letters of the Fathers. He embraces and kisses the image of Jacob. And in Israel, they sanctify the name of the Holy One, Blessed be He, below in His Holy House or in a "miniature sanctuary" <note>original: "mikdash meat" — a common term for a synagogue</note>. "Holy, Holy, Holy." It says in the *Book of the Palaces* <note>original: "Sefer Hekhalot" — a core text of early Jewish mysticism</note>: The Holy One, Blessed be He, embraces and kisses the name of the image of Jacob which is engraved on the Throne of Glory, and He places His two arms upon the two arms of Jacob and says: "Happy is the Father whose sons praise Him with these sanctifications." 

And the Holy One, Blessed be He, showed Balaam the "Work of the Chariot" <note>term: "Ma'aseh Merkavah" — the deepest secrets of the divine realm and the Chariot of Ezekiel</note> and the place of Jacob on the Throne. He said: *"How goodly [Tobu] are your tents, O Jacob."* <note>Numbers 24:5</note> In Gematria <note>term: "Gematria" — Jewish numerology where letters have numerical values</note>, "Jacob" [Yaakov] equals [the value of] "on the Throne" [Ba-Kiseh]. And it is written: *"His left hand is under my head"* <note>Song of Songs 2:6</note>—this is Israel—*"and his right hand embraces me."* Therefore it is said: *"Let Your hand be upon the man of Your right hand."* <note>Psalm 80:17</note> Upon "there is" [Yesh] Your right hand; in Gematria, "Israel" [Yisrael] is the same as "God" [El]. And upon the "son of man" whom You have made strong for Yourself—"You have made strong" [Imatzta] in Gematria equals "Israel." And it is written: *"Your statutes are my strength [amatztekha]"*; also *"You uphold me with Your righteous right hand."* <note>Isaiah 41:10</note> 

And one says "Holy, Holy, Holy" three times, corresponding to: 
1. *"He is God in heaven above"* 
2. *"and upon the earth beneath"* 
3. *"there is no other"* <note>Deuteronomy 4:39</note> in the air. 
This is to say that there is none like Him in the upper realms, the lower realms, or the middle air. And corresponding to the three unifications we say: *"Hear, O Israel: The Lord our God, the Lord is one."* <note>Deuteronomy 6:4</note> One is in the blessing before [the Shema], one in the Shema in the morning, and one in the evening. 

*"Who has performed and done it, calling the generations from the beginning?"* 
1. *"I, the Lord, am the first"* 
2. *"and with the last, I am He."* <note>Isaiah 41:4</note> 
3. *"Thus says the Lord, the King of Israel and his Redeemer, the Lord of hosts: I am the first and I am the last, and besides Me there is no God."* <note>Isaiah 44:6</note> 

And corresponding to the three "Living Creatures": 
1. *"As I looked, thrones were set in place, and the Ancient of Days took His seat. His clothing was as white as snow; the hair of His head was white like wool. His throne was flaming with fire, and its wheels were all ablaze."* <note>Daniel 7:9, translated from Aramaic</note> 
2. *"In my vision at night I looked, and there before me was one like a son of man, coming with the clouds of heaven. He approached the Ancient of Days and was led into His presence."* <note>Daniel 7:13, translated from Aramaic</note> 
3. *"Until the Ancient of Days came and judgment was given... and the holy ones possessed the kingdom."* <note>Daniel 7:22, translated from Aramaic</note> 

Correspondingly, in the prayer "Blessed be He who spoke" <note>original: "Barukh She'amar"</note>, we say "Holy, Holy, Holy" three times: once in the Creator blessing, once in the Standing Prayer <note>original: "Amidah"</note>, and once in the Order of Sanctification <note>original: "Seder Kedushah"</note>, to proclaim His holiness in my house, in the air, in the heavens, and on the earth. And so, three prayers are prayed each day. 

And the prayers and blessings—one who prays them with intention is saved from the seven, eight, and nine handbreadths of Gehenna <note>term: Gehenna — the place of spiritual purification or punishment</note>, which is one hundred and eight cubits, according to the numerical value of "Gehenna" [108]. This is the meaning of: *"Therefore Sheol has enlarged its appetite and opened its mouth without limit."* <note>Isaiah 5:14</note> And he shall enter the Garden of Eden, which is 1.8 million years [in size?]. *"No eye has seen any God besides You."* <note>Isaiah 64:4</note> Regarding Him whom no man can see, there are 18 vertebrae in the spine, and it is written: *"And He dwells in the life of the worlds."* The Holy One, Blessed be He, has 18,000 worlds that He looks upon every day, and they are hidden; therefore, *"His mercy is forever"* is written, for they are hidden. <margin>On page 51b: One should always say the blessings in the Standing Prayer, as it is written "forever," etc.</margin> There are 18 chapters in the Throne, as it is written "forever," etc. And just as the Power created everything and His glory fills all the air and He is exalted over the Holy Living Creatures in the heavens, so...

# The Great Book of Raziel

He is magnificent in His holy glory above the Living Creatures <note>term: Chayot — the highest order of angels, described in the vision of Ezekiel</note> upon forty-nine thrones, corresponding to His Throne of Glory. Below is like above, for there His Divine Presence <note>term: Shekhinah — the indwelling presence of God in the world</note> dwells. It is written: *"The heavens are My throne, and the earth is My footstool"* <note>Isaiah 66:1</note> and it is written: *"His praise fills the earth."* <note>Habakkuk 3:3</note> And that place of the "feet" of the Divine Presence is surrounded by 18,000 worlds: 4,500 to the North, 4,500 to the South, 4,500 to the West, and 4,500 to the East, as it is said: *"The circumference is 18,000."* <note>Ezekiel 48:35</note> To the **North** are 4,500 [worlds] of praise, glory, power, song, melody, splendor, joy, and majesty. To the **South** are 4,500 full of holiness, purity, and righteousness—there is greatness. To the **West** are 4,500 full of beauty, ornamentation, crowns, kingship, and a "still, small voice." To the **East** are 4,500 full of splendor, majesty, beauty, joy, singing, and new songs. There are those who write that the ones in the North are full of grace, charity, strength, greatness, victory, and power; and the ones in the East are full of splendor, majesty, beauty, crowns, pride, and majesty; and the ones in the East <note>The text repeats "East" here, possibly a scribal variation or a distinction between inner and outer circles</note> are full of a new song, a royal crown, and a still, small voice; and the ones in the West are full of praise, beauty, strength, majesty, joy, and singing. Between all these Seraphim <note>term: Seraphim — "burning" angels of high rank</note> are the "Chariots of God," twice ten thousand, thousands of angels <note>Psalm 68:18</note>. The Lord is among them and the other 18,000, and they minister before Him.

After these 18,000 worlds, they are surrounded by rivers of water. After the rivers of water, they are surrounded by rivers of fire. After the rivers of fire, they are surrounded by walls of fire and water. After the walls of fire and water, they are surrounded by mountains of hail and hills of snow. After these are walls of the gale; after them, walls of the storm; after them, they are surrounded by quaking and trembling; after them, they are surrounded by dread and fear, thunder and lightning. After them are flying Cherubim; after them, wings of the wind; after them, a thousand thousands of camps; after them, ten thousands of "Sweeteners of Pleasantness"; after them, singers of songs; after them, blowers of the Shofar; after them, those who blast trumpets; after them, the "Sweeteners of Pleasantness" in their myriads; after them, the "reckoning of Kislusim" <note>This term is likely a mystical name for a specific group of angels or a celestial calculation</note>; after them, those who say "Holy"; after them, those who say "Blessed," as it is said: *"His praise fills the earth."* After all these are the "99 Measures" <note>original: "tet-tet hin"</note>, and after these there is nothing; from here onward is "Chaos and Void" <note>term: Tohu va-Vohu — the primordial state of the universe mentioned in Genesis 1:2</note> and darkness until it is beyond investigation, beyond number, beyond measure, and without end—as it is written: *"He made darkness His secret place."* <note>Psalm 18:11</note> Every day and every hour, the Holy One, Blessed be He, rides upon a Cherub and is present in all of them. When He goes, He rides upon a swift Cherub, and when He observes, He rides upon the wings of the wind, as it is written: *"And He rode upon a cherub, and did fly: yea, He did fly upon the wings of the wind."* He fills everything. When He rides upon the Cherubim in the South, they move toward the South, and so it is for all the Cherubim and every direction. When He turns to one side, the angels of that side grow strong, and the princes below <note>Refers to the guardian angels of nations on Earth</note> grow strong. This is not the [physical] Glory, for the Glory on one side hints to the other side when He observes it. Thus Solomon said in his prayer: *"Hear You in heaven, Your dwelling place."* <note>1 Kings 8:30</note> Corresponding to the Glory that comes in radiance, the angels bless. Those 18,000 worlds are the place and destination for the righteous, and they are described with precision. One should not say there are "small" worlds included in these, for those are "others"—the "lovers" possess 18 worlds, and there, one world is called "Worlds." In the radiance of the Glory seen from the place of His Throne of Glory, the surrounding radiance reflects: *"Blessed be the glory of the Lord from His place."* <note>Ezekiel 3:12</note> He is everything, fills everything, and His Presence is in everything.

<margin>Behold, he interprets in the **"Alphabet of Rabbi Akiva"** <note>A famous mystical midrash that interprets the letters of the Hebrew alphabet</note>: The word "Full" <note>Hebrew: m-l-a-h</note> is missing the letter *He* in "the earth is full" <note>original: מלאה הארץ</note>. The Holy One, Blessed be He, gave us Greatness to know through the Torah, which is called "Kindness" <note>Chesed</note>, as it is written: *"The Torah of kindness is on her tongue."* <note>Proverbs 31:26</note> What is above? Eighteen. "Gracious" <note>original: Chanun</note> begins with the letter *He* [numerical value 5], fifty-eight. Mercies. Whoever possesses Kindness and Mercy possesses everything to teach.</margin>

---

Regarding every background and firmament, to teach us what is within it: The first is **Vilon** <note>term: Vilon — "The Curtain," the first and lowest of the seven heavens</note>. We said above that He emptied His world; Vilon is like a sheet spread over an entrance so that those in the house cannot be seen. Thus "He stretches them out like a curtain" <note>Isaiah 40:22</note>. In the morning it is spread over the face of the firmament of the heavens to protect against the heat of the sun, and at night it withdraws <note>unclear: possibly "me'ayan" meaning "eyes" or "springs," but contextually refers to the curtain moving</note> because of the stars and constellations. In the morning it returns to its place and covers the firmament; at evening its sheath comes, and in the morning it comes to be inflamed, and the work of creation is renewed every day. In Vilon there are fifty "Redakim" together; for "Heavens" <note>Shamayim</note> is beneath the firmament, separated, as it is written: *"The heavens declare the glory of God"* <note>Psalm 19:1</note>, and afterward, *"the firmament shows His handiwork."* Above Vilon is like a thin veil and is visible during the day. Know that the Holy One, Blessed be He, created 390 firmaments, the numerical value of "Heaven" <note>original: "Shamayim" = 390</note>, and they are folded together like a fold. Every firmament has ten names, and its construction is marked upon it. Each one ministers before the Throne of Glory.

However, **seven firmaments** are the primary ones, and all the other firmaments are included in their names: **Vilon** (Curtain), **Rakia** (Firmament), **Shechakim** (Clouds), **Zevul** (Abode), **Ma'on** (Dwelling), **Machon** (Foundation), and **Arabot** (Clouds/Plains). Their height is a 500-year journey, and its head reaches the "Covering" <note>unclear</note>, a distance like the ladder's journey of 500 years. Noble princes are appointed; seven officials are directed over the lands. In their rising and descending, Metatron <note>term: Metatron — the highest of the angels, often called the "Prince of the Presence"</note> must bring them. The heavens were created from fire and water; by the word of the Lord the heavens were made. How? When the Holy One, Blessed be He, spoke, the power of the fire took hold of the word and the nature of the moisture... and He created the heavens with it. The heavens which are beneath the firmament are called "Water," and the name of the firmament is "Fire." Likewise, there are camps of the Angels of Wrath. There are seven thrones of stars in the heavens, not for sitting upon, but to show their ministers who rule over them. Their conjunctions are "dwellings" of their blessings, and they are strengthened to take vengeance according to the will of the Place, Blessed be He. They may harm a person who occupies himself with angels or who adjures them; the angels are prepared to act. The seven judging princes are created from fire, their appearance is fire, and a fire flashes from them, for they emerged from fire. Their camps do nothing except by the counsel of the officers and by the permission of the Seven, whether for good or evil, for abundance or lack, for war or peace.

Under the **First Guard** among them are ministering princes who obey every matter of healing, to act and to call them all by the name **Tzadam** <note>original: צד"ם</note> and by the name **Toharah** (Purity).
Under the **Second Guard** are ministers before princes and angels, and they are **Mumoka'el** <note>original: ממוכא"ל</note>; they are for striking down enemies among the angels of war and to complete... from the place of death; he shall call to them all by the name **Shacharah** (Dawn).
Under the **Third Guard**, ministers serve before him for four princes/angels; they inform a person of plenty or famine, and everything that will come, whether good or evil; to all of them, he shall call by the name **B'toharah** (In Purity).
Under the **Fourth Guard** are **M.B.** <note>Numerical abbreviation for 42</note>, and they are appointed over the heart of the king and the princes and every man; to all of them, he shall call by the name **Yaka** <note>original: יק"א</note> in purity.
Under the **Fifth Guard** are **K.B.** <note>Numerical abbreviation for 22</note>, and they are heard at night...

It identifies the protective role of the first heaven, Vilon, and lists the angelic guards responsible for earthly matters like healing, war, and the hearts of kings.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the cosmological exposition, moving from the physical and angelic dimensions of the four directions to the mystical significance of the number seven, the divine hierarchy, and the unique relationship between God, the people of Israel, and the Patriarch Jacob. It establishes a link between the celestial Temple and the earthly Tabernacle.</meta>

# The Great Book of Raziel

It is as it is written: *"But where shall wisdom be found?"* <note>Job 28:12</note> and it is written: *"The fear of the Lord is wisdom."* <note>Proverbs 9:10</note> But in the heavens, which are full of [understanding], one understands one thing from another. He [the Sage] said:
"Understanding is in the heavens." And in the depths, which have the [capacity for] knowledge to bring forth two drops corresponding to every [ ] lip and lip. He said: "Knowledge is in the depths." And the book of his destiny and the cord of ten <note>original: "חבל עשרה" (chevel asarah) — likely referring to the ten Sefirot or divine emanations</note> is for His glory.

# **Seven**

"On the day of your praise..." and so forth. The Lord loves all "sevens." He created seven lands and gave His Torah upon the seventh land <note>referring to the seventh "level" of the earth, often called Tever or the inhabited world</note>; He chose *Arvot* <note>the seventh and highest heaven</note> as the seventh firmament; He chose the seventh day, the Sabbath. For they are many, and in coming around the lower earth, behind them they encompass fire and water. Behind the fire and water, they encompass noise and trembling; behind them lightning and thunder; behind them sparks and quakes; behind them the likeness of the *Chayot* <note>term: Chayot — the "Living Creatures" or high-ranking angels from Ezekiel's vision</note>; behind them they encompass "running and returning" <note>Ezekiel 1:14; a mystical description of angelic movement</note>; behind them they encompass those who say "a great sound" <note>Ezekiel 3:12</note>; after that, "a still small voice"; behind them they say "Holy"; behind them they say "Blessed"; behind them they say "Peace." Behind them are all these: the Holy Creatures, the *Ofanim* <note>term: Ofanim — "Wheels," another high order of angels</note>, the Throne of Glory, and the "Feet" of the *Shekhinah* <note>term: Shekhinah — the Divine Presence</note> which are encompassed above their heads, as it is said: *"And I looked at the Living Creatures, and behold, one wheel was on the earth."* <note>Ezekiel 1:15</note> Just as the Divine Presence is above, so His Presence is below, as it is said: *"His praise fills the earth."* <note>Habakkuk 3:3</note> Furthermore: *"The heavens are My throne and the earth is My footstool."* <note>Isaiah 66:1</note> And thousands of thousands and myriads upon myriads of ministering angels guard and stand beneath the feet of the Divine Presence and praise Him with every kind of praise and every kind of glorification, as it is said: *"His praise fills the earth."* Beneath all the lands is His dwelling upon the Throne of Glory; this is the meaning of *"And underneath are the everlasting arms."* <note>Deuteronomy 33:27</note> Why two arms? One that sustains the lower beings and one that sustains the upper beings. His glory is in the heavens above and below, and it enters into all the air and the thickness; He fills every place. If He had only created the firmaments, then His glory would be once above and once below, and it would be a difficult thing [to understand] that it is written *"The Lord is One."* Rather, it is true that He is One and there is no second besides Him, the Creator who fills all, and there is none besides Him, as we shall explain at the end of the book, if the Creator of all grants us merit. His glory fills the heavens above, for the Glory dwells above corresponding to the Throne, and there are the *Seraphim*, the *Chashmalim*, the *Cherubim*, the *Ofanim*, and the *Chayot*. [And] beneath the lowest earth, to inform us that the Glory which is above is praised by those with wings and the rustling of their wings. From the end of the earth we have heard songs <note>Isaiah 24:16</note> from the Holy Ruler, and from below, "Blessed [is the glory of the Lord]." At the hour when the Holy One, Blessed be He, said: *"Let them make for Me a sanctuary that I may dwell among them,"* <note>Exodus 25:8</note> all the ministering princes of the upper realm gathered and came before the Holy One, Blessed be He. They said to Him: "Master of the Universe! Why should You dwell in a place of impurity and reside there? It is better for You to dwell among us, for we are pure and we are in a place of purity and permanence. Dwell among us and do not dwell among them!" The Holy One, Blessed be He, said to them: "I have a man below who is equal to all of you. You sing before me with 'Singing' (*Ranu*), as it is said: *'Sing (Ronu), O heavens, for the Lord has done it.'* <note>Isaiah 44:23</note> The *Chayot* are the letters of 'Sing, O heavens'—these are the angels of the firmament. The 'depths of the earth'—these are Israel, for they are the singers below. Not only that, but they sing before you, as it is written: *'When the morning stars sang together'* <note>Job 38:7</note>—these are Israel—and afterwards, *'and all the sons of God shouted for joy.'*" Moreover, he is more precious before Him than you, for your groups only ascend with 'Singing' (*Ranan*) in the name *Ehyeh Asher Ehyeh* <note>original: "I Am That I Am"</note>, and 'Singing' (*Ranan*) ascends for us through Jacob's praise. Regarding him it is written: *"And when he sees you, he will be glad in his heart."* <note>Exodus 4:14</note> As soon as they heard this, they all became silent, and He commanded Moses: *"And let them make for Me a sanctuary that I may dwell among them."* And corresponding to the letters of Abraham, Isaac, Jacob, Reuben, Simeon, Levi, Judah, Issachar, Zebulun, Dan, Naphtali, Gad, Asher, Joseph, Benjamin—the tribes of Jeshurun—there are 72 letters. Just as there are 72 letters in the [Great] Name, and there are 72 [ ], and 7 and 9. And the letters of [ ] the Matriarchs—like the letters of the Matriarchs, He embraces and kisses the image of Jacob. And the Children of Israel sanctify the name of the Holy One, Blessed be He, [ ] below in His holy house or in a "minor sanctuary" <note>term: Mikdash Me'at — a synagogue</note> by saying "Holy, Holy, Holy." It says in the *Book of the Palaces* <note>term: Sefer Hekhalot — an early Jewish mystical text</note>: The Holy One, Blessed be He, embraces and kisses the name of the image of Jacob which is engraved upon the Throne of Glory, and He places His two arms upon the two arms of Jacob and says: "Happy is the nation that praises Him with such sanctifications!" Through the "vision of the Almighty" <note>Numbers 24:4</note>, the Holy One, Blessed be He, showed Balaam the workings of the Chariot <note>term: Merkavah — the divine chariot-throne</note> and the place of Jacob on the Throne. He said: *"How goodly are your tents, O Jacob!"* <note>Numbers 24:5</note> Jacob in gematria <note>term: Gematria — the calculation of the numerical value of Hebrew letters</note> is *Yokeiv* <note>original: "יוק"ב" — 112 or 118 depending on spelling; Jacob (Yaakov) is 182</note> on the Throne. And it is written: *"His left hand is under my head"*—this is Israel—*"and His right hand embraces me."* <note>Song of Solomon 2:6</note> Therefore it is said: "Your praise is upon the man of Your right hand." "Upon the man of Your right hand" in gematria is "Israel-El" <note>Israel-God</note>. Therefore, "I have strengthened you," I have strengthened the Children of Israel, and it is written: *"Your statutes have strengthened me,"* and also *"Your right hand has held me up."* <note>Psalm 18:35</note> And we say "Holy, Holy, Holy" three times corresponding to: [1] "He is God in heaven above," [2] "and on the earth below," [3] "and there is none else." <note>Deuteronomy 4:39</note> This is to say to the nations of the world that there is none like Him in the upper or lower realms, or in the middle air. And it corresponds to the three declarations of Unity that we say: "Hear O Israel, the Lord our God, the Lord is One" <note>the Shema</note> in the blessing of the Creator of light—which they recite—and in the Shema in the morning and in the evening. "Who has wrought and done it, calling the generations from the beginning?" [1] "I the Lord am the first," [2] "and with the last," [3] "Thus says the Lord the King of Israel and his Redeemer, the Lord of Hosts: I am the first and I am the last, and besides Me there is no God." And corresponding to the three "Living Creatures": [1] "Until thrones were placed and the Ancient of Days sat; His garment was white as snow and the hair of His head like pure wool; His throne was sparks of fire and its wheels burning fire." [2] "I saw in the night visions, and behold, with the clouds of heaven, one like a Son of Man came, and he came to the Ancient of Days and they brought him near before Him." [3] "Until the Ancient of Days came, and judgment was given to the holy ones... and the days arrived for the holy ones to possess the kingdom." <note>Daniel 7:9-22; translated from the original Aramaic</note> Corresponding to these, at dawn they say three times "Holy, Holy, Holy," and then in the *Yotzer* <note>the prayer before the Shema</note>, and in the *Amidah* <note>term: Amidah — the standing silent prayer</note>. One in the *Order of Sanctification* <note>the Kedushah d'Sidra</note> is to proclaim His holiness in the houses, in the air, in the heavens, and on the earth. Thus three prayers are prayed daily.
The prayers and blessings: whoever prays them with intention is saved from seven or eight [levels of] the depths of Gehenna <note>term: Gehenna — purgatory or the underworld</note>, which is 108 cubits in the cubits of the Holy One, Blessed be He, [ ] like the numerical value of "Gehenna." And this "bowl," its mouth is for those who do not have [ ] and he shall enter the World to Come, which is 1,800,000 years. *"No eye has seen, O God, besides You"* <note>Isaiah 64:4</note>—referring to Him whom no man can see. There are 18 vertebrae in the spine, and it is written: *"And He dwells in the Life of the Worlds."* The Holy One, Blessed be He, has 18,000 worlds which He watches every day, and they are hidden [ ]. Therefore, it is written "His mercy is forever" (*L'Olam*)—meaning they are hidden (*Ne'elamim*). (Therefore, forever [there are] 18 blessings in the *Amidah* where it is written "forever," etc.) 72 [ ] forever. 18 chapters in the Throne where it is written "forever," etc. Just as His power is exalted and fills all the air, His glory is also prideful over the Holy Creatures in the heaven...

# The Great Book of Raziel

The angels over whom He is King, and the doubled heavens <note>Hebrew: *Shamayim Hakfulim*. In Jewish mysticism, the heavens are often described as having a dual nature or being comprised of two layers: fire and water.</note> like a Throne. And "the heavens declare the glory of God" <note>Psalm 19:1</note> and stand thus. And above the heavens is the sea, and above the sea is water, lightning, and thunder hovering over them all. Strong walls of fire surround Him. A great ladder is set in the heavens, and its head reaches to the firmament <note>A reference to Jacob's Ladder, Genesis 28:12</note>; holy angels and honored princes are appointed over it. Angels are appointed over the gates of the North and the South: there are six; and over the gates of the South: there are six; over the gates of the East: there are six; over the gates of the West: there are six. And angels reside in the "sole of a calf's foot" <note>original: "כף רגל עגל" (kaf regel egel) — an image from Ezekiel's vision of the Chayot/Living Creatures (Ezekiel 1:7)</note>, and from their wings outward, it is divided into lines, as it is said: "each one went straight forward; they did not turn as they went" <note>Ezekiel 1:12</note>. For the circle is within the protrusions of the calf's foot; its translation is "his feet," and the "soles of the feet" are rounded. The reason their feet are a "rounded foot" is that darkness is the head of creation and its likeness is like an ox <note>In the Merkavah (Chariot) tradition, the Ox is one of the four faces and is associated with the left side/darkness/judgment</note>; therefore Solomon made twelve oxen beneath them <note>The "Molten Sea" in the Temple sat upon twelve bronze oxen (1 Kings 7:25)</note>. And why is it a "straight leg"? For the leg of an ox is no greater than that of a lion, nor that of a man than that of an eagle. "How manifold are Your works, O Lord!" <note>Psalm 104:24</note>. Know that as we have drawn it, so it is: three protrusions for the Throne—one from here and one from there—and it is circular. Thus the great levels and the small levels are all explained.

I shall explain why there are three protrusions: there are levels like the letter *Bet* [ב] in the image of the Throne, and three in the appearance of *Chashmal* <note>term: Chashmal — a mystical amber-colored fire or electrum seen in Ezekiel's vision; often interpreted as "speaking-silence"</note>, and three of sapphire, and one from above in the call. And they were made like the letter *Bet*. Therefore, in the *Alphabet of Rabbi Akiva* <note>A famous Midrashic work on the mystical meanings of the Hebrew letters</note>, at the hour when the *Bet* descended from upon the Throne of Glory, the spheres <note>term: Galgalim — the celestial wheels or spheres</note> were moved. The Holy One, blessed be He, said to them: "Why do you make a noise?" They replied: "The *Bet* that was upon our heads has descended, and all our glory is within it." From the first day it was said: "They shall speak of the glory of Your kingdom" <note>Psalm 145:11</note>, for it is written that the head rests upon the Throne, and beneath His feet are levels, and one small one opposite it. Solomon, peace be upon him, said: "Hear from Your dwelling place in the heavens" <note>1 Kings 8:30</note>; the "two" are His throne and "the earth is His footstool" <note>Isaiah 66:1</note>—a house of rest for the Master. And for His footstool, "the house of our God, and let us bow at His footstool, for He is holy" <note>Psalm 99:5</note>. And it does not mention His footstool [further], but rather the five books [of the Torah] and one Mishnah, the book of *Mishneh Torah* <note>Deuteronomy</note>. Therefore one bows to the Torah, which was His footstool in the Ark between the seven thrones that are in the heavens. Thus He made it, and His throne is nothing other than the doubled heavens, and they are visible within it. Over it is the Appointed Overseer. We shall explain the core of the matter of the Throne of Glory with the help of the King of Glory. Qamuel is the host commander; Suriel <note>Sariel</note> is appointed over his angels, and their script is the overseer appointed over twelve thousand. And angels of destruction stand at the gates of heaven; there are many who "declare the glory of God," as it is written: "The heavens declare the glory of God" <note>Psalm 19:1</note>.

<margin>9</margin>
**9** corresponds to the firmament that divides... nine times "firmament" appears in the Book of Genesis until the waters that praise Him above the heavens. 1: In the heights; 2: His angels; 3: His hosts; 4: Sun; 5: Moon; 6: Stars; 7: Heavens of heavens; 8: And the waters that are above the heavens; 9: <unclear>?</unclear> Thus, "a place for Your dwelling." His dwelling place is the heavens. And there are nine kinds of fire in the *Ma'aseh Merkavah* <note>term: Ma'aseh Merkavah — "The Work of the Chariot," the highest form of Jewish mysticism focused on Ezekiel's vision</note> corresponding to the nine visions in the work of the firmament above the heavens. The firmament in which the sun, moon, stars, and all the ruling constellations are fixed, along with every depth. And a great ladder is set in the firmament, its head reaching the clouds <note>Hebrew: *Shachakim* — the third heaven</note>. Fiery angels and honored princes are appointed over it, and they are seven. They are appointed over the gates of the firmament and over the gates of the "work of the firmament." At the gates of the North wind, six princes are appointed; at the gates of the South wind, seven princes; at the East, seven princes; at the West, six princes. And in the second firmament, there is frost and incense, and storehouses of snow, and storehouses of hail, and angels of fire, and angels of judgment, and spirits of terror, and spirits of fear. That firmament is full of dread, for within it stand angels beyond searching, made into hosts, and over them are honored princes and overseers. And in the firmament are twelve levels (*ma'alot*), and on every single level stand angels in their glory, and over them a King of "body by body" <note>original: "גופה גופה" (gufah gufah) — perhaps referring to the distinct angelic forms or structures</note>, and eighty from the deeds of the children of men for all their callings in purity.

**On the first level** stand nine princes and overseers; their station is in awe, wrapped in dread, girded in fear, surrounding the congregation like Him, in the form of fire. Their faces are like the appearance of lightning, and their mouths do not rise to speak much, and their voice is not heard, for their work is to laugh, to terrify, and to dismay all who stand over them.

**On the second level** stand eleven princes; they stand in strength, filled with power, affection, and love, and opposite them burns fire. They run and bring the hearts of men close to one another.

**On the third level** stand fourteen princes who agitate, shake, and anger men, and cause men to stumble and cause their thoughts to fail. They are mighty in power and dread is before them and trembling behind them. They roar and shake and walk and make noise with the sound of crash and thunder. In their hands are rods of fire, and their faces are like sparks of fire coming out of their eyes, and all of them are ready to nullify and to break.

**On the fourth level** stand sixteen princes. Seven are girded with storm, and the sound of their distress is like the sound of a rushing wave from the East, with faces to the North. Their voice is like lightning, and fire surrounds them. They count the years of men <note>or perhaps "remove sleep from men"</note>, and they have the power to do good or evil.

**On the fifth level** stand twelve princes, holding shield and spear, with helmets on their heads and armor of scales as their clothing. To their right and to their left are hailstones of trembling in the race. They walk upon fire, holding torches, and they are swift, whispering answers. Their mouths do not rise from roaring, and their breath is like a burning fire, and a flaming fire from the wind of the nostrils kindles the fire, for their deeds are in the storehouses of fire. From the fire they emerged, and in the fire is their station.

**On the sixth level** stand seventeen princes; they conduct themselves with humility and are full of glory. They are clothed in white garments, and they are awesome, seated upon many thrones, and are faithful to the Truth and to the true, complete healing.

**On the seventh level** stand eleven princes girded with the strength of their might in awe. Half of them resemble fire and half of them resemble water. They are wondrous in their deeds, and one cannot stand upon the understanding of them, for fire is in their stature and sparks are in their wings. They stand in awe; those who stand beneath them cannot look upon their appearance, and those who stand above them are terrified by their sight. They walk to the three directions of the world.

**On the eighth level** stand nineteen princes...

Each group of princes is characterized by distinct attributes ranging from love and healing to terror, judgment, and the physical elements of fire and water.</summary>

...at night to speak with the stars, to inquire and to speak with the spirits. For all of them, he shall call by name in purity under the **sixth watchman**, thirty-seven <note>The number 37 likely refers to a specific angelic sub-group or numerical signature associated with this watchman.</note>, and the seal of the **Angels of Might** who are girded with strength and power to run and fly to all corners of the world; for all of them, he shall call by name in purity. Under the **seventh watchman**, forty-seven, and they tell all things in a dream; for all of them, he shall call by name in purity, and he shall succeed.

# Amual

**Amual** is the angel appointed over the host of the eighth firmament. He is in charge of twelve thousand angels and many paths in the heavens. When he extends the path in its length, it is made of **black fire**, and in the place where it ceases, there is a great mountain of **white fire** that separates them—a pure fire that cannot be seen. From there onward, it spreads to another path. At the interruption of the path is pure white fire, and so on. The paths become thick, and there are angels of the paths; this one goes here according to his way, and there is one who goes in circuits until he ascends the ladder from firmament to firmament, up to the **Chariot** <term>Merkavah; the Divine Chariot from the vision of Ezekiel</term>. There are angels who do not merit to join <gloss>alt. version: to be commanded</gloss> within the body of the Chariot itself.

Everywhere it is written "the heavens" or "in the heavens," it means within the space of the vault or up to the body of the firmament. But "the heavens above the heavens" is like a chamber-dweller who dwells within a room. Know that all the heavens are made like a dome <note>Hebrew: *Kuba*; a vaulted structure</note> and are stretched out like a tent to dwell in. The pillars and the sea are level with the waters of the **Ocean** <note>Hebrew: *Okyanos*; the Great Sea thought to encircle the world</note>; they are held fast by the waters of the Ocean between the edges of the heavens and the edges of the earth. The edges of the earth and the edges of the heavens rest upon the waters of the Ocean. This is the explanation of the verse: "Who lays the beams of His upper chambers in the waters" <note>Psalm 104:3</note>. The interior of the water rises upward like a spread tent, and human beings stand in its narrowness below and its breadth below <note>The text describes a paradoxical geometry where the heavens are narrow at the foundation and wide above, or vice versa, to encompass the earth</note>. These are the heavens; their narrowness is below and their interior is above. All creatures sit beneath them as in a "tent to dwell in" <note>Isaiah 40:22</note>. 

And the heavens and the earth were sealed with the signet of **Ehyeh Asher Ehyeh** <term>"I Am That I Am"; the Name revealed to Moses at the burning bush</term>. The letters **A-H** <note>Aleph-He</note> are from below and **Y-H** <note>Yod-He</note> are from above. These are the three: *Ehyeh Asher Ehyeh*, *Ehyeh Asher Ehyeh*, *Ehyeh Asher Ehyeh has sent me*. Behold, these are twelve letters corresponding to the twelve hours of the day, the twelve hours of the night, the twelve months of the year, the twelve signs of the Zodiac, and the twelve tribes. 

And how were they sealed? Four for each and every wind <note>Direction</note>: two letters above and two letters below. **A-H** from below and **Y-H** from above for three directions. But the **fourth direction** <note>The North</note> is open and stands unsealed. There are times when it is sealed with **Y-H Y-H-W**: **Y-H** from above and **W-H** from below. The sign for this is: "Let the heavens be glad and the earth rejoice" <note>Psalm 96:11; the first letters of these words in Hebrew—Yod, He, Vav, He—form the Tetragrammaton</note>. "Let the heavens be glad" (**Y-H**) from above; "and the earth rejoice" (**W-H**) from below. Through the rotation of the letters, a Name of ten letters is formed. As for why the **North** is open: it is because through it the **Shekhinah** <term>The Divine Presence</term> descended to confuse the tongues <note>The Tower of Babel</note>, as it says: "And the Lord came down to see the city..." and so on. He descended before it, and He descended...

...the Lord upon Mount Sinai, and so on. He will descend to the New Jerusalem, and "His feet shall stand on that day upon the Mount of Olives" <note>Zechariah 14:4</note>. <gloss>Note</gloss>: It seems to me these twelve letters are *Ehyeh*, and the letters of the seal are like the appearance of lightning and flames of light, visible for thousands of thousands and myriads of leagues. 

**Ehyeh Asher Ehyeh**: that is, if you look upon His stature—and because they are engraved by the Finger, they are bound with crowns of sparks of radiance and the lights of *Ehyeh*. In the illustration before you, you will find three *Ehyehs*. The height of the other is a measure of thousands of myriads of leagues, which is seventy-nine flames, in which he writes *Ehyeh* twice and another time "thousands." Why are the heavens imprinted <gloss>alt. version: sunken</gloss>? Because when we occupy ourselves with them, *Ehyeh* is the seal of heaven and earth. If the world were to use it, it would be consumed by fire, for there are many hosts of heavenly fire—mighty arrays of the triple *Ehyeh*, etc., as explained. 

Legions of fire desire to descend and burn the world, but as soon as they see the wings of the heavens bound to the wings of the earth, and the wings of the earth to the wings of the heavens by the seal of **Ehyeh**, they stand back from their anger and conduct themselves with the attribute of mercy.

And in the **Book of Formation** <term>Sefer Yetzirah; a foundational text of Jewish mysticism</term>, He sealed the height with **Y-H**, three simple letters established in His great Name **Y-H-W** <note>Yod-He-Vav</note>. And He sealed with them the six directions. He turned upward and sealed it with **Y-H-W**. [Regarding the letters] *Kaf-Resh-Aleph-Lamed-Bet* <gloss>alt. version: Lamed-Aleph-Bet</gloss> which are not doubled... <note>This refers to a technical linguistic/mystical categorization of the Hebrew alphabet found in Sefer Yetzirah</note>. He took **Y-H-W**, as we shall explain in the permutation of the letters of His Name, and permuted them one with another. And the Holy One, Blessed be He, made in the heavens a likeness of a Throne—not a literal throne, but only to inform all the angels...
