### The Powers: Understanding the Power of Actions

<margin>H-R-Y</margin> This teaches the Unique Name <note>The Tetragrammaton, YHWH</note> which is composed of all the letter *Yods*. Its combination consists of three powers for a single thing, called the "Land of the Living," which are attached. Its depiction is four powers that teach of the matter, and *this is none other than the Blessed He* <note>original: עזאב"ה — an abbreviation for "Ein Zeh Ela Baruch Hu," a formulaic declaration of God’s unity</note> for He is one in the upper realms.

<margin>H-Q-M</margin> This teaches of two workers and the twelve diagonal boundaries <note>The twelve cosmic directions described in the Book of Formation (Sefer Yetzirah)</note> by which He swore. Its combination is the Crown <note>Atarah, often referring to the Sefirah of Malchut</note> attached to Understanding <note>Binah</note> and Beauty <note>Tiferet</note>, which teaches of the matter. Its depiction is six powers teaching of Understanding, which adjures the heart of the workers. *This is none other than the Blessed He*, a remembrance of God in His name.

<margin>L-A-V</margin> This teaches of the three of them in Beauty <note>Tiferet</note>. Its depiction is six powers: the crown of Beauty is attached, and Beauty is between the two Wisdoms, as it is said, "from between the two Cherubim" <note>Exodus 25:22</note>. It speaks, and Beauty—in which everything is included—is within it. Everything is divided into three sanctities. *This is none other than the Blessed He* teaches of the upper holiness, and its combination is a single name for them.

<margin>K-L-Y</margin> This teaches of the name comprised of the four doors of the upper world. *This is none other than the Blessed He*, who is included in Mercy. Its depiction is four powers teaching of the matter, and their combinations are the Crown, Beauty, and Wisdom, which teach of the entire Structure <note>The "Building" or structure of the Sefirot</note>.

<margin>L-V-V</margin> This teaches of the statement of the twenty-eight <note>Possibly the 28 letters of the first verse of Genesis or the 28 camps of the Divine Presence</note> by which He swore. Its depiction teaches of the power engraved in the twelve constellations of Israel <note>The Zodiac signs</note>.

<margin>P-H-L</margin> This teaches of two workers, one inside the other. *This is none other than the Blessed He*, for the mercies of God are eternal since they are one within the other within the other. Its combination teaches thus, and its depiction is six powers teaching that the Crown is attached and bound in Kindness <note>Chesed</note> and Mercy, approaching the absolute upper realms.

<margin>N-L-K</margin> This teaches of the Great Name that adjures by the power of the Staff of Moses, with the power to perform awesome deeds. Its combination teaches from Understanding to the two, and its depiction consists of the powers teaching of the matter: how one receives from this and from within this, and the emergence of the aforementioned actions.

<margin>Y-Y-T</margin> <note>The OCR reads """, but the 72 Names sequence indicates the triplet Y-Y-T</note> This teaches the third in the secret of the three unique points from the upper A-H-H <note>A name of God associated with the source of life</note>, the softness of the middle line until the letter *Yod* which attaches to the three Sabbaths. *This is none other than the Blessed He*, for the last is the uppermost when they attach in the eternal rest, which is an oath for all. He is with them in the Jubilee for a single power, and the power of the world is in the hint. Its combination teaches thus, and its depiction as well. <margin>M-L-H</margin> This teaches of two workers, for everything revolves under their hand into a single power and a single unity. Thus is *this is none other than the Blessed He*. Its combination teaches thus, and its depiction is six powers teaching that the "daughter returns to her mother" <note>A Kabbalistic idiom for the Sefirah of Malchut (the Daughter) returning to Binah (the Mother)</note>.

<margin>H-H-V</margin> This teaches of the four elements which are under the "Hand" <note>The letter Yud, which equals 10</note> from the power of Beauty. *This is none other than the Blessed He* from the power of the four elements. Its combination teaches of the attribute of Aaron, who draws near by means of the final letter *He* in Beauty. Its depiction is five powers teaching of the matter and the entire Structure.

<margin>N-T-H</margin> This teaches of the Person <note>Adam</note> from whom authority is given to the Destroyer <note>Machbel, a destructive angel</note>, but she herself prepares (<gloss>Another version: the freedom of</gloss>) the world in righteousness to attach to the Righteous One. *This is none other than the Blessed He*, and He is included in two attributes. Its combination teaches thus, and its depiction is five powers teaching the secret of the Soul that emerges from Understanding and passes through the authority of Righteousness <note>Tzedek</note> to Faith <note>Emunah</note>. He spoke of the "Great Might" because Beauty is formed from their combination.

<margin>H-A-A</margin> This teaches of three attributes included in the absolute name A-H-H. *This is none other than the Blessed He*, with one power and one attribute. Its combination teaches of the six included within them, and its depiction is eight powers teaching of the matter in all that is included in Jacob: the form of Kindness and Awe. Furthermore, it is the secret of the form of the Perfect Structure—upper, first, and last—to the one who understands the secrets of the Awesome One. <margin>Y-R-T</margin> This teaches that she is entirely life, and He does not move from His place eternally. *This is none other than the Blessed He* in His unity, for He exists forever. Its combination teaches of two attributes that receive from one another until "Dread" <note>Pachad, another name for the Sefirah of Gevurah/Might</note>. Its depiction teaches that everything shall be joined in the *Yod* and in its "thorn" <note>The decorative tip of the letter Yod, representing the Infinite</note>, like a body that does not move eternally.

<margin>Sh-A-H</margin> This teaches Eternity <note>Netzach</note> in Beauty; with its power, He acted in Egypt, the external realm of the upper world. *This is none other than the Blessed He*, for He went out from His place to judge in Egypt, for the Heart was attached to consume and pass through Egypt to strike. Its combination teaches of the matter, and the depiction is nine powers acting entirely in Egypt. From this, you shall understand its action.

<margin>R-Y-Y</margin> This teaches the letters of the Sefirot that emerge from the Star and Understanding from Wisdom. *This is none other than the Blessed He* from the final Sabbath of Wisdom. Its combination teaches thus, and its depiction is three powers teaching of the inheritance of Wisdom and the Upper Wisdom.

<margin>A-V-M</margin> This teaches of two workers called by a single name, with the Foundation <note>Yesod</note> in their midst, which is a witness for them. *This is none other than the Blessed He*: the "Righteous One is the Foundation of the World" <note>Proverbs 10:25</note>. Its combination teaches thus, and its depiction is six powers teaching the six extremities <note>The six spatial directions or the six Sefirot from Chesed to Yesod</note>. <margin>L-K-B</margin> This teaches of a Throne for a single Throne. *This is none other than the Blessed He*, who "Rides upon the Patriarchs." Its combination teaches of the Crown between Wisdom and Understanding. Its depiction is five powers attached to Beauty and [the remainder is abbreviated].

<margin>V-Sh-R</margin> This teaches the fulfillment of the oath in its strength and its attachment at the end. Thus is the One, the master of Kabbalah. Its combination teaches thus, and its depiction is six powers teaching of the matter. <margin>Y-H-V</margin> This teaches of two workers from the six for a single unity. He spoke of the Lord. Its combination teaches of them and all that is hidden. Its depiction is four powers teaching of the two lights <note>The Sun and the Moon</note> and the secret of two workers for one. Its combination is thus. <margin>L-H-Ch</margin> This teaches of two "Beings" <note>Havayah - names of God</note> that are in the place of Wisdom and Understanding, which flow into the *Yod* which is in the place of Primordial Man <note>Adam Elyon</note>, or is he himself. Its combination teaches of Understanding flowing into the *Yod*, and its depiction consists of powers teaching of the matter.

<margin>K-V-Q</margin> This teaches the Name with the Judgments as a single power. Its combination teaches of the Crown, Beauty, and Understanding. Its depiction is five powers teaching of Kingship <note>Malchut</note> attached to Understanding and with two workers. Here, you shall understand the power of the action.

<margin>M-N-D</margin> This teaches of when Moses ascended to the heights, and it is already explained with its combination. Its depiction is that Understanding acts through Kindness with its two workers, and they cause flow.
<margin>A-N-Y</margin> This teaches that this word is from this, to be a complete *Yod* and comprised of the letter *Yod* and the Fifty Gates <note>The 50 Gates of Understanding</note>. *This is none other than the Blessed He* in the holy *Nun* of the upper world. Its combination...

# The Great Book of Raziel

When a person brings an offering <note>original: "Korban"</note> from among you, from the animals—according to the secret of the face of the ox that was transformed into a pure Cherub <note>A reference to the mystical transformation of the living creatures in Ezekiel's vision</note>—and from the pure birds, it is for the sake of the Sabbatical Cycle <note>original: "Shemittah"; here referring to the Kabbalistic theory of cosmic cycles</note> that causes the animal to be pure. The animal is purified through the secret of the one who purifies with purifying waters, for from the Sabbatical Cycle there remains a portion for the needs of the worlds in the measure of Water and Kindness <note>original: "Chesed," the Sefirah of expansive benevolence</note>. So it is for every Sabbatical Cycle: portions remain from them for the purpose of guiding the worlds that emerge from each cycle. Thus, this Sabbatical Cycle purifies and brings them close from the flock, which is the essence of the offering, as it is said: "You shall bring your offering." Through the "House of Kindness," remember and flow; this is a hidden secret. And if it is from the birds—which is hidden in a secret—even if their arrival for the day of their disappearance is not mentioned, and even if the upper and lower realms are joined in this, it is not at the "Head," for there are thirteen attributes of power <note>The 13 Attributes of Mercy, here associated with "Gevurot" or strengths</note> that do not return empty. This is because of the place of their nourishment, where there are thirteen diagonal boundaries <note>A concept from the "Sefer Yetzirah" (Book of Formation) describing the directions of the universe</note>. From these comes the essence of the oath in its encompassing. Moses alluded to this when he said, "Now, please, let the power of my Lord be great," for with this there will be a complete union for the 613 commandments, a full Name, and an Upper Name. For from there, everything revolves, and there are seventy-two bridges for the seventy-two awesome names; His actions are very great. And this is the first:

<margin>V-H-V</margin> This teaches of the Great and Honored Name <note>The Tetragrammaton, YHWH</note> in its union and its form. There are four powers that teach of the inclusion in the tenth <note>likely referring to the Sefirah of Malchut, the tenth sphere</note> more so than the rest, which is the Crown of Beauty <note>original: "Ateret Tiferet," a title for the Divine Presence</note> called "Gold." Concerning this, it is said: *this is none other than the Blessed He* <note>original: עזאב"ה — an abbreviation for "Ein Zeh Ela Baruch Hu"</note>, for the abundance descends into the Sanctuary of Y-H-V-H, and from there it descends to the whole world.

<margin>Y-L-Y</margin> This is a hint to the Upper "H" <note>The first letter 'He' in the Name YHWH</note>, the Blessed One, the Upper One of complete mercies, entirely white. From Him comes "Power" <note>Gevurah</note> for the upper beings and "Power" to speak blessing. Its form consists of four powers that teach of the matter and "Bless." *This is none other than the Blessed He*, for He is the Ancient of all ancients, and His companion and His form teach of the matter.

<margin>S-I-T</margin> This teaches of great, complete mercies and those that are not complete, through which there is judgment and righteousness in the "Land of the Living." It is a union of three powers that teach of the matter. Its form is five powers, for the two are from the unions, and the two are one within the other—the Shekhinah <note>Divine Presence</note> within the Tabernacle—and the fifth of them is the letter *Vav*, teaching of the place of life in Eternity <note>Netzach</note>. *This is none other than the Blessed He*, the Living One who exists forever.

<margin>A-L-M</margin> This teaches of two Beings <note>original: "Havayot," synonymous with the Name YHWH</note> which are in the place of the complete ones, Y-H. He is exalted in upper judgments, and between them, He judges all. Its form is seven clinging powers that teach of the entire Structure <note>The Sefirotic system</note>. *This is none other than the Blessed He*, for He appeared, and His authority is in the judgments to execute judgment in Egypt with the three verses <note>Exodus 14:19-21, each containing 72 letters, used to derive the 72 Names</note> from which everything comes. The number is four unions: Foundation <note>Yesod</note> to the Crown, Splendor <note>Tzvi</note>, Beauty <note>Tiferet</note>, the Upper, and the Middle; and they teach of the matter.

<margin>M-H-S</margin> This teaches the secret of the four houses of the *Tefillin* <note>Phylacteries worn during prayer</note>. Let the "H" cling to the Upper and hidden light. Its union teaches of the Crown of Beauty, the lower "H" within the "H" included from the *Tefillin* letter *Shin*. Its shape teaches of a complete structure, the garments of examination that teach of the matter. *This is none other than the Blessed He* through the power of Eternity <note>Netzach</note> the conqueror, which is two "Het" letters <note>ח-ח</note>. Its form is the victorious one for righteousness through the righteousness of the Patriarchs and in them.

<margin>L-L-H</margin> This teaches of a Chariot <note>Merkavah</note> upon a Chariot for the Unique One, through the secret of the two that ascend. Its union is Beauty and Beauty for great mercies clinging to a single Unity. Its form is six powers that teach of the matter. *This is none other than the Blessed He*, for the world was created with mercy and with Ten Utterances from the known first utterance—the final "A" <note>Aleph</note>. From its union, you shall understand the matter.

<margin>A-K-A</margin> This teaches of the "B" in the Foundation <note>Yesod</note>, where all souls and Sabbatical Cycles are in His light for eternal life, in the clinging of the Soul and the Sabbatical Cycle. Its union teaches of three Sefirot which teach through three vowel points: *Holam*, *Shuruk*, and *Hirik*. Its form is seven powers teaching that she clings within them. *This is none other than the Blessed He*, for it teaches of atonement, leadership, and life. From within these powers, you will understand the power of the actions in their measure; and so for all of them.

<margin>K-H-T</margin> This teaches as the "Master of the Action" said. Its form is five powers, all of which teach of the "Hand" <note>The Hebrew word for "Hand" (Yad) has a numerical value of 14</note> that clothes itself in the building of these forms that come to it from the Torah. From its union, you will understand that she shall be exalted over the peace of righteousness to righteousness. *This is none other than the Blessed He* said, for He is the covenant of our God in heaven, carved from the Upper Wisdom <note>Chokhmah</note>.

<margin>H-Z-Y</margin> This teaches that the Unity is clinging to the Crown <note>Keter</note>. *This is none other than the Blessed He*, the Lord who is high above all high ones—meaning above all the attributes <note>Middot/Sefirot</note>. Its form is four powers that teach of the matter and the Unity. Its second union is the "H" in the "Hand" <note>Yad</note>, and the "Conqueror" is the exchange in the matter for the wise. For the four were exalted to the "H", and both are in Eternity <note>Netzach</note> as a single Unity.

<margin>A-L-D</margin> This teaches of a Chariot upon a Chariot for the One, which is Beauty. *This is none other than the Blessed He*, for He shall be exalted on high. Its union is three powers: Kindness <note>Chesed</note> and Beauty within the Upper Beauty, in Understanding <note>Binah</note>. Its form is six shapes: Crown of Beauty to another Beauty.

<margin>L-A-V</margin> This teaches of two workers who receive from Understanding. *This is none other than the Blessed He*, for the four were exalted; judgment in Egypt from the place of dominion and fear. Its form is six powers that join to one and teach of the matter. The union of unity is three into one.

<margin>H-H-A</margin> This teaches what the Master of the Action teaches, and from the union, it teaches like two workers.

<margin>Y-Z-L</margin> The letter *Yod* teaches of the Great Name in the power of Eternity <note>Netzach</note>. Its form is four powers that are profound in the matter for the one who understands. *This is none other than the Blessed He* in the Foundation <note>Yesod</note>—He performed everything, and by the will of all powers, the sphere and the angel are from the power of Eternity. Its union teaches of two workers who rule over every power with the power of Eternity.

<margin>M-B-H</margin> This teaches that the Great Name is a name written as "Unity" <note>original: "Yachad"</note>. Its form is six powers that teach of the six directions and the blessings. *This is none other than the Blessed He* is the power of the Holy One, Blessed be He, which He sent to Moses Himself and in His glory. Its union is Beauty in Wisdom, in its dust and in every level as one, and a single power. From within these powers...

# The Great Book of Raziel

<margin>Vav-Mem-Bet <note>The 19th of the 72 Names of God</note></margin> This teaches the four powers of the Blessed Name <note>The Tetragrammaton: Y-H-W-H</note> and the two boundaries that rotate the day to illuminate it and the night with its gifts. Their combination <note>original: "v’ez’a’b’h" — likely a kabbalistic acronym for a meditative formula</note> guides them like a pleasing aroma toward the West for a singular unity. Its connection teaches this, and its form is the letter **He** <note>The fifth letter of the Hebrew alphabet, often representing the breath of creation</note>; everything is included in the secret of the **He**. The Name **Y-H-W-H** teaches of a unique unity with the Crown <note>term: "Atarah," another name for the Sefirah of Malchut (Kingdom), representing the physical manifestation of God's rule</note>. The formula <note>v’ez’a’b’h</note> is the secret of the letter **He** which searches its world based on what it received; its connection teaches this, and its form is the five powers that teach that everything is within the Blessed Name.

<margin>Ayin-Nun-Vav <note>The 20th Name</note></margin> This teaches that the Assembly of Israel is equal in weight to the upper beings. Because of this, when they sanctify and unify—both these and those together—to be attached and included, both the upper and lower realms are joined. However, the "branches" <note>Metaphor for the lower spiritual extensions</note> are alive and exempt. The formula <note>v’ez’a’b’h</note> teaches the dispersion of languages, its connection teaches by word, and its form is five powers; everything is included in the Crown of the letter **He**. From this, you shall understand the power of the mystical action. **Mem-Cheit-Yod** <note>The 21st Name</note> teaches the "Two Sanctifications" <note>likely referring to the Kedushah prayer</note>; the Assembly of Israel is attached to the Upper One in a single unity among the upper and lower beings to "connect the Tent" <note>A Tabernacle reference meaning to unify the spiritual and physical dwellings of God</note>. The formula <note>v’ez’a’b’h</note> is so that He may be with them simultaneously and for another connection. Its connection teaches the secret of "Two Actors" <note>term: "Two Actors" (Shenei Poalim), a kabbalistic concept referring to the balance of two opposing or complementary forces, like Mercy and Judgment</note> with the entire building of creation. Its form is five powers which teach that everything is included in the strength of the Will.

<margin>Dalet-Mem-Bet <note>The 22nd Name</note></margin> This teaches of the seven lamps from the Sefirah of Beauty <note>term: "Tiferet," the central Sefirah representing balance and harmony</note> and seven in the Crown. The formula <note>v’ez’a’b’h</note> teaches that just as seven illuminate above, so do seven below, and they are the seven righteous ones. Its connection teaches this, and its form is five powers teaching that everything is included in the Blessed Name.

<margin>Mem-Nun-Qof <note>The 23rd Name</note></margin> This teaches through the Name of four letters that two attributes surround Beauty, and likewise, from them, two camps of angels from their power surround the Crown, which is called the **Tree of Life** and the well-known **Tower of Jerusalem**. The formula <note>v’ez’a’b’h</note> shows that from them, He radiates into the Upper Eden. Its connection teaches the connection of the entire "building" <note>the structure of the universe</note>, and the letter **He** in this represents one camp. Its form is five powers teaching that everything is included in the Crown. **Aleph-Yod-Ayin** <note>The 24th Name</note> teaches of two actors. Moses inverted it in the secret of his own need, so that he would not see his soul in the Upper Jerusalem while his physical self remained in the Lower Jerusalem. This one requests from the upper to the upper and appears to himself; the Upper accepted him, but the Lower did not accept him because of the secret of the Hidden Upper. The formula <note>v’ez’a’b’h</note> is because, through this Name, he [Moses] sought to enter the Land [of Israel] but was not heard. Its connection teaches of Beauty and the Crown over the seventy judgments, and by its hidden and sublime power, he did not enter the Land. Its form is six powers that teach of this matter.

<margin>Cheit-Bet-Vav <note>The 25th Name</note></margin> This teaches of the absolute unity. Both the formula <note>v’ez’a’b’h</note> and its connection represent "Twenty-Eight" <note>The numerical value of the word "Koach" (Power)</note>, eight to another. Its form is five powers teaching of the matter.

<margin>Resh-Aleph-He <note>The 26th Name</note></margin> These teach of the eight names: the seven in the hinted names corresponding to the seven names of the 42-letter Name, and in the eighth, everything is included in the Jubilee <note>term: "Yovel" (Jubilee), the 50th year or the Sefirah of Binah (Understanding)</note>. The formula <note>v’ez’a’b’h</note> is because they are eight Sefirot in Thought and four in the time of the Sabbatical Cycle <note>term: "Shemittah," the 7-year cycle</note>. So it is among the lower beings, to move until their first place. Its connection teaches this, and its form is six powers that teach the sum of the "Ends" <note>The six directions of the physical world</note>. **Yod-Bet-Mem** <note>The 27th Name</note> teaches that the Crown, through the power of Beauty, is attached one to the other as a single power. The formula <note>v’ez’a’b’h</note> hints at the final attribute in which everything is included. Its connection teaches the matter, and its form is five powers teaching the matter, for everything is included in the Blessed Name.

<margin>Dalet-Qof-Vav <note>The 28th Name</note></margin> This teaches that these caused the entire secret of Creation and the secret of the Ten Sefirot through the secret of the "Three Sanctifications" within them, each of which teaches ten in a similar manner—the House of God to the Sanctuary—as it is said: **Yod** upon **Yod** <note>The letter Yod has a value of 10</note>. These are the three inclusions regarding the secret of the Sefirot and their emanation. They are the three upper ones, three in the middle (where the Torah is hinted), and three lower ones—every utterance, every action, and every commandment. Within the Sabbatical cycle and within the "All," the entire secret of the paths of the Sabbatical cycle is found—their strength, their course, and their end in the "Foundation" <note>term: "Yesod," the Sefirah of connection and procreation</note> for them. This is a connection, and then it is called the "Foundation of the Fifty-Two" <note>The numerical value of the word "Ben" (Son) or a specific Name of God</note>, for everything shall return to Him to whom the Earth belongs. The Exodus from Egypt: the upper beings praised it because they were attached with a strong attachment, like the mountain of the third day. From there to the three upper ones, which are very hidden and concealed in their known secret. The formula <note>v’ez’a’b’h</note> refers to when it was said to the Children of Israel: "This is my name forever, and this is my memorial from generation to generation" <note>Exodus 3:15</note> and all that is written there. Its connection teaches in its way, and its form is four awesome powers that teach of holiness and the entire building.

<margin>Mem-Vav-Samekh <note>The 29th Name</note></margin> This teaches that he sealed with two actors in which and from which everything exists. The formula <note>v’ez’a’b’h</note> is because its beginning and end are in the power of Mercy, as mentioned above. For in the "Eight" <note>representing the realm beyond the physical seven</note> is included the six "Ends" <note>directions</note> which they constantly sanctify; they are two actors, the power that said "the heavens" to include more, and "the earth" to include more. Its connection teaches this, and its form is five, in which everything is included, and everything is within it.

Behold, the secret of the explicit and highly sublime Name is completed. It is explained through its awesome secrets, within which is every secret of heaven and earth and every precious action, by the power of its connection, its form, and its numerical value. Each individual Name is explained through its awesome secrets as it is clarified, with powers that are awesome, sublime, and very high. All of these came from the power of **Understanding** <note>term: "Binah," the third Sefirah, often associated with the Divine Mother and the source of the 50 Gates</note> and the bond of its awesome paths that are attached to the fifty days. These are the **Fifty Gates** in seven complete Sabbaths. The holy power of each soul is included in the power of the secret of the "Two Actors." They are all five, two including and clinging together. From it come powers for thousands of distinct paths, holy angels, every star, sphere, firmament, *Ofan* <note>type of angel</note>, and *Chayah* <note>type of angel</note>, every prince, ruler, and powers—inner and outer, pure and impure, distances, and awesome upper groups, each differing from the other, each at his camp and each at his banner, and awesome names. Everything is by a single power: the **72 Names** in their permutations and connections, by the form and calculation of every single letter in its power and form. Awesome upper powers from the power of the twenty-two <note>The text says "28" (Ch-T) or "22" (K-B) letters — context suggests the 22 letters of the Hebrew alphabet</note> awesome upper letters, from which fine marks are engraved... their forms in their awesome writing are all hidden, sealed, and closed by the power of the **Fiftieth Gate** within the fifty upper and hidden days in the secret of the **Yod** and its sublime tip <note>The "point" on top of the letter Yod, representing the infinite source</note> in its secret, Hallelujah! From there they were emanated into **Understanding** <note>Binah</note> and **Wisdom** <note>Chokhmah</note>, and they brought to light all upper forms and all lower ones to be a single solid work. The hidden holy power in Understanding—behold, they are fifty complete ones, connected and connecting every form to its form and every power to its powers, forms for five connected shapes in the secret of the **Final He**.

# The Great Book of Raziel

<margin>Bread of the Sign <note>original: "פת ת'ו'י" – likely a gematria calculation or specific mnemonic</note></margin> This is attached to Understanding <note>term: "Binah," the third Sefirah, representing the womb of creation and divine intellect</note> and its form consists of five powers that guide the matter: **He-Ayin-Mem** <note>The 21st Name</note> guides through the power of the Moon's camps within the twelve tribes of Israel and the Beauty <note>term: "Tiferet," the central Sefirah of balance and harmony</note> between two attributes for the One. And the meditation on the letters <note>original: "v’ez’a’b’h" — an acronym for "And with the letters of the Blessed He," a meditative formula</note> engraved in the Name consists of ten; everything is from Him and within Him. Its connection guides the entire structure, and its form consists of seven powers that guide the matter.

<margin>Resh-He-Ayin <note>The 22nd Name</note></margin> This guides the Lord who rides the heavens <note>original: "aravot," the highest of the seven heavens in Jewish cosmology</note> over the fourteen living creatures <note>term: "Hayot," high-ranking angels described in Ezekiel's vision</note>, the seraphim, and the "branches," for everything is beneath Him. Its secret is that everything serves as a Chariot <note>term: "Merkavah," the divine throne-chariot representing how God's presence descends into the world</note> for Him, and also everything is for Beauty and the formula of the letters. The Name of four letters <note>The Tetragrammaton, Y-H-W-H</note> is over all, and its connection and form guide this matter.

<margin>Yod-Yod-Nun <note>The 23rd Name</note></margin> This guides with the letters; the letter **Kaph** <note>numerical value 20</note> and the letter **Bet** <note>numerical value 2</note> are a chariot for the One. The final **He** is to provide "flavor" to it. The meditation on the letters is the attribute of God and for the final **He**. Its connection is the two of Wisdom <note>term: "Chokhmah," the second Sefirah, the flash of divine inspiration</note> within Victory <note>term: "Netzach," the seventh Sefirah, associated with endurance and prophecy</note>, which guides the matter, and its form is thus. **He-He-He** <note>The 24th Name</note> guides Understanding, Might <note>term: "Gevurah," the Sefirah of judgment and restraint</note>, and the Crown <note>term: "Atarah," or Malchut, the lowest Sefirah representing the physical world</note>. From them comes the power of the ten living creatures. Its connection is thus and its form:

<margin>The guide in Lovingkindness <note>term: "Chesed," the Sefirah of infinite grace</note> has six powers.</margin> **Mem-Tzadi** <note>The 25th Name</note> guides three actions: the sphere, the garment, and the ten, which are a throne for Him. The "branches" have the power of grace, and concerning the formula of the letters, for the upper and lower are both included in the ten. Everything and its connection is over all, and its form consists of four powers that guide; from this, you shall understand the power of the operation.

<margin>Vav-Vav-Lamed <note>The 26th Name</note></margin> This guides by two agents, which is the Name of 42 <note>An ancient mystical name of God consisting of 42 letters</note>. It is exalted in unity and is called the Foundation of the World, which has twelve diagonal boundaries <note>A reference to the "Sefer Yetzirah" describing the dimensions of space</note>. The meditation on the letters is because they are in the Sanctuary of God <note>original: "b'y'h" — likely referring to the Temple or a divine contraction</note> and the Name is upon it. Its form and connection guide the matter.

<margin>Yod-Lamed-He <note>The 27th Name</note></margin> This guides the upper and lower Wisdom. Regarding the meditation on the letters: five attributes of the thirteen attributes of mercy; the fourth is mercy and grace, and the fifth is included in all of them until the thirteenth. Its connection guides thus, and its form consists of five powers; the letter **Yod** receives from all of them. **Samekh-Aleph-Lamed** <note>The 28th Name</note> guides because there are three angels: Esteriel, Adriel, and Sandalphon. They are the power of three palaces within the ten Sefirot. The meditation on the letters is through the power of three letters that lead the worlds from the supernal power; the upper ones are mercy, and the third is inclusive. Its connection is three times "Tiferet" <note>Beauty</note> for a single unity, and its form consists of seven powers.

<margin>Ayin-Resh-Yod <note>The 29th Name</note></margin> This guides the Crown, which has seventy levels; these are the powers under her hand. All the "branches" are in the Law which is under her hand until the tenth, which is the letter **Qof** <note>numerical value 100</note>, meaning for the Honorable Name. The meditation on the letters with the holy **Yod** are all doubled, for the tens are doubled in everything, and in the night in everything. Its connection guides in this way and its form consists of five powers that guide the matter. **Ayin-Shin-Lamed** <note>The 30th Name</note> guides the Crown, the fulfillment of the oath, and the Moon's camps in thirteen diagonal boundaries. The meditation on the letters is because they are the "Seal of the Rainbow" for the sustenance of the earth. Its connection and form consist of nine powers that guide in matter and in secret.

<margin>Mem-Yod-He <note>The 31st Name</note></margin> This guides by two agents for a single unity. Regarding the meditation on the letters: because the hidden name was not known to them, for they belong to Moses. Its connection is Beauty, and she and the people <note>original: "v’ha-am"</note> are the Crown. Its form consists of five powers that guide for the recipient from the right side in the secret.

<margin>Vav-He-Vav <note>The 32nd Name</note></margin> This guides already that "3" is part of "3," and it is in the saying "the end is wedged in the beginning" <note>A famous quote from Sefer Yetzirah: "The end is wedged in the beginning and the beginning in the end"</note> for a single unity. The meditation on the letters is the secret of the final **He** of the Name in the final third part. Its connection is the entire structure for the unity from the **Yod** to the Crown, and its form consists of four powers that guide the matter; from this, you shall understand.

<margin>Dalet-Nun-Yod <note>The 33rd Name</note></margin> This guides that it is the root of three names for the Crown, three letters from the three of them. The meditation on the letters is because it is the root of the Name from them: **Y-H-V** in three sanctities: upper, lower, and middle. Its connection guides thus, and its form consists of fifty powers <note>A reference to the Fifty Gates of Understanding</note>, guiding everything as a single unity.

<margin>He-Het-Shin <note>The 34th Name</note></margin> This guides the essence of the structure within the Name, and everything is included in ten. Regarding the meditation on the letters: the third includes everything in the Upper Victory. Its connection guides the matter, and its form consists of eight powers that guide the entire structure. **Ayin-Mem-Mem** <note>The 35th Name</note> guides the branches of the Tree from the eight and from the five into the eight for all. The meditation on the letters: from the branches of the tree, power is given to the celestial spheres and stars from the power of the "movers." Its connection guides upper and lower for one connection in the Foundation <note>term: "Yesod," the ninth Sefirah, which funnels divine energy into the world</note> of the people. Its form consists of seven powers that guide the matter. **Nun-Nun-Aleph** <note>The 36th Name</note> in the Foundation guides the secret of four powers that come from the power of life and blessings. These are awesome powers emerging from the radiance of the glow of Wisdom. Everything is hidden in the ancient Wisdom—upper and awesome beings—and a single unity over every hidden and concealed thing. The meditation on the letters is because the upper powers praise the Name every day with one hundred types of praise; because of this, the "rectification of Israel" is one hundred blessings. Its connection and form consist of five powers that guide the matter.

<margin>Nun-Yod-Tav <note>The 37th Name</note></margin> This guides the secret of the letter **Tav** <note>The last letter of the Hebrew alphabet</note>, which gives permission to the "destroyer" but is turned into a blessing for Israel. The meditation on the letters: because He is the Righteous One <note>term: "Tzadik," a title for the Sefirah of Yesod</note> who possesses the power of the ten, and from there to the world comes the "leanness" <note>original: "razon," perhaps meaning mystery or contraction</note>, and everything is one seal. Its connection guides thus, and its form consists of four powers that guide a complete unity.

<margin>Mem-Bet-He <note>The 38th Name</note></margin> This guides through the power of the Crown with its hosts; everything is fashioned within Man <note>Refers to Adam Kadmon, the primordial cosmic man</note>. The meditation on the letters: He commanded to descend into Man, who is a miniature world <note>The concept of the microcosm</note>. Its connection guides everything toward a single unity, and its form consists of six powers that guide the ten and are included in the five. **Pe-Vav-Yod** <note>The 39th Name</note> guides by Himself and by His glory what He performed in Egypt. The meditation on the letters: the ten plagues are equal in weight to the ten blessings. Its connection is Sanctuary within Sanctuary, a connection of two agents. Its form consists of four powers that guide the matter; from these powers, you shall understand the operations. **Nun-Mem-Mem** <note>The 40th Name</note> guides like the light of the Moon within the Sun; the light of the Sun and Moon are both attached to the fifty <note>Gates of Understanding</note>. Then the Shekhinah <note>term: "Shekhinah," the Divine Presence dwelling in the world</note> will dwell at the time when Israel makes joy and a Jubilee <note>term: "Yovel," the 50th year of liberation and rest</note> to attach them together. Concerning this, it is said that the Holy—which is the "Nets" <note>original: "ha-netz," perhaps meaning the sprout or the hawk/eye</note>—is seven within the holiness of the Crown. Its connection guides two lights to join them in the Jubilee, and its form consists of five powers that guide the end of joy in the Jubilee. **Yod-Yod-Lamed** <note>The 41st Name</note> guides that the letter **Yod** is exalted over everything He created to do. The meditation on the letters: for that power is exalted over all. Its connection guides the structure and the people toward a single unity, and its form consists of four powers that guide the matter.

<margin>Dalet-Resh-He <note>The 42nd Name</note></margin> This guides in complete unity, and thus is the meditation on the letters. Its connection guides the Land of the Living toward a complete structure, and its form consists of five powers included in everything.

<margin>Mem-Tzadi-Yod <note>The 43rd Name</note></margin> This guides as these two names whose operation changes before the Place <note>term: "Ha-Makom," a name for God</note> where they are found for the needs of the year in secret. Regarding the meditation on the letters: everything is one and there is no second in power. Its connection guides in this way: the Crown of Beauty is in Understanding. Its form consists of five powers of influence, including two.

# The Great Book of Raziel

**Page 28**
**Header: The Great Book of Raziel**

In truth, the power of His Name is divided into the portions of His mighty deeds, which ascend in degrees, as above so below. This is the meaning of: "And let them make Me a sanctuary" <note>Exodus 25:8</note>, which is aimed toward its counterpart, the Sanctuary above. As it is said: "High and lifted up" <note>Isaiah 6:1</note>. And corresponding to it are the six pillars, which are the foundation of the building, like the "stones of void" <note>Isaiah 34:11</note>, for the structure of the building is upon the foundation. Regarding this, the six pillars are within all—meaning that "one high official is watched by a higher" <note>Ecclesiastes 5:7</note>. This is the Sanctuary above, which is the inner holiness. It emerged from potential into actualization below in order to facilitate the work of the perfect, upright, and complete practitioners. Their hidden essence and their seal <note>original: "hatimam," referring to the completion of the ritual</note> are the offerings: the burnt offerings, peace offerings, and sacrifices, intended to atone for man’s earthly actions. Then the divine will shall return to His will, and the action which they performed, and the deed and the power He gave them, and "the spirit shall return to God who gave it" <note>Ecclesiastes 12:7</note>. Then all shall become one place. "On that day the LORD shall be one and His name one" <note>Zechariah 14:9</note>.

In this, the four-letter Name <note>The Tetragrammaton: Y-H-W-H</note> is found complete in the name **Y-H** in its fullness. For **Y-H** <note>numerical value 15</note> in our calculation with **W-H** <note>numerical value 11</note> rises to twenty-one <note>Note: 15+6=21, the value of 'Ehyeh,' I AM</note>, adding to it this **H** to make a complete name. For the power is **Y-H**, as it is written: "For in Y-H, the LORD, is an everlasting rock" <note>Isaiah 26:4</note>. The letter **H** hints at the "Double He" <note>original: "He-He-Tav," likely referring to the two H's in the Tetragrammaton</note> which represent Understanding, Might, and Sovereignty <note>term: Binah, Gevurah, and Malchut — three of the ten Sefirot or divine emanations</note>, three for the last three attributes. The active force is **W-H**, as it is written: "If My covenant be not..." <note>Jeremiah 33:25</note>; it cannot exist without this. As written above, it is called "Essence" because each letter stands for itself, like five times **Y-H**. The uprightness of the LORD endures. Thus, every light changes from **H** to **H** for the health of the lower beings.

With the letter **H** He created them <note>referencing the Midrash that this world was created with the letter He</note>; it stands in the Might of its Creator, blessed be He. It would have been appropriate to say the Essential Name is **Y-H-W**, as has already been explained elsewhere in the secret of the ten letters, which are the ten Sefirot. It is called "Explicit" <note>term: "Mephorash" — the Explicit Name of God</note> because it rotates through seventy-two. This is what is meant by the verse: "I hope for Your salvation, O LORD" <note>Genesis 49:18</note>. And David said: "I hope for Your salvation, O LORD," and "With long life I will satisfy him and show him [My salvation]" <note>Psalm 91:16</note>. **Y, Y-H, Y-H-W, Y-H-W-H** <note>A common Kabbalistic progressive expansion of the Name</note>. 

From there emerge the seventy elders of the assembly, for "all the congregation are holy, and the LORD is among them" <note>Numbers 16:3</note>. Each one is in his greatness and might, "his sword upon his thigh" <note>Song of Songs 3:8</note>. They walk in the light of the King, following the form mentioned below, to bring to light His beauty, His praise, and the Name of His holiness in their form upon them, their King. As it is written: "Their King passes before them, and the LORD at their head" <note>Micah 2:13</note>. And behold, the first is called:

Its mystical form and its numerical value point to **Y-H-H** and to the six pillars with a **Vav** at their head.
Its mystical form **Y-K-W** points to it, and with it the letter name **A-H-H**, for they are the name of Might and Greatness.
Its mystical form **K-W-Sh** points to it, and with them the letters of the second **A-H-H**.
Its mystical form is with **A-K-W**. **K-W** <note>numerical value 26, the Tetragrammaton</note> points to two Names of Being <note>Y-H-W-H</note>, exalted in the seven holy names.
Its mystical form is **K-W** and the **Vav** of Israel, which were the memory of the Rainbow, but it points to the verse: "By God Almighty [El Shaddai], but by My name the LORD [Y-H-W-H]..." <note>Exodus 6:3</note>.
Its mystical form is **K-W, K-W**, and **D**, pointing to the four directions of the world which were created with ten utterances and sealed with the Names of Being.
The "Being" of the Name in the Holy of Holies is the seventh day, which is the "Future to Come." Their seal is in the name **A-G-A** <note>a protective divine acronym</note>. As you say: "on the tenth of this month." This is not so, rather it is **A-K-A**, which is rest and atonement for the worthy and pure souls in the Future to Come.
Its mystical form is in two Essential Names, and with the letter name in its power.

Its mystical form **K-D-Y-W** points to the thirty-two paths of Wisdom <note>the 32 paths of the Sefer Yetzirah</note> and the two tablets of the covenant, etc., which are in heaven; and below it mentions the secret of the Torah.
Its mystical form **D-Y-D** points to 26 <note>Y-H-W-H</note> and the final Aleph, and in its totality "Ram" [High], pointing to "High and Lifted Up."
Its mystical form **K-W, K-W-R** points to two Names of Being and the four wheels of the Chariot <note>term: Merkavah — the divine throne-chariot</note>.
Its mystical form **K-W, K-W-W** points to the Name of the Ruler over the heavenly host, and the second over the host of the Sanctuary, corresponding to the Sanctuary, and they are the six ends of the world.

<margin>
**H-H-A** <note>Name 12</note>
**Y-Z-L** <note>Name 13</note>
**M-B-H** <note>Name 14</note>
</margin>

Its mystical form points to **D, D-Y-A**, pointing to the Essential Name, and explicit in the 72-letter Name engraved upon the forehead-plate of Aaron the High Priest when he entered the Holy of Holies.
Its mystical form **Z-W-K-W** points to the Name of the LORD which is exalted over ten spheres and rules over the seven heavens in which His throne and host reside.
Its mystical form **K-D-W-D-Y** is the Great Prince, for the LORD called him "my beloved," saying: "My beloved [Dodi] is white and ruddy, preeminent" <note>Song of Songs 5:10</note>, for He sits upon the holy Chariot, and He is Y-H-W-H with two letters.

Its mystical form **D-Y-K-W-S** points to the Essential and Explicit Name, and the twelve stones that were engraved in the Sanctuary were these names.

<meta>This page continues the exposition of the mystical architecture of the heavens, moving from the concept of the earthly Sanctuary to the celestial Throne of Glory. It describes how the Hebrew letters are not merely linguistic tools but are engraved upon the very fabric of the divine realm to direct the forces of creation.</meta>

# The Great Book of Raziel

Just as the vessel <note>original: "kos," which can mean cup or vessel, here likely referring to the receptive form of the Throne</note> is the shape for its joining, He engraved them upon the Throne of Glory, each one in its place, to surround the Name of the Lord, the Honored and Awesome. These are the statutes within the tablets, and they are the source of all emanation, all power, and every action for everything that God created. Everything that exists in potential and in action—every upper power and deed, all the host of heaven and earth—exists through the upper power, by the decree of the Most High, by command, utterance, speech, and calling. All decrees come from the power of the letters, which are drawn, joined, and engraved upon the high and exalted Throne. They are engraved around the Throne called *Arvot* <note>The seventh and highest heaven in Talmudic cosmology</note>, which contains everything. As it is written in the Tractate Hagigah: "In *Arvot* are found the treasuries of righteousness, justice, blessing, life, and the peace of the Resurrection of the Dead, and the powers of souls."

This is called the "Throne for the Throne of His Glory," which is also called "Heaven" <note>original: "Shamayim"</note>, receiving from the Crown of Beauty <note>term: Ateret Tiferet; a reference to a specific divine emanation or Sefirah</note>. Therefore, it contains many storehouses of the souls of the righteous. It encompasses the ten *Sefirot* <note>The ten divine attributes or emanations in Kabbalah</note> within three Palaces, corresponding to the three divisions of the *Sefirot*. There is the Palace of Angels in the Garden of Eden, the World to Come, and the "Sanctuary within the Sanctuary"—meaning within His palace and His treasury. There, the Seraphim, the *Chayot HaKodesh* <note>term: Holy Living Creatures; high-ranking angels described in the vision of Ezekiel</note>, and the Holiness are depicted. The letters are engraved around the Throne in His holy habitation called "Heaven," which receives from the Crown. All of this is included within it just as it is in the Crown, for it is a Throne for the Lord. From there comes every decree and form for everything that God created to do. It is divided into three camps and palaces, corresponding to the Palace of the Lord, the Holy Palace in which everything exists, called the "Habitation" <note>original: "Ma'on"</note>. As it is said: "Look down from Your holy habitation, from heaven, and bless Your people Israel" <note>Deuteronomy 26:15</note>, for from there the blessing comes, as does every thing, for it is the Throne and the Throne of the Lord.

These are the twenty-two celestial letters engraved upon the Throne of Glory. There are also five "double" letters <note>The letters (Kaph, Mem, Nun, Pe, Tzadi) that take a different form at the end of a word</note>, which hint at the end of the final millennium of the Name, to give power, meaning, and expression to a matter. Thus, there are twenty-seven letters hidden and engraved around the Throne. From them, two awesome powers are hidden to provide strength and form to all: the letters *Yod* (י) and *Vav-Vav* (ו-ו). These are two names for each one, containing the secret of the *Sefirot*, the Paths, and the Names; every exalted and hidden secret comes from them. They are "Fire" <note>original: Esh</note> and "Spirit" <note>original: Ruach</note> cleaved to one another. From them comes every power, angel, and celestial prince. Everything is directed toward one power and one form that includes all. From them comes one awesome and supreme form made of the two letters *Yod* and *Vav-Vav*, which is called *Aleph* (א) <note>In mystical calligraphy, the letter Aleph is often drawn as a diagonal Vav with two Yods</note>. It is the first, giving power, form, and connection to the letters. Within it is the secret of the *Sefirot* and the holinesses for the six directions <note>North, South, East, West, Up, and Down</note>, through which everything above and below exists, as do the four doors of the world. This "vessel" is called the Secret of the Ten Sefirot and the twenty-two awesome letters; they are the second secret of the thirty-two awesome and hidden Paths of Wisdom.

The gathering of the letter *Yod* represents the 150 <note>The numerical value of the word "Ken" (Nest/Base) or a reference to the 150 Psalms</note> celestial and hidden days called *Hallelujah*. "Happy is the man who fears the Lord" <note>Psalm 112:1</note>. Let him guard his soul diligently when entering this path, not doing so without receiving the statutes regarding every matter and every single letter in its form and its awesome seals. If one changes them, the Name remains in them, but if used with proper tradition and uprightness, then he shall be called an "angel of the living God." His actions and his soul will be cleaved to the Spirit of the living God, and his soul will be very pure and holy. How wondrous is the secret of the honored Lord, explained in His secrets and all His actions and seals! Concerning this it is said: "Because he has set his love upon Me, therefore I will deliver him; I will set him on high, because he has known My name. He shall call upon Me, and I will answer him; I am with him in trouble; I will deliver him and honor him. With long life..."

->...will I satisfy him, and show him My salvation. Blessed be the Lord forever, Amen and Amen.<-

## The Gemara <note>term: Gemara; here used in the sense of "Completion" or "Study" of the preceding mystical theory</note>

**God** looked down from heaven upon the children of men to see if there were any who were wise, who sought God. For "let him who glories glory in this: that he understands and knows Me" <note>Jeremiah 9:23</note>. The magnitude of this matter upon the earth is that the Lord swore by His right hand and by His mighty arm, saying: "By My right hand you shall swear." The secret of this matter continues until the verse where the thing is revealed, in which the Great Teacher <note>possibly referring to Moses or a primary angelic teacher</note> was of our camp. He says: <note>original: "AAA AAA A'N ANO YHV YaB," a series of mystical letter permutations</note> "You are great, and Your name is great in might. Your right hand, O Lord, is glorious in power; Your right hand, O Lord, dashes the enemy in pieces. Your right hand, O Lord, is exalted; the right hand of the Lord performs valiantly. You have a mighty arm; strong is Your hand, and high is Your right hand" <note>Various fragments from Psalms and Exodus</note>.

He is "glorious in holiness," the Name that is supreme in the highest levels. He is the "High and Exalted God, inhabiting eternity," whose wonders are marvelous. He is high and wonderful in a great, awesome, and exalted wonder, uplifted in the secret of great holiness. The secret of His Might <note>original: Gevurah</note> is exalted, as it is said: "For the Lord GOD will do nothing, but He reveals His secret unto His servants the prophets" <note>Amos 3:7</note>. There, the awesome and hidden secret was revealed—He who is awesome among the powers and exalted alone in the secret of the holy ones, the Seraphim, the *Ophanim* <note>term: Ophanim; "Wheels," a class of angels from Ezekiel's vision</note>, the holy angels, and all the host of the heights who stand in awe and trembling. The earth and heavens shake when they hear the Name, the Name of the Lord of Hosts who sits upon the Cherubim. Those to whom He belongs saw the glory of the living God. There His might was revealed, as it is said: "I made Myself known to them." Indeed, the secret of the might of His Name was revealed to the man who was faithful and humble, as it is said: "In all My house he is faithful" <note>Numbers 12:7, referring to Moses</note>. This is the secret of which it is said: "For a hand is upon the throne of Yah" <note>Exodus 17:16</note>, and "Where is the house that you may build for Me, and where is the place of My rest? For all these things My hand has made" <note>Isaiah 66:1-2</note>, for "in Yah the Lord is an everlasting Rock."

"I will turn My hand upon you." It is further written: "The hand of the Lord was upon me. Hallelujah!" For the name of the Lord is called upon you. One should not speak at length about the depths of the depths; that is the "Great Deep" <note>original: Tehom Rabba</note>. Whoever has received the tradition of the forms of the Alphabet or understands the great and awesome secret—for the mountains are great and awesome sages, they are the early *Gaonim* <note>term: Gaonim; the heads of the Babylonian academies, here referring to ancient spiritual authorities</note> who did not reveal this secret. It is the Essential Name <note>term: Shem HaEtzem; the Tetragrammaton</note>, and it is written: "A garden locked, a spring sealed" <note>Song of Solomon 4:12</note>.

**"But where shall wisdom be found?"** <note>Job 28:12</note> And where is the place of understanding? For the first, the second, the second, and the second are **Yah**, and He is **Y-H-W-H**. Where is the place of the Holy Name? One cannot exist without the other, as it is said: "God has made this as well as that" <note>Ecclesiastes 7:14</note>. This is the power, and this is the action; the power emerges into action. This is what is meant by: "If My covenant be not with day and night, if I have not appointed the ordinances of heaven and earth" <note>Jeremiah 33:25</note>, for the divine action does not change. Everything goes to one place, for "I will proclaim the name of the Lord." This is the secret of His divinity, His unity, and His existence, as found in the secret of this book in truth.

# The Great Book of Raziel

And the second is "Their king shall pass before them, and the Lord at their head" <note>Micah 2:13</note>, and the third is "In me is the Lord." There are also three Palaces engraved upon the Throne of the Lord, as well as the Aleph-1, Bet-1 <note>Likely referring to a specific cipher or the hierarchy of letters</note>. This Name contains a great secret, as it says "The Lord, the Lord, God, merciful and gracious" <note>Exodus 34:6</note> three times together.

<margin>ERI</margin> And its form is set against the Name of the Lord <note>original: "Y-Y," often used as an abbreviation for the Tetragrammaton</note>, for there are 70 degrees within the power of the celestial spheres. It teaches that the Name of 72 is found within these degrees of the Sabbath <note>original: "sh-b," likely meaning the seventh level or the Sabbath power</note>, and the power of the ten spheres. This is as it is said: "I see Your heavens, the work of Your fingers" <note>Psalm 8:4</note>.

<margin>ASHL</margin> And its form is set against the letter *Lamed* <note>Numerical value 30</note>. it comes to invoke the covenant of Noah for the sake of the three Patriarchs contained within it, so that the world will not be destroyed any further. It is written: "And I will remember my covenant" <note>Genesis 9:15</note>—this follows the other—until the Covenant of the Rainbow. It is in this manner that "I will remember the land," as we mentioned regarding the form of the Name. Its numerical value is 26 <note>The value of the Tetragrammaton (YHWH)</note> and 70, representing two Names. After His Name, He rides over all.

<margin>MYH</margin> And its form is like the letter *Yod*. This is what is written: "I am the first and I am the last" <note>Isaiah 44:6</note>. It begins with "Their king shall pass before them, and the Lord at their head." His name is YHWH, as explained in the secret of the form when it says: "But by my name YHWH I was not known to them" <note>Exodus 6:3</note>, for He was not known to any creature among the first beings. Its numerical value is 26 and 26.

<margin>VHV</margin> And its form is *Vav-Dalet-Vav*. The first is like the last. Furthermore, "I am the first and I am the last" is what is stated: "See now that I, even I, am He, and there is no god with Me" <note>Deuteronomy 32:39</note>. Among the nations, this is what began with "And the Lord" and concluded with "Who" <note>original: "mi"</note>, and began with "And the Lord" and concluded with 49. For the secret is that the first is like the last. One should not elaborate further on these deep mysteries.

<margin>DNY</margin> And its form is itself, and it is the Name of the Lord. It is from the "Mothers" <note>term: "Imot"; in Kabbalah, these are the primary letters Aleph, Mem, and Shin, but here it refers to the root letters of the divine names</note>. Because of this, the letter *Aleph* is missing from it, just as it is missing from the Name YHWH to serve as "Mothers." It is the essential root for all the holiness of the Name, which are the "Fathers" according to a great secret. It indicates the six wings <note>referring to the Seraphim in Isaiah 6</note> toward the ends of the world. Because of this, three are removed and three remain, in order to bring the Chariot into the root of the form. Thus, there are two Living Creatures <note>term: "Chayyot"; the holy beings carrying the divine chariot</note> and two *Vavs* for the six branches and six cups <note>referring to the design of the Temple Menorah</note>. The *Vavs* are divided among them to illuminate them from the radiance of the Divine Presence (*Shekhinah*); they shall shine toward the face of the upper Menorah.

<margin>HCHSH</margin> And its form is *Dirosh*. It is a Name of *Shaddai* <note>Almighty</note>. The letter *Shin* in its form indicates that He appeared to the three Patriarchs, as it says: "And I appeared to Abraham, to Isaac, and to Jacob as God Almighty (*El Shaddai*)" <note>Exodus 6:3</note>. For the light of the Active Intellect which He planted in them says: "Seven lamps... for the pipes which are on top of it" <note>Zechariah 4:2</note>, for He mounted it upon the ten.

<margin>AMM</margin> And its form is three *Nuns*, 26, 26. This indicates two instances of the Name YHWH and the letter *Ayin* <note>70</note> for the 70 ministering angels who sanctify Him every day and prostrate themselves before His glory. It is written: "And they prostrate themselves before all the gods" <note>referring here to the heavenly host</note>, for they are the seven classes who sit first in the Kingdom. The form indicates the eighth sphere, for there lies the power of the souls of the stars that receive the decree from these princes.

<margin>NNA</margin> And its form is *NN-KV* <note>50-26</note>. It indicates the melody concerning what is written: "The fiftieth year shall be holy to you" <note>Leviticus 25:11</note>. These are twenty sanctities, and the "Holy Glory" remains. Behold, fifty sanctities come to one sanctity, for they are the one hundred blessings that Israel prays every day, and the Holy One, Blessed be He, watches over them. It is written: "What <note>original: "mah"</note> does the Lord your God ask of you" <note>Deuteronomy 10:12</note>—do not read it as "what" (*mah*), but as "one hundred" (*me'ah*). There is a guardian over them: "Their king shall pass before them, and the Lord at their head."

<margin>NYT</margin> And its form is *NY-VV*. It comes to mention the mighty deeds of the Holy One, Blessed be He, like the power of 410 <note>The numerical value of "Tav-Yod," the number of years the First Temple stood</note>. When He created the world, He established it upon one pillar, as it says: "The righteous is the foundation of the world" <note>Proverbs 10:25</note>. For the entire world was in darkness until the pillar arose to establish the world in the light of the 410. For it is a Name of 26, as we explained above, because it is the light of the great sun. We did not wish to elaborate there, for the light of the sun is the light of the Foundation (*Yesod*); this is the 410, and it is 26. What remains is "Sixty heroes surround it" <note>Song of Songs 3:7</note> for the 410, which is the Holy Name emerging from the known verse that is a blessing for Israel.

<margin>MBH</margin> And its form is like *YVHY*, two *Vavs* and two *Yods* with two letters. It is the name of Metatron <note>The supreme Archangel and celestial scribe</note>. For the Name is "Kiau Dori," as it says: "My beloved <note>original: "dodi," perhaps a variant of "dori" here</note> has gone down to his garden" <note>Song of Songs 6:2</note>, to the small world <note>The human being, often called the "microcosm" in mystical texts</note>.

<margin>PVY</margin> And its form is *KV-YY*. This is the Name whose name was sanctified in the Ten Commandments, and He showed His might in the ten plagues that He brought upon the Egyptians.

<margin>NMM</margin> And its form is *NKVK-Z*. These are two additional Names sanctified above the supreme holiness. It refers to the saying: "The fiftieth year shall be holy to you," for in the time of holiness, it is holy, for His Name is sanctified in holiness.

<margin>YYL</margin> And its form is *YY-KV*. His Name, which is sanctified, sustains His Throne of Glory upon ten spheres. His greatness is mostly in the Ten Utterances <note>The ten sayings by which the world was created</note>, as it says: "The heavens are My throne and the earth is My footstool" <note>Isaiah 66:1</note>, and it is written: "Like the dance of the two camps" <note>Song of Songs 7:1</note>.

<margin>HRCH</margin> And its form is *RYRR-V*, with the letter *Resh* lacking. It is a Name of 26, as it says: "But He is at one, and who can turn Him?" <note>Job 23:13</note>.

<margin>MTZR</margin> And its form is *KV-Y YNR*. And "My Lord" (*Adonai*) with the three letters of the Name. This is what is said: "The Lord God <note>original: "YHWH Adonai"</note> is my strength" <note>Habakkuk 3:19</note>.

<margin>YHH</margin> And its form is *YDYDY*. This is based on the deep form of the Name explained here. After it is the letter *Dalet* <note>4</note>, for after His truth and His oneness, there is no room to challenge. And it says "Enough" (*Dai*) of the healings to elaborate further <note>A common closing phrase in these sections, punning on the word "Dai" (enough)</note>.

It is exactly like the letter *Ayin* with seventy, and *Adonai* is the whole—all becomes unified, for *Adonai* rides upon seventy cubits and upon seventy degrees, and He is the Chariot and He is the Secret.

And so they are half of the Name, for with the other half, the peace is completed, and in it the matter is also found by the one who understands. # The Great Book of Raziel
### Page 5

<margin>The Staff</margin> One letter is engraved upon them, as it is written: "as frontlets between your eyes" <note>Deuteronomy 6:8; refers to the Tefillin worn on the head</note>. These are engraved in the three Palaces of the Throne. This form is the form of the Alphabet which Moses received from the mouth of the **Power** <note>original: "הגבורה" (*Ha-Gevurah*) — a traditional Jewish epithet for God, emphasizing His might</note>. Just as it is written, so is it engraved upon the Throne.

He chose four letters—*Y-H* and *H*—and placed Kindness <note>The Sephirah of *Chesed*</note> in the *Yod* and the *Heh*. For these are the two names of the Holy Essence if you investigate them, and each one is an essence unto itself. In the second, He chose *Yod* and *Vav* because the Name chose them according to the strength of their might and the power of their greatness. In them alone is the likeness of the Might, for the first is **fire** and the second is **spirit** <note>original: "רוח" (*Ruach*) — can mean wind, breath, or spirit</note>. As it is written of fire: "He is God," and of spirit: "The Spirit of God." The angels who surround the Throne of Glory are fire and spirit, as it is said: "He makes His angels spirits, His ministers a flaming fire" <note>Psalm 104:4</note>.

Two letters in the Power are similar to their Creator to show "the power of His works He has told to His people, to give them the heritage of the nations" <note>Psalm 111:6</note>. Their might is in them alone and their power is for themselves. They do not receive any form, power, or greatness from others; they do not move or stir from their place. Rather, through their power, a holy form is joined: "the King's daughter within" <note>Psalm 45:14; often interpreted in Kabbalah as the *Shekhinah* or the soul</note> to a King who is great, mighty, holy, and the unique Glory of His people. He gives strength, greatness, and a holy form to all His people who follow Him. His name is like the name of his Creator <note>likely referring to the angel Metatron, whose name is numerically equivalent to Shaddai</note>, and her power is also similar to her Creator, to say that He sustains, provides, and leads with the attribute of Mercy.

They are a "clanging symbol" <note>referencing rhythmic prayer or praise</note> to be His alone, the work of His hands. They crown them, sanctify them, declare them kings, and bow down to Him. From the strength of their might, Moses gave them in those days when they were made known to him. He sought mercy from before the **Power** and supplicated, saying: "The Lord, the Lord, God, merciful and gracious" <note>Exodus 34:6</note>, corresponding to the six wings of the angels who sanctify Him always. For he was in awe and supplicated thus before the Holy ones, for two correspond to two. This is a hint to include the **Ten Sephirot** <note>The ten divine emanations/channels through which God interacts with the world</note> which David mentioned, for he understood the hint of the matter and its secret. He said to you: "Yours, O Lord, is the greatness and the power..." <note>1 Chronicles 29:11</note> and they are ten in total, as the Kabbalists have explained in the Great Secret.

These words correspond to the five names mentioned by Moses, which together are six out of the ten, as Moses prayed: "O Lord God," "The Lord," "The Lord," "God" <note>original: "ה' אלהים ה' ה' אל"</note>. These correspond to the secret of the three "Holinesses," for three to three are like those who set out at evening. Understand this secret, for "In *Yah*, the Lord, is an everlasting Rock" <note>Isaiah 26:4</note>. The three are the secret of the **Three Mother Letters** <note>The letters Aleph, Mem, and Shin, which *Sefer Yetzirah* describes as the elements of the universe</note> explained in the *Book of the Ten Sephirot* regarding the twenty-two letters of the Torah. All of them are under their King, joining each one to its form and shape. These are the twenty-two letters, and they are those who "eat at the King’s table," each receiving a form according to its rank, deriving from the radiance of their King. It is said: "And their King shall pass before them, and the Lord at their head" <note>Micah 2:13</note>. As mentioned above, his name is like the name of his Master.

From their forms, the power of the Name is explained, as is the essence and power of the Holy Name of Names. From the **Explicit Name** <note>original: "שם המפורש" (*Shem HaMephorash*)</note> called the **Name of 72**, which is the Explicit Name, comes the power of the Name of the Essence. Everything goes to one place and everything is for one purpose. In the power of His trust and His mercy we lean, and in His salvation we trust. We approach with our souls and serve Him with a whole heart and a willing soul. In fear and awe we shall rejoice, and He will forgive our iniquities and our sins, as it is written: "And the Lord said, 'I have forgiven'" <note>Numbers 14:20</note>. He will restore our captivity and have mercy on us, as it is written: "I will be gracious to whom I will be gracious" <note>Exodus 33:19</note>, for He is a merciful and gracious King who abounds in doing good. Blessed be the Lord forever, Amen and Amen.

### The Operations
<note>This section shifts from theory to the practical requirements for the "Operations"—the mystical or theurgical rituals</note>

**Father:** I will come with the mighty acts of the Lord God; I will make mention of Your righteousness, Yours alone. O God, Your way is in holiness; who is so great a god as God? O God, You have taught me from my youth. Furthermore, I will tell of wonders. "Gird up your loins like a man." My son, "if your heart is wise, my heart will be glad, even mine." The wise will understand these things.

And now, my wise son, I will discipline you as a man disciplines his son. I will instruct you in this way you should go, for behold, "darkness shall cover the earth, and gross darkness the peoples," but "the Lord shall arise upon you." And now, my wise son, "be wholehearted with the Lord your God." Open your eyes and remove all "foreskin" from your heart <note>metaphor for spiritual blockages or insensitivity</note>. Let all your thoughts be surrendered into your hand. At all times "let your garments be white" and "put off your shoes from your feet," for the place where you enter is God's. Stand outside until the man who carries for you comes. You must inquire and search your entire palace, lest there be a stranger, or a "standing grain," or a "princess" <note>likely metaphorical warnings against impure influences or distractions</note>. "Make a parapet for your roof" <note>Deuteronomy 22:8</note>, lest the one who falls should fall from it near you.

The man is the holy one who shall be chosen from among you, from the Priests and your Levites, for the Lord your God has chosen them from all your tribes to seek and investigate all your desires. He shall offer your sacrifices. A stranger shall not eat of it. He shall not turn his heart away from your firstborn with all his soul and heart. "The living will lay it to his heart."

And now, my wise son, sustain the world, fear God, and turn away from evil. See, understand, and set your heart toward God in heaven, for "God will bring you into judgment for all these things." Whether you justify me or convict me by these words of mine, be glad, and let your thoughts rest. The High Priest shall be drawn from your brothers and your people—he who offers your sacrifices, the Levite who accompanies you. Let your heart be supported by the Name, and your life shall hang in doubt at your dwellings. But if you fail to see and look upon your nobles and your prophets, the holy things are in your hands; the Priest shall take the basket from your hand and set it down before the altar of the Lord your God.

And now, my wise son, the conclusion of the matter: "Fear God and keep His commandments." I will explain the **Tradition** <note>original: "קבלה" (*Kabbalah*)</note> to you through the explanation of the Honorable and Awesome Name, detailed with its powers, its wondrous secrets, and its essences, just as **Our Holy Rabbi** <note>Likely Judah the Prince, editor of the Mishnah</note> and **Rabbi Ishmael** <note>A famous scholar and mystic of the 2nd century</note> used it in their generation. This includes the secret of the calculation of the letters, the power, the form, and the action—all in the appointed times, hours, and known seasons.

If you are pure and upright, you will understand my words with wisdom. Hide them in your inner parts and write them on the tablet of your heart. Be strong in your intellect, for "it is the glory of God to conceal a matter." Know regarding these matters: the permutation is a wisdom and power unto itself; the reversal is a wisdom and power unto itself; and the calculation <note>Gematria</note> is a wisdom and power unto itself. All of them are powers of wondrous, mighty, and awesome forms. If the person of understanding comes with a pure heart, subtle, upright, and wholehearted—with praises, song, melody, and joy of heart—he may contemplate them, the first first and the last last, forever, without a ladder and without steps. And "lest the fallen one fall from his place."
