<margin>M-CH-Y</margin> And its form is thus: <note>original: "י' י"מ ב' ב'" — these are likely numerical or structural ciphers</note> The two sanctifications with which He is always sanctified are the secret of the Cherubim. In truth, these two sanctifications represent two groups: one group is Israel below, and the second group is the heavenly hosts above, who sanctify Him continuously.

<margin>D-M-B</margin> And its form is <note>numerical cipher</note>: seven within seven moldings to house the luminaries before Him.

<margin>M-N-Q</margin> And its form is <note>numerical cipher</note>: this points to the fifty hosts in the fifty-three <note>possibly referring to the 50 Gates of Understanding and 53 portions of the Torah</note>, for the ministering angels who always travel on the missions of the Name—whose mission is the twenty-two Foundations <note>The 22 letters of the Hebrew Alphabet</note>—their strength and essence surround the Glory, guarding the Glory of the Throne. They perform the mission of their Creator, as it is said: "He makes His angels spirits, His ministers a flaming fire" <note>Psalm 104:4</note>. Some of them guard the "flaming sword which turns every way" <note>Genesis 3:24</note> within the Garden. The second part points to the Name **Ehyeh** <note>original: אהי"ה (*Ehyeh*) — meaning "I AM," the name revealed to Moses at the burning bush</note> along with the sign of its explicit form and the Essence which is above all.

<margin>A-Y-A</margin> And its form is <note>numerical cipher</note>: this points to the two Awesome Names with which the righteous man supplicated when he said: "O Lord God, You have begun..." <note>Deuteronomy 3:24</note> with two letters.

<margin>CH-B-V</margin> And its form is upon it: he opened and said, "You are the Great God, the Lord, Holy and Awesome is His name forever," for He alone is the Essence, may His name be blessed.

<margin>D-A-H</margin> And its form is like the glories of truth. For with this Name, the prophet mentioned the names of His might and His essence to call upon their power for the newborn child: "And his name shall be called Wonderful, Counselor, Mighty God, Everlasting Father, Prince of Peace" <note>Isaiah 9:5</note>. The Name of the Essence is among them to show that their power comes from the Might <note>term: Gevurah (*Might/Power*), one of the ten Sephirot or divine attributes</note>, and the Might itself is in the Kindness <note>term: Chesed (*Kindness/Mercy*), another Sephirah</note>. These are called "Behold," for the Prince of Might is included within the eight; they are in the thought of the Eight that appears before Him. This indicates that they are those who dwell upon a single sanctification, which is the Name of Might that is within them.

<margin>Y-B-M</margin> And its form is <note>numerical cipher</note>: the Name itself indicates that He rides upon ten wheels <note>original: גלגלים (*Galgalim*) — can mean spheres, wheels, or celestial orbs</note>, as it is said: "Extol Him who rides upon the clouds" <note>Psalm 68:5</note>. But its form indicates that the Four-Letter Name <note>term: Tetragrammaton (*Y-H-V-H*), the most sacred name of God</note> was condensed and magnified like the three tens mentioned above in the number of **Ehyeh**, as it is said: "**Ehyeh Asher Ehyeh**" <note>Exodus 3:14: "I AM THAT I AM"</note>. These three instances of **Ehyeh** correspond to: **Ehyeh** for the ten words of the Torah <note>The Ten Commandments</note>, **Ehyeh** for the ten Sephirot <note>The ten divine emanations</note>, and **Ehyeh** for the ten plagues in Egypt. The masters of the Kabbalah mention this in the name of **Ehyeh**, which makes the entire world tremble—the ten of the Torah with the ten utterances <note>by which the world was created</note> and the ten of Egypt. In truth, everything depends on it: "The earth shook and trembled" <note>Psalm 18:8</note>. This is as it is said: "And I heard behind me a voice of a great rushing" <note>Ezekiel 3:12</note>, and he remembered Hezekiah very much. There are many secrets in this, for they are found in the numerical value of the name *A-K-A*, which contains the secret of the Seventh <note>The Sabbath or the Sabbatical year</note> and the Sephirot, along with the seventh millennium, as it is written: "And the Lord alone shall be exalted in that day" <note>Isaiah 2:11</note>. Furthermore, it is written: "This is My name forever, and this is My memorial unto all generations" <note>Exodus 3:15</note>, for with it He sealed the Mights, all the Sanctifications, all the commandments of the Torah, and all the powers that exist within them. As the Sages of the Kabbalah explained: "He was, He is, and He will be." "He was" is the secret sealed in the Ten Commandments; "He is" is sealed in the ten utterances; and "He will be" is sealed in the ten plagues for all eternity. Because of this, the Sages commanded a complete obligation to mention the Exodus from Egypt by day and by night, as it is said: "That you may remember the day when you came forth out of the land of Egypt" <note>Deuteronomy 16:3</note>. They interpreted this as extending to the days of the Messiah and the World to Come. We shall not expand further, for it is a hidden and sealed secret for the masters of Kabbalah, being a hint from the song *Ha'azinu* and from the Vision, etc.

<margin>H-Y-Y</margin> And its form is <note>numerical cipher</note>: it points to three sanctifications, each one of ten, and the fourth is over all.

<margin>M-V-M</margin> And its form is <note>numerical cipher</note>: it points out that He maintains His unity and His greatness in His beauty in truth. For He sealed with two names of the Essence as one, for He is the first and He is the last, and furthermore: "I, even I, am He, and there is no god with Me" <note>Deuteronomy 32:39</note>. Between them is the letter *Vav*, a hint to the six directions <note>original: קצוות (*Ketzot*) — literally "extremities"</note> in which everything is contained: East, West, North, South, Up, and Down. This is a hint to the six wings <note>of the Seraphim</note> that always sanctify Him, which are the six directions of the world, as it is said: "Kings of the earth and all peoples, princes and all judges of the earth..." <note>Psalm 148:11</note> and "Your righteousness, O God, is very high" <note>Psalm 71:19</note> and "Your judgments are a great deep" <note>Psalm 36:7</note>.

The explanation of the composition and form of the counting of the holy Kabbalah is completed—that which was received from the mouth of the **Power** by Moses at Sinai, and handed down to Joshua, and Joshua to the Elders, and the Elders to the Prophets, and the Prophets handed it down to the Men of the Great Assembly. For they were masters of wisdom, masters of Kabbalah, masters of awe, masters of honor, and masters of understanding. Concerning them it is said: "The secret of the Lord is with them that fear Him" <note>Psalm 25:14</note>. They hid it within the secrets of the Talmud, for everything is Kabbalah to Moses from Sinai. These things are not handed over to mockers, nor to the empty-headed, nor to youths. Rather, they are for a man of understanding and wisdom. One who "has not filled his belly with all food" <note>a metaphor for one who has not first mastered the primary layers of Torah and Law</note> should not enter the **Orchard** <note>term: Pardes (*Orchard*) — a famous acronym for the four levels of Torah interpretation, specifically referring here to the deepest mystical level</note>. It is handed over to a man of understanding and wisdom who fears God and shuns evil, so that he may direct his mind and perfect his intellect, opening his eyes and heart in uprightness to look upon the Light of Life and perceive some of the ways of the Name, may He be blessed and exalted, and His awesome and great powers. For "who is like God in heaven or on earth who can do according to Your works and according to Your mighty acts?" <note>Deuteronomy 3:24</note>. Then he will understand a portion of the Supreme Light that shines in the eyes of the wise. It is written: "Light dawns in the darkness for the upright" <note>Psalm 112:4</note>. It mentions the pure and good intellect, and they seat him in the Supreme Light, of which it is said: "For the commandment is a lamp and the Torah is light" <note>Proverbs 6:23</note>. If they take care to set their intellect on the straight path to understand the form and composition of the holy number and the blast <note>original: תרועה (*Teruah*) — usually a shofar blast, here likely a mystical vibration or sound</note>, and the Honorable and Awesome Name is explicitly declared in holiness and purity—happy is he and happy is she who bore him. If he always directs himself for the sake of Heaven, then his light and intellect will reach the level of the light of the angels, for he is a "messenger of the Lord of Hosts." For this form is engraved upon the Throne, for the High Throne consists of the twenty-two letters of the Torah, which are the secret of **Ehyeh**, and they are light for every creature, the heavenly angels, and Israel...

<margin>H-R-Y</margin> This indicates that He is One among the celestial beings. **H-Q-M** indicates the tribes of God <note>original: "שבטי י"ה" (*Shivtei Yah*)</note> engraved upon the stones <note>The twelve stones on the High Priest's breastplate</note> as a remembrance before the Lord.

<margin>L-A-V</margin> This indicates the sanctifications of the realms above. **K-L-Y** indicates that the four doors of the world are open with mercy. **L-V-N** indicates the twelve tribes within whom the Holy Name dwells, and their rank is high above. **P-H-L** indicates that the mercies of the Holy One, Blessed be He, are eternal.

<margin>N-L-K</margin> This indicates how the Name "Lord, Lord" <note>original: "ה' ה'" (*Y-H-V-H Y-H-V-H*)</note> is engraved in the soul that performed these actions. These are seven holy names [used] to make an amulet <term>Kame'a|A protective scroll or charm</term> for youths against any evil spirit, the "evil eye," fear, and crying. It is good to seek mercy before the Name, that He may save me from all fear. One should say them toward the four directions of the world seven times and say: "May it be Your will, O Lord my God and God of my fathers, in the name of these souls in which there is the power of mercy, that You save me from this fear." <margin>Y-V-M</margin> [This corresponds] to the attribute of Abraham, in his day, his hour, and through his angel. <margin>L-Y-V</margin> This indicates three "Sabbaths" of the Lord, corresponding to three instances of the Name **Ehyeh** <note>original: אהי"ה (*Ehyeh*), meaning "I AM"</note>, for the last is the "Hanging One" <note>original: "התליין" (*Ha-Talyan*) — a kabbalistic term often referring to a celestial axis or the constellation Draco</note> in pure language <note>a euphemism for a specific mystical transition</note>, hinting at the final attribute <note>the sefirah of Malchut</note>. It stems from the power of the Eternals <note>original: "נצחי"ם" (*Nitzachim*)</note> and hints at the three sanctifications of the Name; it is all one power, and the power of the world is its celestial study.

<margin>M-L-H</margin> This indicates that the upper and lower [realms] are moved by a single power. **H-H-V** indicates the Name from whose power the four elements [emerge].

<margin>N-T-H</margin> This indicates the Torah. The [letter] *Vav* is the essence, but the [letter] *Resh* is similar to the firmament and is the head of the lower world. However, the [letters] *He* and *Vav* signify "Righteousness shall call him to his feet" <note>Isaiah 41:2</note>, and it is comprised of two attributes, and He is the Righteous One. **H-A-A** indicates that the thirteen attributes [of mercy] are included in a single attribute and a single power... known as the Complete Ones.

<margin>Y-R-T</margin> This indicates that by the hand <note>original: "בי"דו" (*B'Yado*) — a wordplay on the numerical value of the hand (14) and the letters of the Name</note> of the Name, His name stands forever. **Sh-A-H** indicates that the Name goes out from His place to bring judgment upon Egypt and to show His might there. These seven pure names are good for wayfarers to use to adjure the "Place of Fear" <note>dangerous locations</note>. These names should be written on a copper plate; he should mention them and write them in purity and after immersion, in the name of Isaac, through his attribute and power, in his day and hour; Wednesday is his day.

<margin>R-Y-Y</margin> This indicates the twenty-two letters of the Torah from the power of the Name **Ehyeh**, which are above the final Sabbath, and they are the secret of the third Sabbath.

<margin>A-V-M</margin> This also indicates the "Righteous One, the Foundation of the World" <term>Tzadik Yesod Olam|A concept identifying the righteous person as the pillar that sustains existence</term>. **L-K-B** indicates the Name who rides upon the Patriarchs.

<margin>R-Sh-R</margin> This indicates the Rainbow, hinting at three types of colors; they are this attribute, the "Weeks of the Land of Israel," to have mercy upon them as a holy people and to cause His Name to dwell there. **Y-H-V** indicates the power of Y-H-V... hinting at 610. **L-H-CH** indicates the Spirit of the Living God, the spirit of Wisdom, and Understanding that flows into the Supernal Man <note>Adam Elyon</note>, who is a microcosm <term>Olam Katan|The idea that the human being is a miniature version of the universe</term> that performs everything. **K-V-Q** indicates that the Name of 26 <note>the numerical value of the Tetragrammaton Y-H-V-H</note> is a single essence. These seven holy names, for one who mentions them in holiness and purity, are good for learning Torah, wisdom, and craftsmanship. When written on a single silver plate, they are good against forgetting anything one has learned; [they should be written] on Monday, through his attribute, power, and hour, written in holiness and purity.

**M-N-D** indicates when Moses ascended to the heights and all wisdom and understanding were delivered to him, as well as the thirty-two paths of wisdom. It hints at the Supernal Holy Wisdom where the celestial power is opened.

<margin>A-N-Y</margin> This indicates the celestial holiness corresponding to the ten holy palaces of Moses. **CH-A-M** indicates that the twelve tribes are engraved in the Holy Name.

<margin>R-H-E</margin> This indicates that the celestial power corresponds to the lower power... there are seventy-six levels, and the Name of 72 letters is over all. This is the secret of Understanding... a hint to Jacob and his sons. **Y-Y-Y** indicates the attributes of God—the powers within the letter *He*.

<margin>H-H-H</margin> This indicates the power of the three attributes of the Name "EL," and the power of the "faint animal" <note>possibly referring to the soul or a specific angelic rank</note> among them, which is the power of life from God.

<margin>M-Y-K</margin> This indicates that with the three sanctifications, the upper and lower [realms] stand by the power of seventy names. These seven holy names are good for a **Dream Inquiry** <term>She'elat Chalom|A ritual to receive answers to specific questions through divinely inspired dreams</term> to ask about celestial and hidden matters. If one writes them and mentions them along with R-H-S and K-V-Q, and includes the Psalm "A Psalm of David: The Lord is my shepherd" <note>Psalm 23</note>, he will see wonderful and glorious things in great holiness and purity. This should be performed while wearing fine white linens, using pure parchment and ink, on the night of Tuesday or Wednesday. He should mention them seven times, place the writing under his headrest in purity, and say in his request: "May it be Your will, O Lord my God and God of my fathers, and before you, O holy and awesome letters, that you show me tonight regarding such-and-such a matter, whether it be 'yes' or 'no'." From midday onward he should not eat, he should perform a ritual immersion, and then ask his question.

**V-V-L** indicates the foundation of the world and its holiness, the Holy Temple, with the Name upon it, where the tribes of Israel are sealed. **Y-L-H** indicates ten attributes, all of which are mercy, except for the five which are within it and included. **S-A-L** indicates the form of the letters corresponding to the three powers that lead the three worlds through celestial power and kindness... for the celestial beings are all mercy. **E-R-Y** indicates the seventy levels in the seven heavens, but up to the tenth, they are all holy and doubled. **E-Sh-L** indicates that by the righteousness of the Patriarchs the world stands, for they are the holy seal of the earth.

<margin>M-Y-H</margin> This indicates that the form of the Name and the letters were known to none but Moses. **V-H-V** indicates its first foundation, which is the secret of the final *He* in the Name... these seven holy and pure names are good for seeking mercy for any action or any desire one wishes, whether with a king or any person, for grace and love, because their power is very great and strong in mercy. Thus one should say: "May it be Your will, O Great and Mighty Name, that You hear my prayer and my requests, and fulfill my desire and my action regarding such-and-such a matter which I desire... May the words of my mouth and the meditation of my heart be acceptable before You, O Lord, my Rock and my Redeemer." [This should be done] on Friday morning, through his attribute and his angel, in complete purity with prayer and fasting.

**D-N-Y** indicates the root of the Name Y-H-V, which is the secret of the third sanctification. **H-CH-Sh** indicates that the attribute of *Shaddai* <note>Almighty</note> is found in the second and third Patriarchs... it is the eternity of wisdom.

# The Great Book of Raziel

And now, listen to me, my wise son, and I will enlighten you on how to ascend and how to enter into their secret, how to find their power and their operation, and how to open their light. This is what you must do for them: if you grasp their beginning, you will find their end. Therefore, understand deeply, investigate, and inquire into the three verses—their origins and their entrances in the plain sense <note>original: "בפשוט" (*bi-fshat*). This refers to the literal reading of Exodus 14:19-21, where each verse contains exactly 72 Hebrew letters.</note>. Furthermore, investigate them in the hidden mysteries; investigate the words; investigate every letter by itself; and investigate their structured permutations, their combinations, their shapes, and their numerical values <term>Gematria|The kabbalistic method of interpreting words by the numerical value of their letters</term>. Carve them by the name of their holiness and see what each and every one of them indicates. Afterward, you shall investigate their operation. I will explain them to you clearly, provided that you are wholehearted in your heart with the matters written above.

I tell you that you already know what is written: "And the angel of God moved..." and "the pillar of cloud moved..." <note>Exodus 14:19</note> for these are the supernal powers, engraved and awesome, and the camp of the Children of Israel was between them. For they are the power of God <note>original: "כח אל" (*Koach El*)</note> to separate in the midst, and they cover Him. These three verses hint at this: He is entirely an immense Might <term>Gevurah|The divine attribute of strength, judgment, and restraint</term>, and He is the actor. The "East Wind" <note>Exodus 14:21</note> is also the power of Might—the bitter, hard strength that is the most difficult of all supernal powers, for the power of the sea is strong, and this Might is over it. As it is written: "With an east wind You shatter the ships of Tarshish" and "The east wind carried the locusts." Regarding what is written: "And the Lord moved the sea"—it began for him with love and mercy, but [the sea] did not listen immediately. Then He came upon it with a fierce measure all through the night because of the "attribute of night" <note>Associated with judgment</note>. Then it was turned and hidden greatly, and all the kings of the east <note>original: "מלכי חילום" (*malkhei chilom*)</note> knew of it, for they heard its voice.

The reason the third verse was placed between them was to separate two strong attributes <note>The first and second verses represent different aspects of divine power; the third acts as a balance</note>, for it is a softened bitterness. It placed its head above and its end below, for they are engraved powers descending for judgment, and this one ascends to separate between them. This is necessary, for if they were not arranged so, but were instead connected in another way—strength with strength and fire with fire—then they would burn the entire world and destroy the whole world, and the entire world would be ruined in an instant. It would be impossible to work with them except to destroy, ruin, and annihilate, for such is their power. But now, the practitioners of Kabbalah <note>original: "מקובלים" (*Mekubalim*)</note> find in it [the Name] that which is for their benefit: for good and for bad, for judgment and for mercy, for love and for hate, for peace and for war, and also for healing and for distancing a bad and difficult spirit, according to the power of their shape, the weight of their numerical value, and the translation found within them.

And now, my wise and faithful son, I adjure you by the Lord, God of Heaven, that you do not come to gaze upon these matters at just any time or any moment, lest you find yourself guilty. I have already warned you at the beginning of my words: only at known times when the knowledge of your heart is good and upright, and your mind is well-settled. I have sworn to you in truth that when it seems to you that you are close to them, you are actually very far, until you have looked into them many times very well and seen the explanation of every name and every power according to how it is spread out in calculations, in shapes, and in their explanation. 

Understand what was said: that "Y-H" <note>The two-letter name of God</note> is the "Hand of Y-H" <note>original: "יד י"ה" (*Yad Yah*). The word "Yad" (Hand) has the numerical value of 14, and the letter Yod is shaped like a hand.</note> in its drawing, and it is literally the power of the Blessed One. However, it contains three attributes and also three letters corresponding to the Supernal Crown (*Keter*), Wisdom (*Chokhmah*), and Understanding (*Binah*). But in truth, the three attributes hint at the true Patriarchs in every single power. For the power of water is found in it, as in "Is the Lord's hand shortened?" <note>Numbers 11:23</note>—behold, mercy; and also "the hand of the Lord was against them"—behold, judgment. The practitioners of Kabbalah use it to turn it toward whichever power they wish to operate with. This is also corresponding to the lower realms, and it is the power of the Lord, for all is one power and one name. Also, in its drawing, the "Hand of Y-H" is "H-V" <note>The letters Heh and Vav</note> corresponding to the "Pillar of the World," which is the Temple below. "And He was" <note>original: "והיה" (*ve-hayah*)</note> is forever the Earth, as it is written: "The whole earth is full of His glory" in the Temple, and from the Temple to all the earth. Also, the seventy holy [names] that emerge from the power of this Name—each one according to its might and its power.

## The First Operation

<margin>V-H-V</margin> This indicates that the abundance <term>Shefa|Divine flow or energy</term> descends to the Temple, and from there it descends to the world. **Y-L-Y** indicates that the Name is ancient above all ancients.

<margin>S-Y-T</margin> This indicates that He was, and He is living and enduring. **A-L-M** indicates that He showed His might in these three verses—the wonders that were performed with them in Egypt and at the Sea. **M-H-Sh** indicates the endurance of the three Patriarchs; they are the three attributes of the Name in the power of Victory <term>Netzach|The sefira of endurance and triumph</term>, for through them, they are victorious.

<margin>L-L-H</margin> This indicates the creation of the world, mercy, and also a delayed judgment. **A-K-A** This indicates the World to Come, atonement, forgiveness, and rest for the soul; it also indicates the resurrection of the dead from its form in mercy. These are the seven holy names that, if one whispers them or writes them upon a plate of silver for a sick person who is leaning toward death—and let one who is expert in them write them—it is because their power is to conquer evil, to silence every evil spirit and difficult bitterness, and to strengthen them at the time of the twilights <note>original: "שמשות" (*shmashot*)</note>. One who writes it on the first day [Sunday] at the first hour should mention the class of the "Coming One"—meaning that angel over that hour—in purity, with fasting, and with a pure ritual immersion, and he shall be pure from sexual relations.

**K-H-T** This indicates that the Supernal Wisdom is the Torah, and it is the covenant of our God in heaven; for this reason, the word was not mentioned. **H-Z-Y** This indicates that wisdom is the absolute Unity, for He is God who is exalted above all who are exalted. **A-L-D** This indicates that He is elevated upon the Chariot of the Seraphim <term>Seraphim|High-ranking angels associated with fire and purification</term> of above and below. **L-A-V** This indicates that when they saw His wonders in Egypt and the staff which He used in heaven and earth, then dominion and fear were with Him, and the whole world feared Him. **H-H-E** This indicates that Moses sold <note>This likely means "passed on" or "designated"</note> to Aaron a Name that would come into the Temple to mention the powers of the Holy One, Blessed be He, which are engraved upon the Breastplate—and it is the Name of 72 letters. **Y-Z-L** This indicates that the Holy One, Blessed be He, elevated His might and sat as King upon His Throne of Glory to judge all who come into the world, and also to judge the stars which are in the firmaments; He did not leave them to whoever was performing all the acts of judgment in Egypt. **M-B-H** This indicates that the power of the Holy One, Blessed be He, is the power in Himself and in His Glory, which the Holy One, Blessed be He, sent to Moses to operate with the name "Y-H" by the word of the Lord. 

These are seven pure names for remembering wisdom: one who writes them upon a plate of silver and drinks them with "living water" <note>Spring water</note> from a spring on the first of the month of Nisan, or hangs them upon himself. They are good for protecting a person from every evil spirit, the [evil] eye, and from every damaging thing, when hung upon them on deer parchment in holiness and purity, after the manner of Aaron. One should focus on the proper hour; the first is the attribute of Moses.

<meta>This page continues the discussion on the mystical 72-letter Name of God and the three verses of Exodus 14. It transitions from the theoretical structure of these names to the inherent dangers of their study—likening them to a consuming fire—and begins a detailed analysis of the mystical properties of the Hebrew alphabet and the role of the angel Metatron.</meta>

# The Great Book of Raziel

And by the power [of these names], those who study them will not be burned, for they are fire and pure light, burning and consuming. One who is initiated into their combinations and their forms <note>original: "בצירופם ובציורם" (*be-tzirufam u-ve-tziyuram*) refers to the kabbalistic practice of permuting letters and visualizing their shapes.</note> can transform every power within himself and for his own strength, compelling it to perform its function. Then, he shall call upon that angel of the Living God and the Supernal Power, for from those powers, influence flows to these below; power acts and connects to power according to their movement, their intent, and their secret purpose. Know for a certainty that the proper divine flow <term>Shefa|The divine emanation or "flow" of energy from the spiritual realms to the physical world</term> does not descend except through great strength, great purity, immense holiness, and subtle, upright contemplation. If you are "living" <note>An idiomatic way of saying "if you are spiritually awake."</note>, you will understand my words: for in the land below, seers and prophets are not found except for the need of the hour, because the divine flow does not descend outside the Land [of Israel]. This is the secret of what the Sages, of blessed memory, said: "Whoever dwells outside the Land [of Israel] is as if he has no God." <note>A famous Talmudic statement (Ketubot 110b) emphasizing the unique spiritual status of the Land of Israel.</note> If you cut them off at their beginning, or if your [spiritual] dryness increases, then they will burn you and you will perish. Regarding this, the Sages said that it is a great prohibition to force the supernal powers without an extremely great necessity.

This clarifies for you the Throne: after Wisdom and Understanding <term>Chokhmah and Binah|The second and third emanations (Sefirot) of the divine tree, representing the seeds of thought and their developmental reasoning</term>, there is the Spirit of the Living God. These are the inner powers before Him—mighty and awesome. A *Seraph* <note>original: "שרף" (*Seraph*), literally "burning one," a high-ranking angel of fire.</note> is on this side and a *Seraph* is on that side, and the Chariot <term>Merkavah|The "Chariot" or divine assembly seen in the visions of Ezekiel</term> of the Patriarchs is between them. As for what they say—that the Patriarchs are higher than the Seraphim—this is their intended meaning. Nevertheless, both are wondrous and mighty powers. But the verse testifies to this: "Seraphim stood above Him" <note>Isaiah 6:2</note>—above all supernal powers. They are a portion of fire surrounding them, running and flying with all their might from the day of their "youth," day and night, seasons and times, in their race to reach the glory of their Creator, as it is written: "Then one of the Seraphim flew to me." They call to one another and ask of one another: "Where is His holiness? Where is His glory?" They call out the *Kadish* <note>The sanctification prayer.</note> and say "Holy," and others respond. This is the tablet of "Holy is the Lord of Hosts." From the great strength of their running and their holiness, they bow with a loud voice and sanctify in dread and fear, saying: "Blessed be the glory of the Lord from His place." All the supernal beings tremble and fear, saying: "Blessed be the name of His glorious kingdom forever and ever." The Chariot and the other Seraphim above, along with the other supernal powers, angels, and the souls of the righteous together with Israel below—all of them together in dread and awe respond and say: "Holy, Holy, Holy is the Lord of Hosts." At that hour, the Lord is exalted, magnified, and uplifted upon His throne of kingship; He sits upon the Throne of Grace and Mercy and He also says: "You are My servant, Israel, in whom I will be glorified." Happy is the man who visits [the house of God] every day to praise Him, to glorify Him, and to revere Him in dread and fear, sanctifying Him and saying his prayers early; for "He who trusts in the Lord, mercy shall encompass him." 

All these powers are distinct from one another according to their strength and stature. Each is inscribed in His Palace—meaning, in the place appropriate for its operation. Some are to the East and some to the West, some to the North and some to the South; some for Mercy and some for Judgment, some for Good and some for Evil; some to grant kindness and some to punish transgression; some for Charity and some for Justice; some for Charity and some for the Outcry; some for Peace and some for War; some to kill and destroy and some for peace and to save from evil; some for hatred and some for love. Everything is according to the will of their Creator and King, who prepared them and established them, each man to his service and his burden. They are all living, awesome powers, and they bow to Him every day and say their prayers early. Happy is the man who fears God and understands these great, awesome things, and who sets his heart to this, casting off "filthy garments" <note>A metaphor for sins or worldly attachments.</note> and putting on "garments of salvation" and "white garments."

->For they are your life and the length of your days.<-

**And also the Rectification** <term>Tikkun|The process of repair or spiritual alignment</term> of the letters is as follows: The *Aleph* and the *Tav*, and the *Tav* and the *Shechem* <note>Likely a reference to a specific permutation or a misspelling in the transcription.</note> in another way, and the Name is over them, for they are powers within great and awesome powers. When the Name is decreed with them, He seals them for Mercy or for Judgment. When they are joined, they are called *Emet* <term>Emet|Truth (אמת), composed of the first, middle, and last letters of the Hebrew alphabet.</term>, for as the Sages said: "Truth is the seal of the Holy One, Blessed be He," because from before Him comes Judgment and is transformed into Mercy. All its letters are placed in one form and stand until "One" <note>The letter Aleph.</note> on the Throne, indicating that they are not like those below. For the *Aleph* (א) has fifty letters; the *Bet* (ב), the *Gimmel* (ג), the *Dalet* (ד)—each lies in its own way and has no connection [to the others], for it is a holy letter of the Name *Shaddai* <term>Shaddai|A divine name often translated as "Almighty."</term> in two forms, for they are not joined because they are holy. The *Vav* (ו) are the pillars of holiness. The *Chet* (ח) and the *Tet* (ט) are joined thus, and here is their form, for after them comes the calculation [of their values], and from it the Evil Abyss was created, for from it darkness went out into the world, and there the wicked of the nations of the world are judged. The *Yod* (י) is a point that is entirely holy within, and the Lord alone is exalted. Likewise, the other letters are in holiness: *Lamed* (ל), *Khaf* (כ), *Vav* (ו), *Nun* (נ) are holy; *Samekh* (ס), *Khaf* (כ), *Vav* (ו), *Ayin* (ע)... <note>The text continues listing the letters and their mystical associations.</note> *Shin* (ש) represents the Patriarchs; *Tav* (ת), *Resh* (ר), *Vav* (ו); for everything is directed toward that which is above. The four Patriarchs of the Chariot correspond to the four directions of the world, for they are the pillars upon which the world stands in its righteousness.

The Palace of Metatron <term>Metatron|A high-ranking angel in Jewish mysticism, often described as the celestial scribe or the "Prince of the Presence."</term> is the Pure Intellect from which influence flows to the soul of the perfect man—the pure and clean intellect. The Palace of the Separate Intellects <term>Sekhalim Ha-Nivdalim|A term from medieval philosophy referring to purely spiritual beings (angels) that are "separate" from matter.</term> consists of the intellect of the angels, the intellect of the righteous, and the intellect of the spirits and souls; it is the Upper World. But the World to Come is the Palace of Jacob, the Palace of Metatron, for there come the souls of the righteous which are above the lowly world. Metatron is the Active Intellect <term>Sekhel Ha-Po'el|In Aristotelian and Maimonidean philosophy, the "Active Intellect" is the bridge between the divine mind and human reason.</term> and he is the power of *Shaddai*, and it is all one power. He is called Metatron because he comes "from below" <note>A folk-etymology linking the name to the Hebrew "mitahat" (underneath).</note>, for he is pure and clean and separated from matter, and he comes to that place which is his source, to the place from which he was hewn—which is the Pure Soul. It is the world of "50 Gates" <note>Likely referring to the 50 Gates of Understanding (*Binah*).</note> of the upper realm, pure and upright, and all is the power of the Most High God. Also, the letters are great and mighty powers beyond investigation, and from them other powers receive. See that all is One... it is the head of all supernal powers. 

The forms of the *Aleph* were joined when Moses went up to receive the Torah. They [the angels] knew that Moses wanted to bring them down from their high and holy level, and the multitudes [of angels] prepared to burn him. But God commanded them to descend to the world of matter, and He hinted to them regarding "My servant Moses," for the souls of the righteous are also taken from the supernal and holy realms to be within the body of man. Because of this, they have a higher standing than the rest of the supernal powers. He hinted that "The Lord is high, yet He regards the lowly" and "Extol Him who rides upon the clouds," and "Father of orphans and judge of widows." You must understand this: it means the powers of the Name, which are higher than all, are yet "lowly" to all <note>Meaning they extend down to the humblest levels of existence.</note>. Regarding this, they said that the souls of the righteous are higher than the angels. When they heard the word of the Lord from His rebuke, they all trembled, and immediately every letter was joined to its form and its companion; they came to Moses and the engravings descended with him on the Tablets. There, they taught him the secret of the Holy Names and the *Sefirot*, which are the twenty-two letters of holiness from which the entire Torah was joined, for from their light is the light of the upper and lower worlds, and the 70 Names...

<meta>This page continues the exposition of the 72-letter Name of God, detailing the specific meanings and powers associated with various three-letter combinations. It transitions from a list of these mystical attributes to a solemn instructional warning addressed to the student regarding the purity required to handle such holy mysteries. # The Great Book of Raziel

<margin>Amas</margin> <note>original: עמס (*Amas*)</note> indicates seventy powers under the Name, and among them is the power over the seven wandering stars <note>The five visible planets plus the Sun and Moon.</note> that move within the celestial arrangement. **Nana** <note>original: ננא (*Nana*)</note> indicates that the Supernal powers praise the Name every day with one hundred types of praises and blessings; corresponding to these, Israel established one hundred blessings to be said every day. You should pray them with focused intent <note>original: נק"א (*NKA*), likely an abbreviation for *Nikiut, Kedusha, Ometz*—purity, holiness, and strength.</note>, for this name is also from the Supernal realms. **Nait** <note>original: נית (*Nait*)</note> indicates that this pillar is the "Righteous One," the foundation of the world <term>Tzaddik Yesod Olam|A kabbalistic concept where the righteous individual serves as the metaphysical pillar supporting all of existence</term> upon which the world stands. This is the attribute of Truth, for it is the Supernal Light, the seal of the Holy One, blessed be He. **Mebah** <note>original: מבה (*Mebah*)</note> indicates that the spirit of God is the pure intellect which He commanded to dwell within man, who is a "small world" <note>The concept of the microcosm: man as a reflection of the entire universe.</note>. **Poi** <note>original: פוי (*Poi*)</note> indicates that the ten plagues of Egypt are weighed equally against the Ten Commandments <note>original: עשר הדברים (*Eser HaDevarim*) literally "the Ten Words."</note>. These ten names are pure, clear, and strong; one may seek any request from them through the way of prayer. Let him say them over pure, living water from a flowing spring three times, then wash his face and pour the remainder over his head, saying: "May it be Your will, through these Your holy and great names, that I may learn every matter of Torah and wisdom." **AAA MMM** <note>original: אאא םםם</note>. This should be done every Thursday morning from the New Moon of the month of Nisan until the middle of the month. **Nemam** <note>original: נמם (*Nemam*)</note> indicates that the Fiftieth Gate is the power of God, hidden from every living creature that dwells in the sanctuary below. When the Holy Jubilee <note>A cycle of 50 years; in Kabbalah, it represents the Sephirah of *Binah* (Understanding).</note> was nullified, the power of the letter *He* (ה) was also diminished, for during the time of holiness, it was truly holy.

<margin>Yal</margin> <note>original: ייל (*Yal*)</note> indicates that this power was exalted above all that is Supernal. **Harat** <note>original: הרת (*Harat*)</note> indicates the Holy Name, which is uniquely sublime in the letter *Aleph* (א), and who can turn Him back? 
<margin>Metzar</margin> <note>original: מצר (*Metzar*)</note> indicates that these two letters <note>original: ב"ב (*B-B*)</note> are the Name *Adonai* <note>The Lord</note> corresponding to one another, and it is a single power. **Umab** <note>original: ומב (*Umab*)</note> indicates that the Name gives strength in a time of need for comfort, day and night.
<margin>From the teacher to the West:</margin> **Yaha** <note>original: יהה (*Yaha*)</note> indicates the secret of the letters *He-He* (הה) which take hold of the entire world with justice and mercy. **Anu** <note>original: ענו (*Anu*)</note> indicates that He appears at times with the attribute of Mercy during the "shattering of languages" <note>A reference to the Tower of Babel</note> in order to bring them back in repentance; for there are seventy nations corresponding to the seventy powers above. These ten holy names are good for seeking prayer, forgiveness, and atonement for all one's sins. One should confess in awe, fear, and purity if he knows he has committed a great sin or transgression, or if he is in great danger from the government or any great man. Let him confess all his sins with this Name and say: "May it be Your will, Great Name, for You are the Master of forgiveness and atonement, that You forgive me for all my sins and save me from this wrath." This is good for every trouble. One should pray it with perfect intention and great purity on a Thursday, in the "measure of Moses" <note>A specific time or spiritual state associated with the prophet Moses.</note> at his hour. **Mechi** <note>original: מחי (*Mechi*)</note> indicates that they sanctify Him above and Israel sanctifies Him below, so that they may be joined in one power and become a single Tabernacle. **Damab** <note>original: דמב (*Damab*)</note> indicates that just as He illuminates seven powers above, so too seven powers below shine before the Name in their righteousness. Thus is every righteous person.
<margin>Menak</margin> <note>original: מנק (*Menak*)</note> indicates that within these seventy armies, the powers surrounding the Throne are always on the Name's mission, and from them, He bestows the divine influence <note>original: משפיע (*mashpia*)</note> through the three levels of Godly power.
<margin>Aya</margin> <note>original: איע (*Aya*)</note> indicates that with this Name, [Moses] pleaded to enter the Land, but he was not answered. **Chavu** <note>original: חבו (*Chavu*)</note> indicates His unity. **Ree** <note>original: ראה (*Ree*)</note> indicates that here the prophet hinted at the powers of God, may He be blessed, revealed from the ten Sephirot; for they are four hundred in Thought, and the "power of the garden" <note>original: חג' (*Chag*)—potentially an abbreviation for the first three Sephirot: *Chesed, Gevurah, Tiferet*.</note> is from them. They are ten, yet they are eight in Thought; he called it "child" because of the flow of influence, and also because of the power, for power is "man," and man is the Supernal. **Yibam** <note>original: יבם (*Yibam*)</note> indicates that it hints at the final attribute <note>The Sephirah of *Malchut* (Kingship).</note> which is inclusive of everything. **Heyi** <note>original: היי (*Heyi*)</note> indicates that these three "tens" are three Sabbaths, and they sustain His great Name; for through them the Torah was given and the world was created. With three of them, the might of the Lord was sealed, and He brought them forth with awesome seals, with great wonders and laws in the upper and lower worlds, as it is written: "And they gave wonders in the heavens and the earth"—great and faithful plagues which are remembered for all eternity to all coming generations. This is what is written: "Thus shall you say to the children of Israel, *Ehyeh* <note>I Am / I Will Be</note> has sent me." This is My name forever, for all who come into this world and for all eternity; this is My memorial from generation to generation, to resurrect the dead. And "generation to generation" refers to the six thousand years of all the days of your life forever, until the "second generation" <note>A reference to the world to come or the messianic age.</note>. This is what is written: "The Rock, His work is perfect," the Name who is living and strong forever and ever. **Mum** <note>original: מום (*Mum*)</note> indicates that the beginning of the Name's work is with mercy and the end is with mercy. Therefore, all His creatures praise Him, magnify Him, recognize, and know that He is the God who feeds and sustains all; and everyone praises Him, including the kings of the earth, all nations, princes, and all judges of the earth.

**And now**, my wise son, behold I have explained to you the great, awesome, and powerful things, as well as precious, glorious, clear, and written operations. Do not look upon them constantly, and do not enter the Holy place at all times until you have written and donned "holy purity," just as you, the High Priest, would. The people shall not ascend with you when you come to see the face of the Lord your God in beauty and awe, with a pure heart and perfect intention, with clear and glorious thought, to bring to light the "Great Vision"—the honored and awesome Name, its powers, its wonders, their connections, their permutations, and their reversals, their forms, and the number of their holiness. This is appropriate only for one who is complete in all things, for these are the "seven words of the Living God," and not for others who are hungry and thirsty to drink living and awesome waters. Then he shall understand the secret of the form of the Alphabet. However, the letter *He* (ה) is engraved upon the High Throne, and all its hosts bow to it, for there are three Palaces <term>Hekhalot|Celestial halls or palaces that a mystic must traverse during spiritual ascent</term> where laws are set. They are all like awesome living creatures, diverse from one another, changing in their powers, higher than one another, more awesome than one another, more wondrous than one another, stronger than one another, purer than one another, clearer than one another, cleaner than one another, straighter than one another, more innocent than one another, more perfect than one another, more radiant than one another—sephirot upon sephirot, burning ones, living ones, inner ones, holy ones, each different from the other. All of them are a flaming fire, standing in dread and awe, and with fear they sanctify and revere the God who created them and who placed each one in its dominion to do the will of its Creator. The letters are engraved in pairs in these three Palaces, and the form of each letter is unique. Truly, these are called the Supernal Powers. I shall reveal to you a hidden, deep, and awesome secret; and you, my son, be upright and faithful to me. Let your intention be set. Know that these are truly the powers of the Name, called Supernal Powers, which are engraved and sealed upon the Throne, each letter by itself, to form with its power. They are the "Holy and Beautiful" ones, angels of the living and awesome God, and holy, supernal signs. Then they were joined together, for Moses joined them so that they might emerge from their connection, their form, and their power.

# The Great Book of Raziel

And from their power, [care must be taken] so that those who study them are not burned, for they are a pure, licking, and consuming fire. One who is initiated in their combinations and their forms <note>This refers to the Kabbalistic practice of *Gematria* (number values) and *Tzerufim* (letter permutations).</note> can exchange each power for its own sake and its strength, and prove it for its specific action. Then, he shall call it "an angel of the living God" and a "supernal power," for from those [higher] powers, influence flows to those below. Power shall act and join with power according to their movement, their strength, and their secret purpose.

Know for yourself that the proper abundance <term>Shefa|The divine flow or energy that sustains the universe</term> does not descend except through great strength, great purity, much holiness, and through fine and upright contemplation. If you are "living" <note>Meaning spiritually awake or initiated.</note>, you will understand my words: for in an impure land, there are no seers or prophets found, except for the need of the hour, because the abundance does not descend outside of the Land [of Israel]. This is the secret of what our Sages, of blessed memory, said: "Anyone who dwells outside the Land of Israel is like one who has no God" <note>A famous quote from the Babylonian Talmud, Ketubot 110b, emphasizing the spiritual primacy of the Land of Israel in Jewish thought.</note>. 

And if you should cut them off <note>Meaning to use these powers inappropriately or to separate the divine names from their source.</note> through your wisdom, or if many [forces] are tested within you, then they will burn you and destroy you. Regarding this, our Sages, of blessed memory, said that it is a great prohibition to cut off the supernal powers without an exceedingly great necessity. 

This explains to you the Throne: Crown, Wisdom, and Understanding <term>Keter, Chokmah, Binah|The three highest emanations of God in the Kabbalistic Tree of Life</term>. This is the spirit of the living God. These are the innermost and most awesome powers. A Seraph <note>A high-ranking type of angel, literally meaning "burning one."</note> is here, and a Seraph is there, and the "Chariots of the Patriarchs" are between them. As for the saying that the Patriarchs <note>Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob, often associated with the Chariot (Merkavah) in mystical literature.</note> are higher than the Seraphim—that is their will. Nevertheless, both these and those are wondrous and mighty powers. 

However, the verse testifies to this: "Seraphim stood above Him" <note>Isaiah 6:2</note>, above all the supernal powers. They are a portion of fire surrounding them, running and flying with all their might from the day of their birth, day and night. They stand in their existence to present the glory of their Creator, as it is written: "Then one of the Seraphim flew to me." They call to one another and ask of one another: "Where is His holiness? Where is His glory?" They call out "Holy" and say "Holy," and the matter of "one to another" is: "Holy is the Lord of Hosts" <note>Isaiah 6:3</note>. 

From the greatness of the power of their running and their holiness, they praise and sanctify with a loud voice, in awe and fear, saying: "Blessed be the glory of the Lord from His place." All the supernal beings tremble and fear, saying: "Blessed be the name of His glorious kingdom forever and ever." The Chariot, the other supernal Seraphim, the other higher classes, the angels, and the souls of the righteous together with Israel below—all of them together, in awe and fear, answer and say: "Holy, Holy, Holy is the Lord of Hosts!" 

At that hour, the Lord is exalted, magnified, and uplifted. He is magnified, sanctified, and uplifted upon His throne of kingdom. He sits upon a throne of kindness and mercy and He, too, says to me: "Israel, My servant, in whom I will be glorified." 

Happy is the person who visits every day to praise Him, to glorify Him, and to revere Him in awe and fear, and sanctifies Him, saying: "Blessed be the name of His glorious kingdom forever and ever." For one who trusts in the Lord, kindness shall surround him. 

All these powers are distinct from one another according to the action of each and every power in its "length"—meaning, in the place appropriate for its action. Some are for the East, some for the West, some for the North, and some for the South. Some are for mercy, some for judgment, some for good, some for evil, some to reward kindness, some to punish transgression, some for charity, some for justice, some for outcry, some for peace, some for war, some to kill and destroy, and some for peace and to save from evil, some for hatred and some for love. Everything is according to the will of their Creator and their King, who prepared them and established them, each man over his service and over his burden. They are all awesome living powers, and they prostrate themselves to Him every day and say: "Blessed be the name of His glorious kingdom forever and ever." 

Happy is the man who fears God and understands these great, awesome things. Let him also take this to heart: let him remove the filthy garments from himself and clothe himself in garments of salvation and in white garments.

->For the Lord has been gracious, and may your days be long.<-

***

<margin>And also the arrangement</margin> of the letters is thus: the *Alef* (א), the *Mem* (מ), and the *Tav* (ת) <note>These three letters spell *Emet*, the Hebrew word for "Truth."</note>. They are in another way, and the Name is upon them, for they are powers within great and awesome powers. When the Name is decreed with them, they are for mercy or for judgment. When you join them, they are called "Truth" (*Emet*). This is what our Sages, of blessed memory, said: "Truth is the seal of the Holy One, blessed be He," for from before Him, judgment goes forth and is turned into mercy. 

All His letters are given in one form, and they stand as one in the Throne of the Chariot. They are not like that below. For the *Alef* has three letters, the *Bet* three or four, which are written in one way and have no join, for it is a holy letter of the Name *Shaddai* <note>The divine name meaning "Almighty."</note> in three forms, for they are not joined because they are holy. The ten pillars of holiness are the *Chet* (ח), *Tet* (ט), *Kaf* (כ). His glory and His light are His form. *Kaf* (כ) follows it in the count, and from it the evil abyss was created, for from it darkness went out to the world, and there the wicked of the nations of the world are judged. 

The *Yod* (י) is a point that is entirely holy in its construction, "and the Lord alone shall be exalted." Likewise, the other letters are in holiness: *Lamed* (ל), *Mem* (מ), *Nun* (נ) are holy. *Samekh* (ס), *Ayin* (ע), *Tzadi* (צ), *Pe* (פ)... *Resh* (ר) is holy. *Shin* (ש) represents the Patriarchs. *Tav* (ת)... 

For everything is directed toward what is above. The four "Patriarchs" of the Chariot correspond to the four directions of the world, for they are the pillars upon which the world stands in its righteousness. The Palace of Metatron <note>Metatron is the highest of angels in many mystical traditions, often described as the "Prince of the Countenance" and a celestial scribe.</note> is the Pure Intellect, from which soul is emanated to the soul of the "Perfect Man"—the pure and clean intellect. The Palace of the Separate Intellects <term>Sekhalim HaNivdalim|A philosophical/mystical term for angels as purely spiritual, non-physical minds</term> consists of the intellect of the angels, the intellect of the righteous, and the intellect of the spirits and souls. That is the Supernal World. 

However, the "World to Come" is the Palace of Jacob, the Palace of Metatron. For there come the souls of the righteous from the lowly world. Metatron is the "Active Intellect" <note>In medieval philosophy, the *Intellectus Agens* that bridges human thought and divine truth.</note> and he is the power of *Shaddai*. It is all one power. He is called Metatron because he comes from below <note>A traditional etymology suggesting he was once the human Enoch before ascending.</note>, for he is refined, pure, and separated from matter. He comes to the place which is his home, to the place of his quarry—the pure soul. This is the world of the Supernal Garden of Eden, refined and upright. 

Everything is the power of the Most High God. The letters themselves are great and mighty powers beyond investigation, and from them the other powers receive. See that everything is one. The *Yod* (י) and the *Vav* (ו) are the head of all the supernal powers. They joined into the form of the *Alef* (א) at the time Moses went up to receive the Torah. [The angels] were afraid, for Moses wanted to bring them down from their level and their holiness. They were greatly agitated and wanted to burn him. But God commanded them to descend to the world of matter, and He hinted to them regarding Moses—for the souls of the righteous are also taken from the supernal beings. 

There is a holy time for them to be within the body of man, and because of this, they have a higher status than the other supernal powers. He hinted that "the Lord is high, yet He sees the lowly," and "Extol Him who rides upon the clouds," and "Father of orphans and judge of widows." 

You, understand for yourself: through the powers of the Name, which are higher than all and lower than all. Because of this, they said that the souls of the righteous are higher than the angels. When they heard the word of God from His rebuke, they all trembled. Immediately, every letter joined to its form and its companion, and they came to Moses and descended with him, engraved upon the Tablets. There, they taught him the secret of the sanctities and the *Sefirot*, which are the twenty-two holy letters from which the entire Torah is joined. For from their light comes the light of the upper and lower worlds, and seventy names.

# The Great Book of Raziel

<margin>AMS <note>Original: עמס</note></margin> teaches seventy powers under "The Name" and the vision of the power belonging to the wandering stars <note>The planets</note> that move five to a formation. **NNA** <note>Original: ננא</note> teaches that the supernal powers praise The Name every day with one hundred types of praises and blessings; and when the order of Israel enters into one hundred blessings every day and prays them, they too are called "supernal ones." **NYT** <note>Original: נית</note> teaches that this pillar is the "Righteous One, the foundation of the world" <term>Tzadik Yesod Olam|A concept that a perfectly righteous person sustains the existence of the universe</term> upon whom the world stands, and it is the attribute of Truth, for it is the supernal light, the seal of the Holy One, Blessed be He. **MBH** <note>Original: מבה</note> teaches that the Spirit of God is the pure intellect; He commanded it to be joined with the law of Man, who is a "microcosm" <note>Literally "small world," reflecting the idea that the human body and soul mirror the structure of the entire universe.</note>. **PWY** <note>Original: פוי</note> teaches that the ten plagues of Egypt are weighed against ten [divine] things; and these seven names are pure, clear, and strong for requesting any matter of petition through prayer. One should recite them over pure, "living" water from a flowing spring three times, wash his face, and cast the remainder over his head, saying: "May it be Your will before Your holy and great names that You teach me every matter of Torah and wisdom." **HAA** <note>Original: האא</note> teaches that one should perform this every day at five in the morning, from the beginning of the month of Nisan until the middle of the month. **NNM** <note>Original: ננם</note> teaches that the Fiftieth Gate <note>The "50 Gates of Understanding" (*Binah*) are a central Kabbalistic concept</note> is the power of God, the mysteries hidden from every creature that used to dwell in the Temple below; but when the Holy Palace was nullified, that power was also nullified, for during the time of the Temple, it was "holiness within holiness."

<margin>YLL <note>Original: ייל</note></margin> teaches that this power was elevated above all the supernal heights. **HRY** <note>Original: הרי</note> teaches that the holy name is exalted in its creation through the letter *Aleph*, and "who can turn Him back?"
<margin>MTZR <note>Original: מצר</margin> teaches that this name "Adonai" <note>Lord</note> is paired against that [other name], and they are one power. **LLH** <note>Original: ללה</note> teaches that The Name gives power in the moment of leading them day and night from the east to the west. **YHH** <note>Original: יהח</note> teaches the "Secret of the Lord" <note>A reference to Psalm 25:14</note>, Who grasps the whole world in justice and mercy. **ANW** <note>Original: ענו</note> teaches that He appears at times in the attribute of mercy within physical forms <note>Or "tongues"</note> in order to bring them to repentance, for they are seventy corresponding to the seventy [powers] above. And these seven holy names are good for seeking prayer, forgiveness, and atonement for all one's iniquities. One should confess in dread, in awe, and in purity—if he knows he has sinned or committed a great transgression and is in great danger from the government or from any great man—he should confess all his iniquities with this name and say: "May it be Your will before You, Great Name, for You are the Master of forgiveness and atonement, that You forgive me for all my iniquities and save me from this wrath." It is good for every trouble, and if he prays it with complete intention and great atonement on the fifth day, it invokes the "Attribute of Moses" in its proper hour. **MHY** <note>Original: מחי</note> teaches that they sanctify Him above, and likewise Israel below, so that they may become one power with them and be a single Tabernacle. **DMB** <note>Original: דמב</note> teaches that just as seven powers shine above, so too seven shine below before The Name in their righteousness, and thus it is for every righteous person.

<margin>MNQ <note>Original: מנק</note></margin> teaches that among these seventy hosts, these powers surround the Throne; they are always on missions for The Name, and from them, He pours the divine power into the seventy [nations/languages].
<margin>AYE <note>Original: איע</note></margin> teaches that with this name, they were destined to come and enter the Land, but he was not heard <note>Possible reference to Moses' prayer to enter the land of Israel</note>. **HBW** <note>Original: חבו</note> teaches of His unity. **RAH** <note>Original: ראה</note> teaches that here the prophet hinted at the powers of God, may He be blessed; he revealed from the Ten Sefirot <term>Sefirot|The ten creative attributes or emanations through which God interacts with the universe</term> that they are fifty in Thought <note>The realm of *Machshava*</note>. From them comes the "strength" of *Chag* <note>Abbreviation for *Chesed, Gevurah* (Kindness, Severity)</note>, and they are ten, which are eight in Thought; he called it a "child" because of the abundance <term>Shefa|Divine flow</term>, and likewise because of the power, for the "son" is a "man," and "man" is supernal. **YBM** <note>Original: יבם</note> teaches that it hints at the final attribute <note>Usually *Malchut* or Kingship</note> which is inclusive of everything. **HYY** <note>Original: היי</note> teaches that these fifty actions are their "Sabbaths," and they are the sustenance of the Great Name, for through them the Torah was given and the world was created. In three of them, the "Mighty Deeds of the Lord" were sealed, and He brought forth His awesome powers through great and strong wonders in the upper and lower realms, as it is written: "And they shall give wonders in the heavens and the earth"—great and faithful plagues that are remembered forever until all coming generations. And this is what is written: "Thus shall you say to the children of Israel, 'I AM <note>Ehyeh</note> has sent me'"—this is My name forever for all who enter this world, and for all eternity; and He shall be remembered from generation to generation to resurrect the dead. And a "generation" <note>Dor</note> is six thousand [years], all the days of your life forever, until a "generation" of eight years <note>Possibly a reference to a specific cosmic cycle</note>. And that is what is written: "The Rock, His work is perfect"—The Name who is living and strong for all eternity.

<margin>MWM <note>Original: מום</note></margin> teaches that the beginning of God's action is with mercy, and the end is by the hand of mercy. Because of this, all His creatures praise Him and magnify Him, recognizing and knowing that He is the God who feeds and sustains everything; and everyone praises Him, including the kings of the earth and all nations, princes, and all judges of the earth.
And now, my wise son, behold, I have explained to you the great, awesome, and strong things, and also the precious, glorious, and clear operations. They are written here: do not gaze upon them constantly, and do not come at all times into the Holy Place until you have put on the holy linen garments <note>A metaphor for spiritual preparation and purity, referencing the High Priest's garments</note>. You, the "High Priest," and the people shall not go up with you when you come to appear before the face of the Lord your God in dread and in awe, with a pure heart and with complete intention, with clear and glorious thought, to bring to light the "Great Vision"—the honored and awesome Name, His powers, His wonders, their connections, their substitutions, their reversals, their forms, and the number of their holiness. He who is perfect in everything is only "The Lord, The Lord" <note>Y-H-V-H</note> engraved upon the Throne on high, and all His hosts bow to Him. For there are three Palaces <note>Hekhalot</note> where the Names are engraved, and all of them are these awesome powers: "Fruit of Life," distinct from one another, changing in their powers from one another—higher than one another, more awesome than one another, more wondrous than one another, stronger than one another, purer than one another, clearer than one another, cleaner than one another, more upright than one another, more perfect than one another, more complete than one another, more radiant than one another, [higher] *Sefirot* than one another, more "burning" <note>Seraphim</note> than one another, more "living" <note>Chayot</note> than one another, more inward than one another, more holy than one another. They are all flaming fire, standing in dread and in awe, and with fear they sanctify and revere the God who created them, and who set each one in its dominion to do the will of its Creator with the twenty-two letters <note>The Hebrew alphabet</note> which are engraved in these three Palaces. The form of each letter is unique to itself, and they are truly called "supernal powers." And I shall reveal to you a hidden, deep, and awesome secret; and you, my son, be upright and faithful to me, and let your intention be correct. Know for yourself that these are literally the powers of The Name in truth, called supernal powers, which are engraved and sealed upon the Throne. Each letter is unique in the form of its power, and they are the "Chariots" <note>Possibly a corruption of 'Holy ones' or 'Chariots'</note>, the angels of the living and awesome God, and holy and supernal signs. Then you shall be clarified, for Moses joined them so that they might emerge from their joining, and from their form, and from their power—

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the divine names and the spiritual requirements for those who engage with these mysteries. It emphasizes that the power contained within these names is so intense that without extreme purity and holiness, the practitioner risks being consumed by the spiritual fire of the supernal realms. # The Great Book of Raziel

And from their power, lest those who study them be consumed by fire, let each person change every power within themselves to its own force and direct it toward its specific action. Then, that angel shall be called "a messenger of the living God" <note>Original: מלאך אלהים חיים</note> and a "supernal power," for from those celestial forces, influence flows to those below. Power then acts and connects to power according to their movement, their strength, and their secret purpose. Know that the appropriate divine flow <term>Shefa|The spiritual abundance or "flow" that descends from the divine to the physical world</term> does not descend except through great strength, great purity, immense holiness, and subtle, upright contemplation. If you are spiritually alive, you will understand my words: in an impure land, life and prophets are not found, except when required by the necessity of the hour; for the divine flow does not descend outside the Land <note>Referring to the Land of Israel</note>. This is the secret of what our Sages, of blessed memory, said: "Whoever dwells outside the Land is like one who has no God." If you "cut" <note>To "cut" or "sever" here refers to the misuse of divine names or "cutting the shoots" of faith—a kabbalistic term for heresy or spiritual damage</note> these powers by recognizing them too closely, they will burn and perish. Regarding this, our Sages said that it is a great prohibition to "cut" into the supernal powers without a very great necessity.

This explains to you the Throne: Crown, Wisdom, and Understanding <note>The first three Sefirot: Keter, Chokhmah, and Binah</note> constitute the Spirit of the Living God. These are internal, ancient, powerful, and awesome forces. A Seraph <note>A high-ranking angel associated with fire</note> is on this side, a Seraph on that side, and a Seraph on the third side, with the "Chariot of the Patriarchs" <note>The spiritual foundations represented by Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob</note> between them. While some say the Patriarchs are higher than the Seraphim, this is their interpretation; regardless, both are wondrous and mighty powers. However, the verse testifies: "Seraphim stood above Him" <note>Isaiah 6:2</note>—above all supernal powers. They are a "dividing fire" surrounding them, running and flying with all their strength from the day of their creation for all eternity. They stand in their race only to reach the glory of their Creator, as it is written: "And one of the Seraphim flew to me." They call to one another and ask: "Where is His holiness? Where is His glory?" They call out "Holy!" and say "Holy!" and respond to one another: "Holy is the Lord of Hosts!" <note>Original: קדוש ה' צבאות</note>. From the greatness of the strength of their race and His holiness, they cry out with a loud voice, bowing and sanctifying in dread and fear, saying: "Blessed be the glory of the Lord from His place!" All the supernal beings tremble and fear, saying: "Blessed be the name of His glorious kingdom forever and ever!" <note>Original abbreviation: בשכמל"ו — Baruch Shem Kevod Malchuto Le'olam Va'ed</note>. The Chariot, the other supernal Seraphim, the other high powers, the angels, and the souls of the righteous together with Israel below—all of them in dread and awe respond and say: "Holy, Holy, Holy is the Lord of Hosts!" At that moment, the Lord is exalted, magnified, and uplifted upon His throne of kingship. He sits upon the Throne of Grace and Mercy and He also says: "You are My servant, Israel, in whom I will be glorified." Happy is the man who visits every day to praise Him, to glorify Him, to revere Him in dread and fear, to sanctify Him, and to say "Blessed be the name of His glorious kingdom forever and ever." One who trusts in the Lord shall be surrounded by kindness.

All these powers differ from one another according to their strength and rank, each inscribed in His "Palace"—meaning in the place appropriate for its action. They are arranged: this one to the East, that one to the West, this one to the North, and that one to the South. This one is for mercy, that one for judgment; this one to bring back, that one to embolden; this one to reward, that one to punish transgression; this one for righteousness, that one for justice; this one for charity, that one for the cry of the poor; this one for peace, that one for war; this one to kill and destroy, and that one for peace and to save from evil; this one for hate, and that one for love. All is according to the will of their Creator and King, who understood them and established each man over his service and his burden. They are all awesome, living powers, and they bow to Him every day and say "Blessed be the Lord forever, Amen and Amen" <note>Original abbreviation: בכל"ש — Baruch Hashem Le'olam, Amen Ve'Amen</note>. Happy is the man who fears God and understands these great awesomes; let him set his heart to this, cast away his "filthy garments" <note>A metaphor for sins or physical attachments</note> and clothe himself in garments of salvation and white robes, for they are your life and the length of your days.

The rectification of the letters is as follows: The *Aleph* (א), the *Mem* (מ), and the *Tav* (ת) are on one path, and the Name is over them, for they are powers within great and awesome powers. When the Name is sent with them, it seals either for mercy or for judgment. When you join them, they are called *Emet* <note>Original: אמת — Truth. Composed of the first, middle, and last letters of the Hebrew alphabet</note>. This is what our Sages said: "Truth is the seal of the Holy One, Blessed be He," for judgment proceeds from before Him and is turned into mercy. All His letters are given in one form and they stand as one at the Throne, showing that they are not like you below. For the *Aleph* has three letters <note>Visually, an Aleph is composed of an upper Yod, a diagonal Vav, and a lower Yod</note>; the *Bet* has two; the *Gimel*, *Dalet*, and *Resh* lie on one path and have no connection <note>Perhaps referring to their angular shapes</note>, for it is a holy letter. The Name *Dalet* has two forms because they are not connected, for they are holy. The *Vavs* (ו) are the pillars of the sanctuary. The *Chet* (ח), *Chet* (ח), *Tet* (ט), and *Kaf* (כ) are thus connected, and the *Gimel* (ג) is its form, for it follows in the count. From it, the wicked abyss was created, for from it darkness emerged into the world, and there the wicked of the nations of the world are judged. The *Yod* (י) is a single point, entirely holy <note>Numerical value 10</note>; its numerical value <term>Gematria|The kabbalistic practice of calculating the numerical value of letters to find deeper meanings</term> shows that "the Lord alone is exalted." So it is with the other letters in holiness: *Lamed* (ל) is 26, *Nun* (נ) is holy, *Samekh* (ס) is 20 and 6, *Ayin* (ע) is 53, *Pe* (פ) is 20 and 6, *Tzadi* (צ) is 10 and 2, *Kuf* (ק) is 6, *Resh* (ר) is holy, *Shin* (ש) represents the Patriarchs, *Tav* (ת) is 200 and 6. For every thing is directed toward what is above. The four Patriarchs correspond to the four directions of the world, for they are the pillars upon which the world stands through its righteousness.

The Palace of Metatron <note>The highest archangel in kabbalistic tradition, often associated with the transformed Enoch</note> is the Pure Intellect from which influence flows into the soul of the "Complete Man"—the Pure and Acquired Intellect. The Palace of the "Separate Intellects" <note>Disembodied spiritual intelligences</note> belongs to the intellect of the angels, the intellect of the righteous, and the intellect of the spirits and souls; this is the Supernal World. However, the World to Come is the Palace of Jacob, the Palace of Metatron; for there come the souls of the righteous which are above the lower world. He is Metatron, the "Active Intellect" <note>Original: שכל הפועל — The bridge between the divine and human mind</note>, and he is the power of *Shaddai* <note>A divine name often translated as Almighty</note>, and all is one power. He is called "Metatron" because he comes from below <note>A wordplay on *mi-lemata*, from below</note>, for he is pure and clean, separated from matter, and he comes to the place which is his source, the place of his quarry—the pure soul. This is the third world, the Garden of Eden of above, pure and upright, and all is the power of the Most High God.

Furthermore, the letters are great and mighty powers beyond investigation, and from them other powers receive. See that all is One. *He-Yod-Vav* (היו) and *Resh-Vav-Vav* (רוו) is the head of all supernal powers. The forms of the *Aleph* were joined when Moses ascended to receive the Torah. They <note>The angels</note> knew that Moses wanted to bring them down from their rank and holiness, and they were greatly stirred and wanted to burn him. But the Rock <note>God</note> spread His Name over them so that they would descend to the world of matter. He hinted to them regarding "my servant Moses" <note>Original: עברי במשה</note> that the souls of the righteous are also taken from the supernal beings during the time of their existence within the human body, and because of this, they have a rank higher than the other supernal powers. For it is hinted that "the Lord is high, yet He sees the lowly," and "He is the rider in the heavens," and "He is the father of orphans and judge of widows." You must understand this: the powers of the Name, which are higher than all, are also lower than all <note>God's presence is found in both the highest heavens and the lowliest places</note>. Regarding this, they said that the souls of the righteous are higher than the angels. When they heard the word of the Lord from His rebuke, they were all scattered, and immediately every letter was joined to its form and its connection. They came into six and descended with him, engraved upon the Tablets. There, they taught him the secret of the sanctities and the *Sefirot*, which are the twenty-two holy letters from which the entire Torah was joined—for from their light is the light of the upper and lower worlds—and seventy names from them...

<meta>catchword: from them [מהם]</meta>
