->The Great Book of Raziel<-

<margin>Ammam (עמם)</margin> teaches seventy aspects under the Name, and from them comes the power to belong to the "moving stars" <note>Referring to the planets or celestial spheres</note> that shift five toward the formation. **Nena (ננא)** teaches that the supernal powers praise the Name every day with one hundred types of praise and blessings; corresponding to them, Israel instituted one hundred blessings each day, and one who prays them is considered as if they were among the celestial beings. **Nait (נית)** teaches that this pillar is the "Righteous One, the foundation of the world" <term>Tzadik Yesod Olam|A kabbalistic concept where a foundational spiritual force sustains all of existence</term> upon which the world stands. It is the attribute of Truth, for it is the supernal light and the seal of the Holy One, Blessed be He. **Mebah (מבה)** teaches that the spirit of God is the pure intellect, which He commanded to descend into Man, who is the "small world" <term>Microcosm|The philosophical idea that the human being is a miniature reflection of the entire universe</term>. **Poi (פוי)** teaches that the ten plagues of Egypt are weighed against ten things. These seven names are pure, clear, and strong for requesting any matter through prayer. One should recite them over pure "living water" from a flowing spring three times, wash his face, and pour the remainder over his head, saying: "May it be Your will before You and these Your great and holy names, that You shall teach me every matter of Torah and wisdom." **AAA SSS** <note>original: "אאא ססס"</note> This should be done every day at five in the morning, from the New Moon of Nisan until the middle of the month. **Nemam (נמם)** teaches that the fiftieth gate is the power of God; the secrets hidden from every creature used to dwell in the lower Sanctuary <note>The Temple in Jerusalem</note>. When the holy Temple was nullified, that power was also nullified, for in the time of holiness, "holiness was within holiness."

<margin>Yal (ייל)</margin> teaches that this power was elevated above all that is supernal. **Hehah (ההח)** teaches that the holy Name is exalted for the pure ones, and who can turn it back?

<margin>Metzar (מצר)</margin> teaches that this corresponds to the Name *Adonai* <note>The Lord</note>; this is against that, and it is another power. **Umab (ומב)** teaches that the Name gives strength in our time to lead them day and night, from east to west. **Yahah (יהה)** teaches the secret of the "Double Heh" (H-H) which takes hold of the entire world with justice and mercy. **Annu (עננו)** teaches that He is sometimes seen in the attribute of mercy through the "enfolding of tongues" to bring people to repentance, for they are seventy corresponding to the seventy above. These seven holy names are good for seeking prayer, forgiveness, and atonement for all one's iniquities. One should confess on the night following the Sabbath in awe and purity. If one knows they have committed a great sin or transgression and is in great danger from the government or from any great man, they should confess all their sins with this Name and say: "May it be Your will before You, Great Name, for You are the Master of forgiveness and atonement, that You forgive me for all my iniquities and save me from this death." It is good for every trouble. If one prays it with complete intention and great purity on Thursday, it is as the attribute of Moses in his hour. **Machi (מחי)** teaches that they sanctify Him above and Israel sanctifies Him below, so that they may be together in one power and become one Tabernacle. **Damb (דמב)** teaches that just as He illuminates seven powers above, so too seven illuminate below before the Name in their righteousness; and so it is for every righteous person. **Menak (מנק)** teaches that with these seventy praises, the powers surrounding the Throne are always on the missions of the Name, and from them, the "Eight of Divinity" flows into the Garden of Eden.

<margin>Aia (איע)</margin> teaches that with this Name, he <note>Moses</note> pleaded to come and enter the Land, but he was not heard. **Chavu (חבו)** teaches of His unity. **Reeh (ראה)** teaches that here the prophet hinted at the powers of God, may He be blessed, rising from the ten *Sefirot* <term>Sefirot|The ten creative forces or attributes of God in Kabbalah</term>, for they are four hundred in thought, and the third power is from Him. They are ten, which are eight in thought; he called it "child" because of the flow <term>Shefa|Divine abundance</term> and also because of the power, for the "base" <note>Hebrew: 'ken'</note> is "Man," and "Man" is supernal. **Yebam (יבם)** teaches that it hints at the final attribute <note>Malkhut</note> which is comprised of everything. **Heiy (היי)** teaches that these are the "three actions" written as three, and they are the sustenance of His great Name. For through them the Torah was given and the world was created, and with three of them the "mighty acts of the Lord" were sealed. He brought out His awesome powers with great and strong wonders in the upper and lower realms, as it is written: "And I will show wonders in the heavens and on the earth," great and faithful plagues that are remembered forever for all coming generations. This is what is written: "Thus shall you say to the children of Israel, 'I Shall Be' (Ehyeh) has sent me... this is My name forever" <note>Exodus 3:14-15</note> for all who come into this world and for all eternity. It is a remembrance for generation to generation, to return the "wrathful ones" <note>unclear: possibly referring to spirits or humans</note>; "generation to generation" refers to the six thousand years of all the days of your life, until the generation of the second desolation. This is what is written: "The Rock, His work is perfect" <note>Deuteronomy 32:4</note>, the Name that is living and strong for all eternity.

**Mom (מום)** teaches that the beginning of the Name's work is with mercy and the end is with mercy. Because of this, all His creatures praise Him and magnify Him, recognizing and knowing that He is the God who nourishes and sustains everything, and everything praises Him, including the kings of the earth and all nations, princes and all judges of the earth.

**And now**, my wise son, behold I have explained to you the great and awesome things, the statutes, and also the precious and glorious actions that are clearly written. Do not change them to gaze upon them <note>i.e., do not use them for mere curiosity</note>, and do not enter the Holy Place at all times until you have written "Pure Holiness" and clothed yourself as the High Priest. The common people shall not go up with you when you come to see the face of the Lord your God in fear and dread, with a pure heart and complete intention, with clear and glorious thought, to bring to light the Great Vision: the honored and awesome Name, His powers, His wonders, their combinations, their substitutions, and their reversals; the forms and the number of their holiness. This is appropriate for the perfect ones, for these are the "seven words of the living God," and not for others who are hungry and thirsty to drink "living and awesome waters." Then one will understand the secret of the form of the Alphabet, for it is engraved upon the Throne of Glory, and all His hosts bow to Him. For there are three Palaces <term>Heikhalot|Celestial palaces or chambers through which a mystic ascends</term> where there are statutes, and all of them are these awesome powers of the living God. They differ from one another, substituted in powers from one another: higher than one another, more awesome than one another, more wondrous than one another, stronger than one another, purer than one another, clearer than one another, cleaner than one another, more upright than one another, more perfect than one another, more complete than one another, more radiant than one another... burning more than one another, living more than one another, more internal than one another, more holy than one another. All of them are a flaming fire, standing in fear and dread, and in awe they sanctify and revere the God who created them, who placed each one in its dominion to do the will of its Creator. Two letters are engraved in these three Palaces, and the form of each letter is unique. They are truly called "Supernal Powers." I shall reveal to you a hidden, deep, and awesome secret. You, my son, be upright and faithful, and let your intention be correct. Know that these are truly the powers of the Name, called Supernal Powers, which are engraved and sealed upon the Throne—each letter in its own form and power. They are called the "Angels of the Living and Awesome God" and "Holy and Supernal Signs." Then you shall join them, for Moses joined them so they would emerge from their union, their form, and their power.

Let us return and speak a little regarding the explanation of these eight Princes. Know that the name **Aza** <note>Hebrew: עז"א; likely referring to a celestial prince of wisdom</note> indicates Wisdom, and the name **Uziel** <note>Hebrew: עוזי"אל, meaning "Strength of God"</note> indicates the Throne of Glory; he serves as a High Priest. Within their secret, all things are housed; then four mysteries of Wisdom shall be revealed. They shall make our paths prosper and guide us in the way we should walk, delivering us from the "bitterness" that stands against us—which you know from the "foundation of his teacher" <note>An idiomatic or coded reference to foundational spiritual principles</note>. Life and death are in the power of the tongue, for everything has been handed over into the hand of man. If you are among those who love the Lord, strive to know His Name for His glory, and choose life.

Know, my brother, that the numerical calculation <term>Gematria|The kabbalistic practice of assigning numerical values to Hebrew letters to find deeper meanings</term> of this holy Name indicates the supernal powers and their influence. This is a Name of twenty-seven letters. They consist of two groups which are "two faces"—meaning Fire and Water. These are handed over into the authority of **Metatron** <note>The highest archangel in kabbalistic tradition, often called the "Prince of the Face" or the celestial scribe</note>. I wish to explain them to you as they are. Know that the first name of these is this:

<margin>V-H-H-V</margin>
Its definitive calculation is 18, and with the Name itself, it reaches 935 <note>The text uses letter-numerals; these calculations often involve complex permutations</note>. This is the explanation of the oath regarding the "Structure of Fire": I adjure the wheels of the Chariot <term>Merkavah|The "Chariot" seen in the visions of the prophet Ezekiel, representing the divine realm</term> by the power of this Supernal Man <term>Adam HaElyon|The primordial, spiritual prototype of humanity</term> who is appointed over the inner powers of Fire. Act immediately through this power! Understand this matter well, for it is a most beneficial secret. Know that just as we spoke of the three Names which are two opposites with a middle one to decide between them, so too in this "middle" resides the Prince of the supernal powers. I wish to explain to you this deciding middle Name. It functions in two ways, a "chosen circle" as you know; understand that the first way is according to its plain meaning, and it is this:

<margin>Y-H-V</margin>
This is the sign of the memory of 509. It is over the "middle" and is a secret. This is Metatron, the mediator—meaning he rules over them and over the celestial spheres, doing with them as he desires and wills by the power of his Creator, blessed be He. Just as the powers were handed into his hand, he possesses the Name in the second way mentioned. Metatron is the ruler through the power of the cycle of the sphere over Water and Fire. This is the wondrous secret that emerges from him, a crown of understanding. Hear this, if you are a person of intellect, and see the power of these six that emerge from the Unique Name, blessed be He. Yet they themselves perform their opposites, like the dead, and they proclaim His light to stand by their power. After we have spoken of the two names, we shall return and speak of the remaining name, which is the opposite of Fire—meaning Water—and it is this:

<margin>H-V</margin>
Its necessary calculation is 909. The general principle prepared from it is an explanation of what is said: "Firstly, I adjure you, Prince Metatron, who is appointed over humanity, by the [alphabetic ciphers] *ATG* and *ABGD*, and by the spheres above and below; compel them before me by the power of the hand of the Unique Name to fulfill my will immediately!" By this, the upper and lower waters were split. This is the secret of the "lower man," and this is the oath that emerges from his secret. Know that this is a wondrous hint from what is mentioned within two words. Understand this very well and be very careful in preparing these matters, for they are deep secrets and the received traditions of the supernal holy ones. Set your heart, my son, toward Wisdom, and seek from there the Lord your God; you shall find Him if you seek Him with all your heart and all your soul. I shall guide you in this way you shall walk, in every oath whose matter and roots you shall know forever. Always strive to weigh your work and its hidden parts before the one being adjured. If you adjure any Name whose help, root, and manner you know, along with everything that follows it as is fitting, do not adjure it for its own sake, but only for the glory of the Name at the beginning of matters. May we be honored by it. I shall mention the Name several times; perhaps He will answer you in all that you desire from the Lord. This is the form of the oath according to the "weight" in the Name:

<margin>Y-H-W-H</margin>
We have heard the one who surrounds the Tablets: Metatron, Prince of the Face, Prince of Hosts! By the movement of the sphere, behold, you have conquered the entire world by the power of the sphere of Moses, Aaron, and Miriam. The Creator, blessed be He, brings forth from the wisdom of knowledge within the creatures. Many times I adjure by the Vision, by the Image of God, by the Creator, the God of Abraham, Isaac, and the God of Jacob, by the Explicit Name:

<margin>Y-H-W-H</margin>
That You may inform me of the foundation of prophecy at any time that I seek it with my mouth, and that You may teach me of the World to Come, and that You may teach me from that which is Yours another secret, for I am a "member of the household."

**Q: Kamatz** <note>A Hebrew vowel mark ( ָ )</note>. If it comes under the Name... one can use it for dominion. With a **Patach** ( ַ ), one can establish wealth. When it is with a **Tzere** ( ֵ ), one can excommunicate <note>To ban or place under a curse</note>. When it is with a **Segol** ( ֶ ), one can create love and brotherhood. # The Great Book of Raziel

From them and the Proper Name <note>Hebrew: *Shem HaEtzem*; refers to the Tetragrammaton, the essential name of God</note>, everything is one power and everything is one thing. All of these are supernal powers, awesome things, secrets that are sealed and carved—a lock upon a lock. They are statutes and precious essences, stories that are very deep and too distant to be explained to the many, or even to individuals, except to a man who is a High Priest <note>Historically, the only person permitted to enter the Holy of Holies and speak the explicit name of God</note> and who knows the *Urim and Thummim* <term>Urim and Thummim|Oracular elements of the High Priest's breastplate used to discern the Divine will</term>. Such a man possesses clear thought to recognize and know the times and seasons; then he shall sacrifice and offer righteous sacrifices at the precious gates, using pure birds. The rest of the people, restrained and distant, shall stand and prostrate themselves. If even one of them should draw near, the Priest would cease his service, and fire would descend from heaven and consume them, destroying them and casting them out "to another land, as it is this day." <note>A quotation from Deuteronomy 29:27</note> The secret things belong to the Lord our God, but the revealed things belong to us and our children forever, to perform all the words of this Torah. Happy is the man who fears the Lord and greatly desires His commandments, in the Name of the Lord.

<margin>Let it be</margin>
**Y-H** <note>The first two letters of the Tetragrammaton</note> is renewed in the secret of the vocalization <note>Hebrew: *nikkud*; the vowel points added to Hebrew letters</note> of the Unique Name known to every Kabbalist. For the Lord is One, the root and principle of everything. The calculation of the letters <term>Gematria|The practice of calculating the numerical value of Hebrew words to find mystical connections</term> between this and His attributes is three, which is *Adonai* <note>The name of God usually translated as 'Lord'</note>.
And **H-Y** and **V-Y-H-Y** and **Y-H-Y**—this name is an extension of the letter *Yod*. The calculation of the vocalization of the first name, which is the one written here, totals one thousand and... <note>The text becomes technical regarding the sum of vowel values</note> and the necessary sign is under the letter *Shin*. Combined with the *Shin*, it rises to 935. Understand very deeply in your wisdom, and then its hidden meaning shall be revealed to you.

<margin>Let it be</margin>
**Know** that the general principle emerging from the letters and the vocalization depicted above is the drawing forth of the Unique Name through the praise of Metatron <note>The supreme archangel and celestial scribe</note>, the Prince. Then say, if you wish: "I adjure you," and so on. **Sh-R-B-A-Y**. Know that the secret of this name corresponds to the powers of the Sun and its companions. The explanation of its secret is **Azael** <note>Hebrew: עז"אל</note>, Sun, Fire, Light, and Day. These correspond to the letters with which Metatron seals this, just as I informed you in the secret of the vocalization of the letters. Such is the first Name in its letters and its vocalization. Be aware of this note regarding this matter. And behold, I am now writing regarding two names that remain, which are:

<margin>Let it be</margin>
**V-Y-H-Y H-Y**. I have already informed you that the first name—which is Azael, Sun, Light, and Fire—means that the power of the Name compels these to perform the word of the petitioner. Likewise, the second Name, which is the middle one, indicates two names that rule over the creatures, which I will make known to you in the form of the Name. This is the middle Name which indicates both of them.

<margin>Let it be</margin>
The general principle resulting from the knowledge of the vocalizations and letters is 238. It indicates the two rulers, who are the princes **Azael** and **Azaael** <note>Hebrew: עזא"ל and עזא"אל</note>. This [Name] compels between them. This is the secret: Azael and Azaael already indicate to you the two rulers... as it is explained. And after I have informed you of the secret of this middle Name, I shall inform you of the secret of the third Name, which is this:

<margin>H-Y</margin>
**Know** that the general principle coming into our hands indicates the Name in its vocalization and its letters. The necessary signs total 953. Their secret is **Azaael**, Moon, Water, Darkness, and Night. After this, behold, the Name "Let it be" (*Yehi*) compels by the Unique Name corresponding to the six letters from which comes the Prince and the Shepherd. Now that I have informed you of its matter by hint, let us return and remind you of the three of them together. Know that the Great Unique Name compels two princes who are the opposite of one another to dwell together; neither enters the border of his brother, nor does one draw near to the other. For the middle Name, as I have informed you, compels between the two of them—that is, between Azael and Azaael. Behold, I am depicting again the law of their transformation... one against the other, and the middle Name is their seal.
**Y-H-N** is a lasting bond that sustains both of them, and this is the form of your ritual work:

Below it, a large rectangle is divided into sections. 
- The right section is headed "V-H-Y-H-Y" and contains: "His secret is Sun for fire for light for day" written above the name "Azael".
- The left section is headed "H-Y" and contains: "Their secret is Azaael, Moon, Water, Darkness, and Night" and "The form of Aza: Moon, Water, Darkness, Night."
- The center of this large box contains the phrase "This and this is their form."
Below the main rectangle are two distinct elements.
- On the right, a section titled "Behold the order of their arrangement" features a smaller box with "Azael and Sun and Fire for light for day." To the left of this smaller box, the word "Against" is repeated vertically three times.
- On the left, a vertical staff or key-like shape with five circles on its handle, each labeled with the word "Name" (Shem).

Behold the form of their standing, one against the other and one the opposite of the other, and that which sustains in the middle to keep them standing. And after we have spoken to you a little regarding the matter of their existence, let us return...

<meta>This page transitions from the general nature of the "Great Name" and the priestly duties discussed on the previous page to the specific history and content of the Book of Raziel itself. It establishes the book’s lineage from the angel Raziel to Noah, and eventually to King Solomon, before beginning a detailed catalog of the first heaven and its angelic inhabitants.</meta>

# The Book of Raziel

**In the name** of the Lord, the God of Israel, I begin this book, which is one of the books of secrets <note>Hebrew: *Sifrei HaRazim*</note> given to Noah, son of Lamech, son of Methuselah, son of Enoch, son of Jared, son of Mahalalel, son of Enosh, son of Seth, son of Adam, from the mouth of the angel Raziel. It was given in the year of his entry into the Ark, before he went inside. It was written upon a sapphire stone <note>Sapphire in mystical texts often represents divine clarity or the material of the Tablets of the Law</note> very clearly. From it, Noah learned wondrous deeds and the secrets of the levels of understanding, thoughts of humility, and schemes of counsel: how to stand upon the investigation of the degrees of the heights, to roam through all that is in the seventh habitation <note>Referring to *Araboth*, the highest of the seven heavens where the Divine Throne resides</note>, to revolve everything within its cycles, and to contemplate every custom. He learned to examine the cycle of the moon and to know the paths of the constellations—the Great Bear, Orion, and the Pleiades <term>Ash, Kesil, and Kimah|Ancient Hebrew names for constellations mentioned in the Book of Job, used here for astrological calculation</term>. 

He learned to declare the names within each and every firmament, what their tasks are, how they might succeed in every matter, and what the names of the "servants of the officers" are in every season. Through this book, these beings would listen to those who approached them in purity to do their every desire. From it, he also learned the works of death and the works of life, to understand good and evil, to investigate times and moments, and to know the time to give birth and the time to die, the time to strike and the time to heal. He could interpret dreams and visions, stir up strife or make peace in wars, and rule over spirits and demons <note>Hebrew: *Pega'im*, literally "afflictions" or "harmful encounters," often referring to malevolent spirits</note>, sending them away so they would go like slaves. He learned to look to the four winds of the earth, to become wise through the sound of thunders, to tell of the workings of lightning, and to declare what would happen in each and every month. He could oversee the business of each year—whether for grain or for drought, whether for peace or for war—becoming like one of the "Awesome Ones" <note>Referring to high-ranking angels</note> and understanding the treasures of the heights.

**And the wisdom** of the book taught Noah how to make the Ark of gopher wood and how to hide from the rushing waters of the Flood. It taught him to bring in with him the pairs of twos and the sevens <note>Referring to the clean and unclean animals mentioned in Genesis 7:2</note>, and to bring in every type of food and sustenance. He placed the book in a golden chest and brought it first into the Ark. It served as a witness for him for the day and the times of the night, showing at which season he should stand to offer his cry. When he exited the Ark, he used it all the days of his life. At the time of his death, he handed it to Shem, and Shem to Abraham, and Abraham to Isaac, and Isaac to Jacob, and Jacob to Levi, and Levi to Kohath, and Kohath to Amram, and Amram to Moses, and Moses to Joshua, and Joshua to the Elders, and the Elders to the Prophets, and the Prophets to the Sages. 

So it continued in every generation until King Solomon arose. The Book of Secrets was revealed to him, and he became very wise in the words of the secrets. He gained power over and ruled all the spirits, all desires, and the demons that roam the world; he bound them and released them and governed them. He built and succeeded through the wisdom of this book, and many other books were delivered into his hand, but this one was found to be the most precious and honored of them all. Happy is the eye that sees, the ear that hears, and the heart that understands and becomes wise by its wisdom. In the seven firmaments, everything that is within them and their camps can be learned, to become wise in every matter and to succeed in every deed, to act and to think through the wisdom of the book.

## The Name of the First Firmament
It is called *Shamim* <note>A variation of *Shamayim*, the Hebrew word for Heavens</note>. Within it are camps of angels, and seven thrones are prepared there. Upon them sit seven officers, surrounded by camps on this side and that. They listen to human beings at the time of their occupation—to anyone who stands in the business of their names and mentions them in the season when they listen—to make a deed succeed. Over all these camps of angels, these seven officers rule, to send them for any desire or will, and to bring success.

**And these** are the seven names of those who sit upon the seven thrones:
1. The first name is **Orpaniel**.
2. The second name is **Igda**.
3. The third name is **Duchal**.
4. The fourth name is **Palmiya**.
5. The fifth name is **Asmir**.
6. The sixth name is **Paskar**.
7. The seventh name is **Pu'el**.

They were created from fire, and their appearance is fire, and their fire is flaming, for they came out of fire. The angels who serve them do not go out to engage in any task or deed until a word goes out to them from the mouths of the seven officers sitting on the thrones who rule over them. They walk beneath the [throne] and the wheels, and each one stands at his task. They are prepared for whatever they are sent for—whether for good or for evil, for lack or for war or for peace—and all of them have been called by name from the day they were created.

**And these** are the names of the seven camps that serve the seven officers, for each camp belongs to one of the officers. Mention the name of the angels serving before him seven times and say thus: "I, [So-and-so] son of [So-and-so], request before you that you bring success through the healing of [So-and-so] son of [So-and-so]"—or whatever one requests for healing, whether in writing or by a spoken request. Purify yourself from all ritual impurity and cleanse your flesh from all lewdness, and then you shall succeed.

**And these** are the names of the angels who serve **Orpaniel**:
Hamuyidi, Ramna, Afur, Alfi, Amuyik, Katki, Patafar, Gamtar, Pa'aur, Nutak, Rakhatu, Udna, Ma'ot, Prusa, Akua, Vaharkua, Bruki, Hasdura, Atnani, Golan, Hamah, Adnoa, Akka, Nataniel, Aduiel, Anuf, Hari'ur, Abdiel, Ton, Alin, Mul, Lalaf, and Hasfat, Rahmaniel, Rumalo, and Kativ, Adnuiel, Ashmu, Yoash, Kafin, Karbu, Gershom, Pri'af, Shemash'e, Pukin, Kriel, Zabriel, Agdalan, Migal, Nefial, Karuna, Kulda, Digal, Albi, Hohali, Suklit, Ariel, Eyal, Kas'el, Sukma, Ashka, Nutna, Haliel, Ast'el, Tasha, Michael, Nataniel, Afonia, Telgal, Na'ana, Ostiel.

**And these** are the angels of the second camp who serve **Igda**:
Akshur, Markas, Karbak, Kamshan, Ishpak, Radatiel...

# The Great Book of Raziel

...apparently <note>Hebrew: *Lich’* - likely an abbreviation for *lichora*, meaning "apparently" or "it seems"</note>. And when the Name is vocalized with a *Great Shuruk* <term>Shuruk|A Hebrew vowel mark producing an 'oo' sound</term>, no destroyers or demons shall harm him. When it comes with a *Sheva* <term>Sheva|A Hebrew vowel mark indicating a very short 'e' or a silent break</term>, it is for healing the sick. When it comes with a *Small Shuruk* <note>Also known as *Kibbutz*</note>, it is for knowing the constellations. When it is with a *Kamatz* <term>Kamatz|A vowel mark usually producing an 'ah' sound</term>, it is to prevail over one's companion. When it is with a *Patach* <term>Patach|A vowel mark producing a short 'a' sound</term>, it is to become wise in his presence. When it is with a *Tzere* <term>Tzere|A vowel mark producing an 'ay' sound</term>, it is for enrichment. When it is with a *Segol* <term>Segol|A vowel mark producing an 'eh' sound</term>, it is for love. When it is with a *Cholam* <term>Cholam|A vowel mark producing an 'oh' sound</term>, it is for success. When it is with a *Chirik* <term>Chirik|A vowel mark producing an 'ee' sound</term>, it is to cast down an enemy. When it is with a *Great Shuruk*, it is to subdue a ruler. When it is with a *Sheva*, it is for going out and coming in. When it is with a *Small Shuruk*, it is for grace and favor. The secret of the hint of the Name is complete; let the wise understand.

## An Awesome and Holy Prayer for when one reaches "He who hears prayer"

<margin>**Master of the Universe**</margin> It is revealed and known before You that I have sinned, I have been perverse, and I have transgressed, and I have committed every transgression in the world. I am not worthy to request anything from You. But because You are full of mercy, as it is written in Your Torah: *The Lord, the Lord, God, compassionate and gracious, slow to anger, and abundant in kindness and truth, preserving kindness for thousands, forgiving iniquity, transgression, and sin, and cleansing* <note>Exodus 34:6-7, the Thirteen Attributes of Mercy</note>, I have come to pray before You in Your Great, Mighty, Strong, and Pure Name, the Explicit Name <term>Shem HaMephorash|The hidden, multi-lettered name of God used in Kabbalistic practice</term>, who sits upon the wings of the high and holy wind, which is written but not read, the God of Hosts. **Y-H-W-H, Y-H-W-H, Y-H-W-H** <note>The Tetragrammaton is written with vowel points here</note> who are holy in Your awesome crown, whose name is in You. We adjure <note>The text shifts from "I" to "We," possibly a communal or formal adjuration</note> Gabriel and Misgabiel <note>Angelic names meaning "God is my strength" and "God is my tower"</note> that you shall save me from every evil thing and every evil mishap, I and all my household, and that You shall command blessing upon all the toil of my labor, and grant me grace, favor, and mercy in Your eyes and in the eyes of all who see me. Blessed are You, O Lord, who hears prayer. And afterward, he says the prayers *Retzei*, *Modim*, and *Sim Shalom* <note>The concluding blessings of the *Amidah*, the standing silent prayer</note>.

## This is the Secret of the Unique, Honored, and Awesome Name

<margin>**A Dream Inquiry**</margin> Let him write this Name on ruled parchment and place it under his head, and let him say: "I adjure you, Prince of Dreams, by the Great, Mighty, and Awesome Name, that you shall come to me this night and tell me my question and my request, whether by dream, or by vision, or by a biblical verse, or by speech, or by a matter of law, or by writing, in a manner that I shall not forget, and I shall remember my question: **AAA SSS**." And this is the Name: **MAABRIT ABRIT BRIT RIT IT T** <note>Original: *Ma'avrit*. This is a common Kabbalistic 'disappearing' name where the word is shortened letter by letter to focus its power</note>.

<margin>**Vocalizing the Name**</margin> When a person is in distress, let him focus on the Name and its vocalization, and these are the vowel points as it is vocalized: *Forever, O Lord, Your word stands firm in heaven* <note>Psalm 119:89</note>. Let him say: "May it be Your will before You, this Strong, Mighty, Illustrious, and Powerful Name, that You save me from such-and-such, or do such-and-such with me." I received a tradition concerning one vowel point that was with Elijah, regarding the verse *Because he has set his love upon Me* <note>Psalm 91:14</note>. [Abbreviation: *Bila"v*].

<margin>**With the letter He**</margin> The Holy One, blessed be He, made the heavens and the earth, as it is said: *In their being created* <note>Genesis 2:4; Hebrew *Be-hibaram*</note>—He created them with the letter *He* [ה]. With the letter *Yod* [י], He created the sun and the moon, as it is said: *The work of Your fingers* <note>Psalm 8:4</note>, and let the wise understand. With the letter *Vav* [ו], He made the light and the darkness, and the creeping things, swarming things, wild beasts, and cattle, as it is said: *And she lies [v'shachava]... and my sin [v'chatati]* <note>The text here uses a cryptic biblical reference to link the letter Vav to the animalistic/physical nature</note>. With the letter *He* [ה], He strengthened and established everything, and it is doubled, as in *I Shall Be That I Shall Be* <note>Exodus 3:14; *Ehyeh Asher Ehyeh*</note>, and let the wise understand. Anyone who knows this Name and does not guard it is a companion to a destroyer; but anyone who knows it and guards it in purity, his days and years shall be lengthened, and he shall be honored forever and in the exalted upper world, as it is said: *He who guards the fig tree shall eat its fruit* <note>Proverbs 27:18</note>. Regarding him, it is said: *He who guards his master shall be honored*—meaning, the one who guards and honors his Master [God], his Master will honor him, as it is said: *I will deliver him and honor him; I will set him on high because he knows My name* <note>Psalm 91:14-15</note>. This refers to the one who knows the glory of his Creator, as it is said: *For over all the glory shall be a canopy* <note>Isaiah 4:5</note>, and let the wise understand. Blessed be the Name of His glorious kingdom forever and ever. Blessed be the Lord forever, Amen and Amen.

# The Book of Raziel

Gronza, Barona, Zenozam, Chachmiel, Turiel, Paniel, Kadmiel, Kapliya, Armiel, Armon, Harmarzar, Achumal <note>original: נ"א - "another version reads": Abisal</note>, Sapriel, Kananniel, Shababiel, Arminus, Tumi, Pius, Pastiel, Chatfiel, Parsamon, Nachliel.

**And these** are the Angels of Might and others of valor and strength, who run to every place and fly to every corner to bring all that they desire, and to bring a person who is in a distant land or one who has fled. Take four plates of copper and write upon each one of them his name, his mother’s name, and the name Paskar <note>A specific power-name used in various magical incantations</note>, and the names of the angels who serve him. And say: "I hand over to you, O Angels of Might, [Name] son of [Name], that you shall not delay him in any place where he dwells. Overturn his heart, whether in a city or a province, by day or by night, at sea or on land, in eating or in drinking. Fly him like a bird and bring him against his will, and do not let him tarry by day or by night." Place the four copper plates in the four corners of the house, and they will bring him, whether he is in a city or a province, whether it be a man or a servant who fled, or any lost thing.

**Upon** the sixth ascent stand these: Abahyu, Kanor, Dalkiel, Sachiel, Adrach, Nachliel, Tambiel, Samachiah, Rebaiel, Yokmiel, Shemihur, Mahariel, Rumiel, Bichos, and Zakanan, Kaniel, Pastian. At the time they perform their work, their heads are all shaped like citrons <note>original: *istrogilin* — likely referring to a rounded or citrus-like shape</note>. These who stand upon the sixth ascent conduct themselves with humility; their faces are full of glory, and they wear white garments. They go forth like men of great stature, awesome like members of the Great Assembly <note>original: *anshei yeshiva* — literally "men of the academy," implying a scholarly and dignified presence</note>. Their seats are upon thrones of glory; they are faithful to the truth and stand over the art of healing. Blessed be their Creator and Former, who formed all things by His word. To You, O Lord, is the greatness.

## This is the Work of Creation

**"In the beginning** God created the heavens and the earth" <note>Genesis 1:1</note>. Do not read *Bereshit* ("In the beginning") but rather *Bara Shit* ("He created six"). Thus you find it is written as *Bara Shit*. What does "In the beginning" (*Bereshit*) come to teach? It refers to the letters with which the heavens and the earth were created, as it is said: "For in Yah (*Yud-He*), the Lord (*Y-H-W-H*), is the Rock of Ages" <note>Isaiah 26:4</note>. *Yah* is two letters, and the Tetragrammaton is four letters—behold, there are six. Thus you have learned that with these six letters God created the heavens and the earth. And should you say only the heavens and the earth were created with these six letters? No, for it says "Rock of Ages" <note>literally "Creator of Worlds"</note>, teaching that with these six letters two worlds were created: This World and the World to Come. Therefore it is said *Bereshit*. What is "In the beginning"? With one letter God created the heavens and the earth and sealed them from the fire. And which is the one letter with which heaven and earth were created? It is the letter *He*, as it is said: "These are the generations of the heavens and the earth when they were created (*be-hibare'am*)" <note>Genesis 2:4</note>. Do not read *be-hibare'am* but *be-He bera'am* ("with the letter *He* He created them"). With this letter God created the heavens and the earth, This World and the World to Come, and sealed them from the fire of Gehenna <note>Hell</note>. They are divided from the fire, for the wicked are judged by burning hail—it burns within them like fire. They are judged from the fire into that which is like hail <note>Another version: They are judged in hail from fire to burning fire, and from burning fire to hail</note>. The Angels of Destruction descend upon them and preserve their souls within their bodies, as it is said: "For their worm shall not die..." <note>Isaiah 66:24</note>. And every single day the Angels of Destruction descend upon them, and every single day the Angel of Death descends upon them and drives them from the hail to the fire and from the fire to the hail, like a shepherd who drives his sheep from mountain to mountain, as it is said: "Like sheep they are laid in Sheol; death shall be their shepherd" <note>Psalm 49:14</note>. The Angels of Destruction descend upon them and judge them in Gehenna for twelve months. After twelve months, they bring them down to the Gates of the Shadow of Death and judge them there for twelve months. After twelve months, they bring them down to the Gates of Death and judge them there for twelve months. After twelve months, they bring them down to the Miry Clay and judge them there for twelve months. After twelve months, they bring them down to the Pit of Destruction and judge them there for twelve months. After twelve months, they bring them down to Abaddon <term>Abaddon|A place of utter destruction or ruin</term> and judge them there for twelve months. After twelve months, they bring them down to the Lowest Grave (*Sheol Tachtit*) and judge them there for twelve months, until all the righteous see them and say to the Inner One <note>God</note>, whose mercy is over all His works: "Judge these!" The Holy God says to them: "My soul is not satisfied with them, for they destroyed His House and killed My children," as it is said: "The fierce anger of the Lord shall not return until He has executed and performed the thoughts of His heart" <note>Jeremiah 30:24</note>. After twelve months, they bring them down to Arka <term>Arka|One of the seven layers of the earth, often associated with the place of no return</term> beneath a river of fire that goes out from under the Throne of Glory, as it is said: "Behold, the whirlwind of the Lord goes forth in fury, a settling whirlwind; it shall fall upon the head of the wicked" <note>Jeremiah 23:19</note>. From Arka, one never ascends again, as it is said: "The gods that have not made the heavens and the earth, even they shall perish from the earth (*Arka*)" <note>Jeremiah 10:11, written in Aramaic</note>. This is the fifth abyss. Above Arka is an abyss, and above that abyss is Void (*Tohu*), and above Void is Emptiness (*Bohu*), and above Emptiness is the Sea, and above the Sea is Water, and above the Water is the inhabited world (*Tebel*). Upon the inhabited world are mountains and hills and all their inhabitants, as it is said: "The mountains quaked before Me, and the hills melted." This is the sixth abyss. Above the inhabited world is an abyss, above the abyss is Void, above Void is Emptiness, above Emptiness is the Sea, and upon the Water walk man and beast, the beasts of the field, the birds of the sky, and the fish of the sea, and the Torah, and good deeds, and the fear of heaven, as it is said: "Hearken, all you inhabitants of the world (*Chaled*)." This is the seventh abyss. Above the world of *Chaled* is the *Vilon* <term>Vilon|The first and lowest of the seven heavens; literally "Curtain"</term> which enters in the evening and goes out in the morning, and renews every day the Work of Creation. Above the *Vilon* is the Heaven (*Shamayim*), as it is said: "Who stretches out the heavens like a curtain." And why are they called *Shamayim*? Because the Holy One, Blessed be He, mixed fire (*Esh*) and water (*Mayim*) and beat them together, and created with them the heavens, as it is said: "Indeed, my hand..."

# The Book of Raziel

Aria, Gaba, Anbar, Kevar, Tala, Kavyahak, Harchos, Knofiel, Noyiel, Rohoin, Shalhavin, Ashlachach, Mashchav, Giha, Ha-ata, Hagda, Hagra, Ammiel, Haglat, Laga, Menuziel, Tanimiel, Avrita, Brita, Rechal, Chashthach, Patish, Astiraz, Odfaniel, Ashnol, Malchiel, Adonai Shir, Shanoh, Hamich, Ternah, Zamchit, Hatznifa, Hatzniflahu, Vashva, Ashpor, Arq, Qnumiel, Nekiel, Gadiel, Oq, Ramimiel, Frog, Rehuniel, Zagdiel, Afninon, Dorniel, Dalphit, Hachol, Haloel, Eliel, Mot, Ochal, Peniel, Potfia, Lfot, Oranmoch, Adliel, Istorin, Azoti, Ostor, Tid, Ototit, Bergmo, Dmo, Dinua, Protal, Kumia, Danrnana, Delgiel, Pedutiel.

**And these** are the messengers of the third camp who serve Dohal: Izarchno, Anchoshal, Berahoviel, Daluviel, Azehiel, Birchatan, Beracha, Daonis, Amen, Avar, Atlegna, Otot, Ashchaziel, Ashpan, Terniel, Amfiel, Atrafaz, Uriel, Achmir, Elohin, Achal, Agaiel, Aschira, Lapael, El-Asah, Chasniel, Lemashuta, Danit, Chichas, Alphi, Amin, Argala, Magiel, Eliel.

**And these** are the known angels <term>Sovev Davla</term> <note>These specific names appear to be technical magical identifiers for a group of angels</note> and it is written upon Okir Glimu, and write each script for itself. And take a new vial and put good oil into it and cast these words into it, and stand facing the sun at the time of noon, and say: "I adjure you, O Sun who illuminates all the world, in the name of the understanding angels who contemplate hidden and sealed wisdoms and the makers of knowledge, that you perform for me my request and inform me of what will be in this year, and do not hide anything from me."

**And these** are the angels who serve in the fourth camp [under] Belmiah: Avoh, Demania, Amtai, Emunatir and Emunich, Pamavia, Toviel, Gilael, Aphiri, Gamchi, Oraniel, Priphohu, Ophai, Raq, Lemuel, Oritzi, Tumini, Omriel, Meniel, Gramto, Sarto, Tzbaqtani, Chortias, Sarsit, Parsin, Amaph, Pavchar, Hastar, Satriel, Elism, Halsiel, Strasti, Krastom, Malchiel, Ardiq, Chasdiel, Achsaf, Amonel, Partas, Gadael, Savaviel, the star Venus <note>original: *Kokhav HaNogah*</note>, Shaha, Aphrodit <note>original: *Afrorit* — a Hebrew transliteration of Aphrodite</note>, and the angels of Chasdiel.

**This fifth firmament** <note>original: *Raki'a*</note> is exalted into a precious luminary with a million clouds of glory, and it is full of angels of majesty. Within it, they are silent in fear, and their positions are in ranks upon ranks, glorified as if hewn from flame, and the sound of their running is like the roaring of the sea, and they walk in wheels of thunder. And there, twelve princes of glory sit upon thrones of splendor, and the appearance of their thrones is like the sight of fire, squared in the middle of the firmament and facing the four winds of the world. From their guard-posts go forth lightnings and flames from wings of fire, and from the radiance of their faces the firmament shines. They are appointed over the twelve months of the year, and they prepare what will be in each month. Aside from them, nothing shall happen, for they are the servants of the watch and their formation, and their standing is what maintains the months and that which shall come throughout the year.

## And These are the Names of the Twelve Princes:

**The first name** is Taphiel, and he stands over the month of Nisan.
**The second name** is Daghael, and he stands over the month of Iyar.
**The third name** is Didnaor, and he stands over the month of Sivan.
**The fourth name** is Taachnu, and he stands over the month of Tammuz.
**The fifth name** is Tochargar, and he stands over the month of Av.
**The sixth name** is Moriel, and he stands over the month of Elul.
**The seventh name** is Hehadan, and he stands over the month of Tishrei.
**The eighth name** is Yalregan, and he stands over the month of Marheshvan.
**The ninth name** is Anchananor, and he stands over the month of Kislev.
**The tenth name** is Mafniel, and he stands over the month of Tevet.
**The eleventh name** is Tashgadarnim, and he stands over the month of Shevat.
**The twelfth name** is Avrachiel, and he stands over the month of Adar.

**These are the constellations** <note>original: *mazalya* (Aramaic)</note> and these are the angels who serve with each and every one:
**The Sun** <note>original: *Chamah*</note>: Its constellation is Leo, and its angel is Raphael, and he serves on the first day of the week [Sunday].
And following him, on the 15th of Chanchal <note>A likely corruption of a month or period name</note>, Saturn/Jupiter/Mars/Sun/Venus/Mercury/Moon <note>original: *ShTzM ChaNKeL* — a common mnemonic for the seven planets</note> in the middle of the second day:
**The Moon** <note>original: *Levanah*</note>: Its constellation is Cancer, and its angel is Gabriel. These serve on the second day of the week [Monday] in the first hour.
**Tuesday** [The third day]: Mars <note>original: *Ma'adim*</note>. Its constellations are Aries and Scorpio, and its angel is Samael.
**Wednesday** [The fourth day]: Mercury <note>original: *Kokhav*</note>. Its constellations are Gemini and Virgo, and its angel is Michael.
**Thursday** [The fifth day]: Jupiter <note>original: *Tzedek*</note>. Its constellations are Sagittarius and Pisces, and its angel is Zadkiel.
**Friday** [The sixth day]: Venus <note>original: *Nogah*</note>. Its constellations are Capricorn and Aquarius <note>Note: Traditionally, Capricorn and Aquarius are ruled by Saturn, but the text associates them here with Venus/Nogah and the angel Kaphziel</note>, and its angel is Kaphziel.
**Sunday Night** [The eve of the 1st day]: Mercury. Its constellations are Capricorn and Taurus, and its angel is Michael. First hour.
**Monday Night**: Jupiter. Its constellations are Sagittarius and Pisces, and its angel is Zadkiel.
**Tuesday Night**: Venus. Its constellations are Gemini and Virgo, and its angel is Anael. First hour.
**Wednesday Night**: Saturn <note>original: *Shabbetai*</note>. Its constellation is Capricorn, and its angel is Kaphziel. First hour.
**Thursday Night**: The Sun/Mercury. Its constellation is Leo, and its angel is Raphael. First hour.
**Friday Night**: The Moon. Its constellation is Cancer, and its angel is Gabriel. First hour.
**Saturday Night** [The eve of the 7th day]: Its constellations are Aries and Scorpio, and its angel is Samael. First hour.

**And these** are the names of the angels serving in the sixth camp [under] Pashkar: Oyiel, Arbiel, Triphon, Ponchom, Pastamar, Luniel, Qroniel, Shochin, Savin, Aziel, Paniel, Chavmiel, Chammiel, Tzomiel, Nimisim.

# The Book of Raziel

**And Kanaviel** is at the head of these princes. They sing together, and songs and praises are in all their throats. He has paths among them and they have ways; one says to another: "What shall we do?" And a descending angel teaches them the "ladders of strength" <note>original: *sulammot azzah*</note> for one another, by which they ascend and by which they descend. They ascend through them to recite their praises, and they descend through them to establish peace in the world.

**The length** of the world is a journey of 503 years, and its width is a journey of 518 years. It is circular, and the Great Sea surrounds the entire world. The whole world is like a dome, and they all stand within it. The entire world stands upon the fins of the Leviathan <note>The Leviathan is depicted here as a cosmic world-bearer, a common motif in Jewish mystical cosmography</note>, and the Leviathan dwells in the lower waters—in pure, rushing waters within the sea. The lower waters rest upon the Waters of Creation <note>original: *Mi-mei Bereishit*</note> like a small spring on the shore of the sea. And the Waters of Creation stand upon the waters of the Ocean like a small spring on the shore of the sea. And the Ocean stands upon the Weeping Waters like a small spring on the shore of the sea. And why are they called "The Weeping Waters"? Because at the hour when the Holy One, Blessed be He, divided the waters, some He took above and some He placed below. The waters He placed below began to tremble and wail, crying out and lamenting in confusion, saying: "We have not merited to be near to our Creator!" What did they do? They grew bold and breached the abysses, seeking to rise to the heights until the Holy One, Blessed be He, rebuked them and subdued them beneath the soles of His feet, as it is said: "Thus says the Lord, who makes a way in the sea and a path in the mighty waters" <note>Isaiah 43:16</note>. The Holy One, Blessed be He, said to them: "Since you have done this for My sake, the Upper Waters shall have no permission to sing praise until they receive permission from you," as it is said: "More than the voices of many waters, mightier than the breakers of the sea," and afterward, "the Lord on high is mighty" <note>Psalm 93:4</note>. Do not read "the breakers of the sea" <note>original: *mishberei yam*</note> but "from the voice of the song" <note>original: *mi-kol shirah*</note> that the waters shall speak; only then do the Upper Waters take permission and say: "The Lord on high is mighty." For this reason, they are called the Weeping Waters. And the Weeping Waters are suspended and stand above the Lower Earth, and the Lower Earth is spread far over the waters, and the waters stand upon pillars of *Chashmal* <term>Chashmal: often translated as 'amber' or 'electrum,' it refers to a glowing, divine substance seen in Ezekiel's vision</term>, and those stand upon the Mountains of Liberty, and the Mountains of Liberty stand upon the treasuries of snow, and the treasuries of snow stand upon the waters, and the waters stand upon fire, and they stand upon the Abyss.

**And the Abyss** <note>original: *Tehom*</note> stands upon Chaos <note>original: *Tohu*</note>, and Chaos stands upon Void <note>original: *Bohu*</note>, and Void stands upon the Spirit, and the Spirit is suspended by the Storm and bound to the dome of *Adamah* <note>Ground/Earth</note>, and *Adamah* is suspended by the Storm and bound to the dome of *Charevah* <note>Arid Land</note>, and *Charevah* is suspended by the Storm and bound to the dome of *Yabashah* <note>Dry Land</note>, and *Yabashah* is suspended by the Storm and bound to the dome of *Arka* <note>Earth</note>, and *Arka* is suspended by the Storm and bound to the dome of *Tebel* <note>Inhabited World</note>, and *Tebel* is suspended by the Storm and bound to the dome of *Cheled* <note>Duration/World</note>, and *Cheled* is suspended by the Storm and bound to the dome of *Shamayim* <note>Heaven</note>, and *Shamayim* is suspended by the Storm and bound to the dome of *Rakia* <note>Firmament</note>, and *Rakia* is suspended by the Storm and bound to the dome of *Shechakim* <note>Skies/Clouds</note>, and *Shechakim* is suspended by the Storm and bound to the dome of *Machon* <note>Foundation</note>, and *Machon* is suspended by the Storm and bound to the dome of *Ma'on* <note>Dwelling</note>, and *Ma'on* is suspended by the Storm and bound to the dome of *Zevul* <note>Abode</note>, and *Zevul* is suspended by the Storm and bound to the dome of *Arvot* <note>Clouds/Plains</note>, and *Arvot* is suspended by the Storm and bound to the Arm of the Strength of the Holy One, Blessed be He, as it is said: "The pillars of heaven tremble and are astonished at His rebuke" <note>Job 26:11</note>. Therefore it is said: "How manifold are Your works, O Lord! In wisdom You have made them all" <note>Psalm 104:24</note>. And beyond the Lower Earth, fire and water surround it; and beyond the fire and water, gale and storm surround them; and beyond the gale and storm, those who speak the Tumult <note>original: *Omrei Hamulah*</note> surround them; and beyond the speakers of the Tumult, swaying Cherubim surround them; and beyond the swaying Cherubim, the Likeness of the Living Creatures <note>original: *Chayot*</note> surround them; and beyond the Likeness of the Living Creatures, the "Running and Returning" <note>original: *Ratzo v'Shuv*, a reference to Ezekiel 1:14</note> surround them; and beyond the Running and Returning, those who say "Holy" and those who say "Blessed" surround them.

**And in the Lower Earth**, there are the Holy Living Creatures, and the *Ofanim* <term>Ofanim: 'Wheels,' a high-ranking class of angels associated with the Divine Chariot</term>, and the Throne of Glory. The Throne of Glory is the footstool for the feet of the Master of all the earth, as it is said: "The heavens are My throne and the earth is My footstool" <note>Isaiah 66:1</note>. And I shall conclude and see the Living Creatures, and behold, a single wheel <note>original: *Ofan*</note> was upon the earth. Just as His Presence <note>original: *Shekhinah*</note> is above, so His Presence is below, as it is said: "...and there He moved the waters." There are 18,000 worlds surrounding the Lord, as it is said: "The circuit is eighteen thousand" <note>Ezekiel 48:35</note>. To the North wind, four thousand and five hundred; to the South wind, four thousand and five hundred; to the West wind, four thousand and five hundred; and to the East wind, four thousand and five hundred; and all around are 18,000 worlds. The world of the North wind is full of praise and glory, strength and might, song and melody. The world of the South wind is full of praise and glory, strength and might, song and melody, and full of majesty and splendor, rejoicing and honor, pride and eminence. The world of the East wind is full of holiness and purity, courage and righteousness, victory and might. The world of the West wind is full of beauty and ornamentation, the royal crown, and a "still small voice." And behind them, fire and water surround them; and after the fire and water are Tahariel, Nuriel, and Kanaviel. And beyond these eighteen thousand worlds, fire and water surround them; and after the fire and water, gale and storm surround them; and after the gale and storm, quaking and trembling surround them; and after the quaking and trembling, dread and fear surround them; and after the dread and fear, thunder and lightning surround them; and after the thunder and lightning, wings of wind surround them; and after the wings of wind, rivers of fire surround them; and after the rivers of fire, rivers of the sea surround them; and after the rivers of the sea, rivers of water surround them; and after the rivers of water, there is nothing but darkness without end, without number, without measure, without count, and without reckoning, as it is said: "He made darkness His secret place; His pavilion round about Him" <note>Psalm 18:11</note>. And those who surround these are Nahariel, Nuriel, and Gabriel, and the keys of the East wind are in their hands. And over the gates of the West wind, princes are appointed over them, and these are their names: Kuriel, Perael, Sachniel, Anael, and Aniel; and the keys of the West wind are in their hands. Above the Firmament of the Skies <note>original: *Rakia Shechakim*</note> are the treasuries of snow, treasuries of hail, treasuries of dew, and treasuries of manna, and the treasuries of spices for the Resurrection of the Dead, as it is said: "And in His majesty, the skies" <note>Deuteronomy 33:26</note>. And over the gates of the Skies of the North wind, princes are appointed over them, and these are their names: Panael, Peniel, Raphael, Remiel, and Yeremiel; and the keys of the North wind are in their hands. And over the South wind, princes are appointed over them, and these are their names: Peruael, Tzariel, Kiael, Shabtanael, Shemaiel, and Shemael; and the keys of the South wind are in their hands.

# The Book of Raziel

He founded the earth, and "My right hand spanned the heavens" <note>A reference to Isaiah 48:13</note>. Do not read "heavens" (*shamayim*) but rather "fire and water" (*esh u-mayim*) <note>This is a classic Hebrew wordplay: the word for heaven, *shamayim*, is seen as a compound of *esh* (fire) and *mayim* (water), representing the union of opposites in creation</note>. Above the heavens is a sea, and there is singing, and new wine, oil, and grain. When the Holy One, Blessed be He, desires to bless the earth, He brings down dew and rain in their seasons. Above the sea is water, and above the water is the firmament. Between the water and the firmament are suspended sparks of dew, the orbit of the sun, the moon, the stars, and the constellations, which sustain the whole world and rule over every single deep. Their light descends to the lower earth, as it is said: "And God set them..." <note>Genesis 1:17</note>. Above the sea, waters surround the sea; sparks of clarity and rages <note>original: *za'amim*, likely referring to stormy or turbulent spiritual forces</note> drip upon them all, and thin walls of fire encompass them, as it is said: "The heavens and the heaven of heavens." Upon the gates of the heavens of the North Wind, princes are appointed, and these are their names: **Al, El, Al-Bam, Belial, Belezal,** and **Netzaliel**; and the keys of the North Wind are in their hands. Upon the gates of the South Wind, princes are appointed over them, and these are their names: **Dadniel, Darchiel, Hamin, Daniel,** and **Gehenniel**; and the keys of the South Wind are in their hands. Upon the gates of the East Wind, those appointed over them are named: **Duriel, Gabriel, Gadriel, Adriel, Muriel, Rahmiel,** and **Hanniel**; and the keys of the East Wind are in their hands. Upon the gates of the West Wind, princes are appointed over them, and these are their names: **Al-Tzin, Sukiel, Gahsiel, Bachiel, Baqsamiel,** and **Tarfaniel**; and the keys of the West Wind are in their hands. Above the firmament of the heavens, in which the sun, moon, and constellations are fixed, the "wings" of the firmament hint at a sort of dome. The sun, moon, stars, constellations, and the wings of the firmaments rule over every single deep, and their light descends to the lower earth, as it is said: "And God placed them in the firmament of the heaven," etc. Upon the gates of the North Wind—the firmament of the North Wind—princes are appointed over them, and these are the names: **Tiel, Yarel, Yanel, Ganel, Halakim,** and **Hanniel**; and the keys of the North Wind are in their hands. Upon the gates of the South Wind, princes are appointed over them, and these are their names: **Kol A-H, Bachliel, Balhiel, Loel, Liel,** and **Naqriel**; and the keys of the South Wind are in their hands. Upon the gates of the East Wind, princes are appointed over them, and these are their names: **MSM, Carmiel,** and it was sealed with the ring of **I Am That I Am** <note>original: *Ehyeh Asher Ehyeh*, the divine name revealed to Moses at the burning bush</note>. According to this, it was sealed with His seal, for if it were not sealed with His seal, no creature would be able to seek out its letters because of the flame of fire. Anyone who came to open and seek out its letters would immediately be filled with fire; therefore, He closed its letters and sealed them with His seal. Thus it is said, "In the beginning" (*Bereishit*). What is *Bereishit*? In the beginning, He created heaven and earth; He gave dwellings and brought forth deeps. He established seven dwellings above, and corresponding to them, seven deeps below. He stretched out the **Heavens** above; corresponding to it, He founded the **Earth** below. He planted the **Skies** (*Shachaq*) above; corresponding to it, He founded the **Dry Land** (*Yabashah*) below. He stretched out the **Habitation** (*Me'on*) above; corresponding to it, He founded the **Land of Arqa** below. He stretched out the **Residence** (*Zevul*) above; corresponding to it, He founded the **World** (*Tevel*) below. He stretched out the **Clouds** (*Aravot*) above; corresponding to it, He founded the **World** below <note>The text repeats *Tevel* (World) as the lower counterpart for both *Zevul* and *Aravot*</note>. He established His **Throne** above, and from His glory, He stretched it below. These are the works of the First Day.

He divided the waters; some He took below and some He took above, as it is said: "And God made the firmament and divided the waters."
He gathered the waters into one place and showed the dry land and called it Earth. He brought forth from it trees and herbs and all kinds of seeds, and fruit-bearing trees and all kinds of plantings with their trunks and their fruits, as it is said: "And the earth brought forth grass, herb yielding seed after its kind," etc.

On the Fourth

He created lights to divide between the day and the night. He gave the sun to rule by day and the moon and stars to rule by night, as it is said: "And God made the two great lights."

He created fish and all the swarming things of the water and the birds that are upon the face of the earth, as it is said: "Let the waters swarm with swarms of living creatures and let birds fly over the earth," etc.
He created cattle and beasts and creeping things, as it is said: "Let the earth bring forth living creatures after their kind." After everything, He created **Man** to rule over them all. He prepared His hosts, and to each and every one He commanded his service. A great angel is at the head of them all. Whirlwind and storm surround them; they say "Holy" and they say "Blessed," as it is said: "And His praise filled the earth." This is the lowest deep.

And Above

Above the deep is **Tohu** (Chaos); above Tohu is **Bohu** (Void); above Bohu is the **Sea**; above the sea is **Water**. The water flows as a debt <note>original: *chovah*, possibly meaning a boundary or fixed law</note>, and upon the debt are rivers and the plantings of the deep, as it is said: "And I will make the rivers dry" <note>Ezekiel 30:12</note>. This is the third deep. Above the Waste (*Churvah*) is a deep; above the deep is Tohu; above Tohu is Bohu; above Bohu is the sea; above the sea is water; and upon the water is dry land. And the dry land flows into seas and rivers and every gathering of water, as it is said: "The sea is His, for He made it, and His hands formed the dry land." This is the fourth deep. Above the dry land is a deep; above the deep is Tohu; above Tohu is Bohu; above Bohu is the sea; above the sea is water; and upon the water is **Arqa** <note>The Aramaic name for one of the seven earths</note>. And upon Arqa is the **Lower Sheol**, and **Abaddon**, and the **Pit of Destruction**, and the **Miry Clay**, and the **Gates of Death**, and the **Gates of the Shadow of Death**, and **Gehenna**. The wicked are within them, and angels of destruction are appointed over them.

<meta>This page continues the cosmographical survey from the previous page, detailing the celestial hierarchy of song-leading angels and the vertical architecture of the universe—from the lowest depths of the Abyss to the highest layers of the Heavens.</meta>

# And Kenaviel
He is like the head of these princes, and music and two-fold song and praises are in the throats of all of them. He possesses paths among them, and they have ways of aiding one another, saying "What shall we do?" <note>The Hebrew *asadin* is likely a variant of *ozrin*, meaning helping or supporting each other in their liturgical duties.</note> The Angel of the LORD teaches them **ladders** <term>sulamot: spiritual conduits or steps</term> for one another, upon which they ascend and descend; they ascend upon them to speak His praise, and they descend upon them to establish peace in the world.

The **length** of the world is a journey of five hundred and two years, and its width is a journey of four hundred and eighteen years. It is circular, and the **Great Sea** <note>The Mediterranean or the Primordial Ocean</note> surrounds the entire world. The whole world is like a dome, and it all stands upon the fins of **Leviathan** <note>The legendary sea creature who, in this tradition, acts as the structural foundation of the earth</note>. Leviathan dwells in the Lower Waters, in pure waters within the sea. The Lower Waters rest upon the Primordial Waters like a small spring on the seashore. The Primordial Waters rest upon the waters of **Oceanus** like a small spring on its shore. Oceanus rests upon the **Weeping Waters** like a small spring on the seashore. 

And why are they called the "Weeping Waters"? Because at the hour when the Holy One, Blessed be He, divided the waters, He took some above and gave some below. The waters He placed below began to tremble, wail, scream, tear at themselves, and weep. They said: "We have not merited to be close to our Creator!" What did they do? They grew bold and split the depths, seeking to rise to the heights until the Holy One, Blessed be He, rebuked them and subdued them under the soles of His feet. Concerning this it is said: "The LORD says, who makes a way in the sea and a path in the mighty waters" <note>Isaiah 43:16</note>. The Holy One, Blessed be He, said to them: "Since you have done this for My sake, the Upper Waters have no permission to sing praise until they receive permission from you." As it is said: "Above the voices of many waters, mighty are the breakers of the sea," and only afterward, "Mighty on high is the LORD" <note>Psalm 93:4. The text suggests a wordplay: do not read *mishberei* (breakers) but rather *mishirei* (from the songs), implying the music of the lower waters must come first.</note>. Therefore they are called the Weeping Waters. These Weeping Waters are suspended and stand above the Lower Earth, and the Lower Earth is distant upon the waters. The waters stand upon pillars of **Chashmal** <term>electrum: a glowing, amber-like spiritual substance seen in Ezekiel's vision</term>, which stand upon the Mountains of **Deror** <note>Freedom or Flowing Mountains</note>. The Mountains of Deror stand upon storehouses of snow, the storehouses of snow stand upon water, the water upon fire, and these stand upon the Abyss.

# And the Abyss
It stands upon Void (*Tohu*), and Void stands upon Chaos (*Bohu*), and Chaos stands upon the Spirit, and the Spirit is suspended in the Storm, and it is bound to the dome of **Adamah** (Earth). And *Adamah* is suspended in the Storm, and it is bound to the dome of **Charabah** (Arid Land). And *Charabah* is suspended in the Storm, and it is bound to the dome of **Yabashah** (Dry Land). And *Yabashah* is suspended in the Storm, and it is bound to the dome of **Arka** (Earth). And *Arka* is suspended in the Storm, and it is bound to the dome of **Tebel** (World). And *Tebel* is suspended in the Storm, and it is bound to the dome of **Cheled** (Life/Duration). And *Cheled* is suspended in the Storm, and it is bound to the dome of the **Heavens** (*Shamayim*). And the Heavens are suspended in the Storm, and they are bound to the dome of the **Firmament** (*Raquia*). And the Firmament is suspended in the Storm, and it is bound to the dome of the **Skies** (*Shachakim*). And the Skies are suspended in the Storm, and they are bound to the dome of the **Residence** (*Makon*). And the Residence is suspended in the Storm, and it is bound to the dome of the **Dwelling** (*Ma'on*). And the Dwelling is suspended in the Storm, and it is bound to the dome of the **Temple** (*Zevul*). And the Temple is suspended in the Storm, and it is bound to the dome of the **Clouds** (*Aravot*). And the Clouds are suspended in the Storm, and it is bound to the **Arm of Strength** of the Holy One, Blessed be He, as it is said: "The pillars of heaven tremble and are astonished at His rebuke" <note>Job 26:11</note>. Concerning this it is said: "How manifold are Your works, O LORD! In wisdom You have made them all" <note>Psalm 104:24</note>. Beyond the Lower Earth, fire and water encompass it; beyond the fire and water, a tempest and storm encompass it; beyond the tempest and storm, speakers of tumult encompass it; beyond the speakers of tumult, fluttering Cherubim encompass it; beyond the fluttering Cherubim, the likeness of Living Creatures encompass it; beyond the likeness of Living Creatures, "running and returning" <note>Ezekiel 1:14</note> encompass it; and beyond the running and returning, those who say "Holy" and those who say "Blessed" encompass it.

# And in the Earth
The Lower Earth contains the Holy Living Creatures, the Wheels (*Ophanim*), and the **Throne of Glory**. The Throne of Glory is the footstool for the feet of the Master of all the earth, as it is said: "The heavens are My throne and the earth is My footstool" <note>Isaiah 66:1</note>. It is also said: "And I saw the Living Creatures, and behold, one wheel was on the earth." Just as His Presence (*Shekhinah*) is above, so His Presence is below. There are waters there, and eighteen thousand worlds surround the LORD, as it is said: "The circumference shall be eighteen thousand" <note>Ezekiel 48:35</note>. For the North wind, four thousand five hundred; for the South wind, four thousand five hundred; for the West wind, four thousand five hundred; and for the East wind, four thousand five hundred—surrounding eighteen thousand worlds.

The world of the North wind is full of praise, glory, strength, might, acclaim, and song. The world of the South wind is full of praise, glory, strength, might, acclaim, song, and full of majesty, splendor, rejoicing, honor, pride, and loftiness. The world of the East wind is full of holiness, purity, power, righteousness, eternity, and might. The world of the West wind is full of beauty, a crown, a royal diadem, and "a still, small voice."

Behind them, fire and water encompass them, and after the fire and water is the Awesome God, thirty <note>original: *l'*, possibly a numerical notation or shorthand</note> like **Kenaviel**. Beyond these eighteen worlds, fire and water encompass them; beyond the fire and water, a tempest and storm encompass them; beyond the tempest and storm, quaking and trembling encompass them; beyond the quaking and trembling, dread and fear encompass them; beyond the dread and fear, thunder and lightning encompass them; beyond the thunder and lightning, wings of the wind encompass them; beyond the wings of the wind, rivers of fire encompass them; beyond the rivers of fire, rivers of the sea encompass them; beyond the rivers of water... there is nothing but **endless darkness**, without number, without measure, without count, and without reckoning, as it is said: "He made darkness His secret place; His pavilion round about Him" <note>Psalm 18:11</note>.

Those who encompass these are **Nohariel, Noariel,** and **Gabriel**, and the keys to the East wind are in their hands. Over the gates of the West wind, princes are appointed over them, and these are their names: **Kuriel, Perael, Sachniel, Anael,** and **Aniel**; and the keys to the West wind are in their hands. Above the Firmament are the **Skies** (*Shachakim*); there are the storehouses of snow, storehouses of hail, storehouses of dew, and the storehouses of rain for the **Resurrection of the Dead**, as it is said: "And in His majesty, the skies." Upon the gates of the Skies of the North wind, princes are appointed over them, and these are their names: **Panael, Paniel, Raphael,** and **Ramiel**; and the keys of the North wind are in their hands. Over the South wind, princes are appointed over them, and these are their names: **Peruael, Tzariel, Kial, Shabtaniael, Shemiel,** and **Shemael**; and the keys of the South wind are in their hands.
