# The Book of Raziel

"He founded the earth, and My right hand spanned the heavens." Do not read it as *Shamayim* (heavens) but rather as *Esh u-Mayim* <note>original: אש ומים; a traditional wordplay suggesting the heavens are composed of Fire and Water</note>. Above the heavens is a sea, and there are Joy, Innovation, and Brightness. When the Holy One, Blessed be He, desires to bless the earth, He sends down dew and rain in their seasons; and upon the sea, water; and upon the water, a firmament. Between the water and the firmament hang sparks of fire, the rods of the solar wheel, the moon, the stars, and the constellations that sustain the whole world and rule over every abyss. Their light descends to the lower earth, as it is said: "And God set them [in the firmament of the heaven...]" (Genesis 1:17). Above the sea, waters surround the sea with sparks without end, and rages <note>likely referring to storms or celestial disturbances</note> drizzle upon them all. Thin walls of fire encompass them, as it is said: "The heaven and the heaven of heavens." 

At the gates of the North Wind, princes are appointed, and these are their names: **El A'L, El B'M, Bel'iel, Bilqi-El,** and **Netzli'el**; and the keys to the North Wind are in their hands. 

At the gates of the South Wind, princes are appointed over them, and these are their names: **Dadni'el, Darbi'el, Hamin, Dani'el,** and **Gihani'el**; and the keys to the South Wind are in their hands. 

At the gates of the East Wind, princes are appointed over them, and these are their names: **Dorni'el, Gabri'el, Gadri'el, Adri'el, Mori'el, Rahmi'el,** and **Hani'el**; and the keys to the East Wind are in their hands. 

At the gates of the West Wind, princes are appointed over them, and these are their names: **El Tzin, Soki'el, Gani'el, Bakhi'el, Baqsma'el,** and **Tarfni'el**; and the keys to the West Wind are in their hands. 

Above the firmament of the heavens, in which the sun, moon, and constellations are fixed, the wings of the firmament are lofty like a dome. The sun, moon, stars, constellations, and the wings of the firmaments rule over every abyss, and their light descends to the lower earth, as it is said: "And God set them in the firmament of the heaven," and so on. At the gates of the North Wind—the firmament of the North Wind—princes are appointed over them, and these are their names: **Ti'el, Yar'el, Yan'el, Gan'el, Halqi'um,** and **Hani'el**; and the keys of the North Wind are in their hands. 

At the gates of the South Wind, princes are appointed over them, and these are their names: **Kol A'H, Bahli'el, Bilhi'el, Lu'el, Li'el,** and **Naqri'el**; and the keys of the South Wind are in their hands. 

At the gates of the East Wind, princes are appointed over them, and these are their names: **Masim** and **Karmi'el**. 

He sealed [creation] with the ring of <term>I Am That I Am: Ehyeh Asher Ehyeh, the sacred name of God revealed to Moses</term>, and with this countenance He sealed it with His seal. For if He had not sealed it with His seal, no creature could seek out its signs because of the flaming fire; whoever would come to open and seek its signs would immediately be filled with fire. Therefore, He closed its signs and sealed them with His seal. Thus it is said, "In the beginning"—what is "In the beginning"? First, He created heaven and earth; He gave dwellings and brought forth abysses. He established seven springs above, and corresponding to them below, seven abysses. He stretched the Heavens above; corresponding to them, He founded the Earth below. He planted the Sky (*Shachaq*) above; corresponding to it, He founded the Dry Land (*Yabashah*) below. He stretched the Dwelling (*Ma'on*) above; corresponding to it, He founded *Arqa* <note>An ancient Aramaic name for one of the seven layers of the earth</note> below. He stretched the Residence (*Zevul*) above; corresponding to it, He founded the World (*Tebel*) below. He stretched the Clouds (*Arabot*) above; corresponding to them, He founded the World (*Tebel*) below. His Throne is established—

->Above, and from His glory He stretched below; these are the works of the first day.<-

---

<margin>And on the second</margin> He divided the waters; some He took below and some He took above, as it is said: "And God made the firmament, and divided the waters" (Genesis 1:7).

<margin>On the third</margin> He gathered the waters into one place and revealed the dry land and called it Earth. He brought forth from it trees, herbs, all kinds of seeds, fruit-bearing trees, and all kinds of blossoms and coverings and frosts, as it is said: "Let the earth bring forth grass, the herb yielding seed after his kind," and so on.

<margin>On the fourth</margin> He created luminaries to divide the day from the night. He gave the sun to rule by day and the moon and stars to rule by night, as it is said: 
->And God made two great luminaries.<-

<margin>On the fifth</margin> He created fish and all creeping things of the water, and the birds that are upon the face of the earth, as it is said: "Let the waters bring forth abundantly the moving creature that hath life, and fowl that may fly above the earth," and so on.

<margin>On the sixth</margin> He created beasts, animals, and creeping things, as it is said: "Let the earth bring forth the living creature after his kind." After everything, He created Man to rule over them all. He prepared His hosts, and to each one He commanded its service. A great angel is at the head of them all. Whirlwind and storm surround them; they say "Holy," and they say "Blessed," as it is said: "His glory filled the earth" (Habakkuk 3:3)—this is the bottommost abyss.

---

<margin>And above</margin> the abyss is *Tohu* (Chaos), and above *Tohu* is *Bohu* (Void). Above *Bohu* is the sea. Above the sea is water, and the water flows to the wasteland, and upon the wasteland are rivers and the plantings of the abyss, as it is said: "I will make the rivers a dry land" (Isaiah 19:5-6). This is the third abyss. Above the wasteland is an abyss; above the abyss is *Tohu*; above *Tohu* is *Bohu*; above *Bohu* is the sea; above the sea is water. Upon the water is dry land, and the dry land flows into seas and rivers and every gathering of water, as it is said: "The sea is His, and He made it: and His hands formed the dry land" (Psalm 95:5). This is the fourth abyss. Above the dry land is an abyss; above the abyss is *Tohu*; above *Tohu* is *Bohu*; above *Bohu* is the sea; above the sea is water. Upon the water is *Arqa*, and upon *Arqa* are the Lowest Sheol, *Abaddon* (Destruction), the Pit of Corruption (*Beer Shachat*), the Miry Clay (*Tit ha-Yaven*), the Gates of Death, the Gates of the Shadow of Death, and Gehenna. The wicked are within them, and the Angels of Destruction are appointed over them.

The fire of the Lower Sheol is sixty-one times <note>The Hebrew "ס"א" stands for the number 61</note> stronger than the fire of Abaddon. The fire of Abaddon is sixty-one times stronger than the fire of the Pit of Corruption. The fire of the Pit of Corruption is sixty-one times stronger than the fire of the Miry Clay. The fire of the Miry Clay is sixty-one times stronger than the fire of the Gates of Death. The fire of the Gates of Death is sixty-one times stronger than the fire of the Gates of the Shadow of Death. The fire of the Shadow of Death is sixty-one times stronger than the fire of Gehenna.

<meta>This page continues the mystical exploration of the divine realm, transitioning from the celestial architecture of the previous page to the "Shi'ur Qomah"—the esoteric measurements of the Divine Body and the Throne of Glory. It describes the proportions of the Creator, the guardians of the Throne, and the role of the angel Metatron in the heavenly liturgy.</meta>

A cubit <note>original: אמה (amah); a biblical unit of measurement roughly equal to the length of a forearm</note>, and every single cubit is two spans in His span. His span fills the entire world, as it is said: "Who has measured the waters in the hollow of his hand, and meted out heaven with a span" (Isaiah 40:12).

## Rabbi Nathan
Rabbi Nathan, the student of Rabbi Ishmael, said: Even the nose was given a measurement for the right side and a measurement for the left. So too for the lips. And even though he gave me the measurement of the forehead, he gave me the measurement of every cubit upon it. The width of the forehead is like the height of the neck, and so is the shoulder.

**In the length of the nose:** The length of the nose is like the length of the pinky finger. The height of the cheeks is like half the circle of the head, and such is the measure of every human. **His lips are thrones:** My upper lip is fifteen thousand [units] high, and its name is **A-kh-d-r-a**; and the lower lip, **Hor-ni** is its name. His mouth is a consuming fire, a fire that speaks. **Akdar’a** is his name, and whoever wishes to stand before Him must be moderate. The crown upon His head is five hundred thousand by five hundred thousand [parasangs], and **Z-sh** is its name. The precious stone between His horns, **Y-sh** is its name. With Him is **El**, and the essence of **El** is engraved upon it. "My beloved is white and ruddy, preeminent among ten thousand; His head is as the most fine gold, his locks are wavy," and so on (Song of Songs 5:10-11). His eyes are like doves by the rivers of water; His cheeks are like a bed of spices, two thousand myriads of parasangs <note>a parasang is an ancient Persian unit of distance, roughly 3-4 miles; here it refers to cosmic, divine distances</note>. Anyone who does not seal <note>meaning to conclude a prayer or meditation</note> with this verse is considered negligent.

## His Cheeks
...are as a bed of spices, as towers of perfumes; his lips are like lilies, dropping flowing myrrh. His hands are as rings of gold set with beryl; his body is as ivory work overlaid with sapphires. His legs are as pillars of marble, set upon bases of fine gold; his aspect is like Lebanon, excellent as the cedars. His mouth is most sweet; yea, he is altogether lovely. This is my beloved, and this is my friend, O daughters of Jerusalem (Song of Songs 5:13-16). **A-g-t-h Tz-ch-z-v-n Y-ch-z-n**, Good, Pure, **Yod Yod Yod**, He shall be mighty.

## Mighty
Holy, holy, holy is the Lord of Hosts; the whole earth is full of His glory.

## His Eyebrows
...are according to the measure of His eyes. The right one is named **Ha-dar Ve-lar**, and the left is named **Afrah Tzatziyahu**. The height of His ears is like the height of His forehead; the right is named **Atztah’ya**, and the left is named **Munnogahu Tzatziya**. Thus, the entire measurement is myriads upon myriads of thousands of parasangs in height, and a thousand thousand myriads of parasangs in width.

## Said
Rabbi Ishmael: When I spoke these words before Rabbi Akiva, he said to me: "Anyone who knows these measurements of our Creator and the praise of the Holy One, Blessed be He, which is hidden from the creatures, is assured that he is a son of the World to Come. It shall go well for him in this world as in the goodness of the World to Come, and he shall live long days in this world."

## Said
Rabbi Ishmael to his students: Rabbi Akiva and I are guarantors in this matter—that anyone who knows this measurement of our Creator and the praise of the Holy One, Blessed be He, is assured of being a son of the World to Come, provided that he recites it as a Mishnah <note>meaning a repetitive, structured study or oral teaching</note> every single day. His stature fills the Throne of Fire. **R-t B-g B-g N-b H-y-m-g** is His name. His locks and His stature, **D-b-r B-r R-z-g** is His name, and they have half a... **G-l Sh-r-b** is His name. One eye of His sees from one end of the world to the other, **Akfastat** is her name, and the sparks that emerge from it provide light to all creatures. His other eye sees behind Him what is destined to be, **Atnansat** is her name. His body is like a bow, and the bow is like **Qas-shiya** its name; half of it is **M-n K-m-tz** its name. His sword is **Matzmatzit Matza** its name. His Throne of Glory is **Dorban Pirota** its name. The place of His seat is **Dorpaz Pirota** etc. is its name. Likewise, the "Treasures" <note>original: כנזיו (kenazav); likely referring to hidden storehouses or garments</note> and the feet of His glory are the *Chayot* <note>the "Holy Living Creatures" described in Ezekiel’s vision of the Chariot</note>.

The *Chayot* that stand beneath Him: The first leg of the Throne, which is a *Chayah*, its calf is named **Chatzbiya**. The second leg of the Throne, which is a *Chayah*, is named **Kabkab Plavim Mi**. The third leg, which is a *Chayah*, is named **Kakab Algay**. The fourth leg, which is a *Chayah*, is named **Atzbiya Buvaz**.

And the likeness of their faces: the nature of a Lion, the form of an Eagle, the likeness of an Ox, and the face of a Man—hidden. There are four faces to each one, four faces to a corner, and four faces to every single corner; sixty-four faces for every single *Chayah*. There are four faces and wings for each, and four wings for every wing, and four wings for every single face; sixty-four wings for every single *Chayah*. 

The Prince of the Human Face is **El-h Amtzab Amtz Amtm Netz**.
The Prince of the Lion Face is **Hodu Diya Hidu’ah El Uria Hod Hawa Tamnamatz**.
The Prince of the Ox Face is **Shor Eleh Tzamtzamba Maskna**.
The Prince of the Eagle Face is **Affi Eliyah Mamtzat Tzohoririel**. 

When Israel sinned, the face of the Ox was hidden, and He brought a Cherub in its place <note>traditional midrash explains that the Ox was replaced because it reminded God of the Sin of the Golden Calf</note>. The Prince of the Cherub Face is **Tamtam-nali-yah Kerubya Kerubah Patpasit Patzpatziya Han-qaya**. These are the ones who say "Holy," and these are the ones who say "Blessed," as it is said: "He declares His words to Jacob." Anyone who does not seal <note>finish the meditation</note> in the Book of Genesis, in the Order of the Work of Creation, is considered negligent regarding the Beauty of the Holy One, Blessed be He.

His Beauty fills all. Troops of thunder and rage are at His right, and an aid of rage is at His left. The signs of rage are before Him, and within the signs are radiance, darkness, cloud, thick gloom, and miry clay. Before Him is a field of stars. Between star and star is a source of lightning; between lightning and lightning is the opening of the *Chashmal* <note>often translated as "amber" or "electrum," referring to the glowing substance in Ezekiel's vision</note>. From above are spirits, thunders, voices, lightnings, and the bowls of the rainbow. The cords of the seal rise and fall upon the Holy One, Blessed be He. Grace, kindness, glory, mercy, splendor, crown, majesty, beauty, and pride are His.

And the secret of the Holy One, Blessed be He, and His name is **Metatron**. They say: "Mighty and Powerful," and they say: "Holy and Blessed," and they say: "The Tumult." They come before Him and stand before Him. The Youth <note>original: הנער (ha-na'ar); a common title for the angel Metatron in Hekhalot literature</note>, who is Metatron, comes and bows before the Holy One, Blessed be He. **A-H-H** is his name. He praises and glorifies and says: "Blessed be the glory of the Lord from His place" (Ezekiel 3:12). **Y-h-v D-h-v D-h-v Y-v-h-v Y-h-v-y-h**... "Blessed be the glory of the Lord from His place," and so on. They repeat after him: "Blessed be the glory of the Lord from His place" and "Blessed be the name of His glorious kingdom forever and ever."

He enters before Him beneath the Throne of Glory. To our right walk houses of fire and stones of hail and a wall of rage. To His left walk wings like a storm and the power of a Scribe. When Metatron enters before the Holy One, Blessed be He, beneath the Throne of Glory, He strengthens him with many wings. All the Ministering Angels come before the Holy One, Blessed be He, and say: "The great, mighty, and awesome God..."

<meta>catchword: ...who praise</meta>

# The Book of Raziel

You are the one who performs wonders, You are God alone. You are the Lord alone. You made the heavens, the highest heavens and all their host, the earth and all that is upon them. They shall thank You, O Lord, God of the first and the last, for You, Lord, are the first of all firsts and the last of all lasts, <insert>Lord of Hosts</insert>. You are mighty in strength and great to save. O Lord of Hosts, happy is the person who trusts in You.

**Rabbi Ishmael said:** I saw the King of Kings <note>The Hebrew abbreviation מ"מה refers to "Melech Malchei HaMelachim," the King of the Kings of Kings</note> sitting upon a high and lofty throne, and His hosts were standing before Him to His right and to His left. The Angel, the Prince of the Countenance, whose name is <insert>Metatron</insert>, spoke to me: "Spirit of Fiskonia, Paskonia, Achtamon, Hygron, Sigron, Sarton, Sanigron, Mikon, <insert>The Sum</insert>, Katas, <insert>The Early</insert>, Hakidon, Na Dokidon, Zina, Raba, Nantosh, and Natpar Hakipas." <note>These are secret, mystical names of the angel or the divine presence, intended for liturgical or magical use</note>

Rabbi Ishmael says: What is the measure of the Holy One, blessed be He, who is hidden from all creatures? The soles of (other version: the palms of) His feet fill the entire world, as it is said: "The heavens are My throne and the earth is My footstool" (Isaiah 66:1). The height of His soles (other version: so high) is thirty thousand times thousands of <term>parasangs</term> <note>original: פרסאות (parasangs); a Persian unit of distance, roughly 3 to 4 miles. In this context, they represent cosmic, infinite distances</note>. The sole of His right foot is forty-one thousand [parasangs]—**Atrakt** is its name; and the left is **Agmon** by name. From the sole of the foot to the ankles is one thousand times ten thousand and five hundred parasangs in height, and likewise for the left. The ankle of the right is <insert>Tetsnamatnah</insert>, <insert>Tishshakam</insert> is the name of the left. <insert>Astaman</insert> is its name. From the ankles to the knees is nineteen thousand and four parasangs in height, and likewise for the left. The leg of the left is <insert>Kangagi Machraz Tasakim</insert> by name, and likewise for the right—<insert>Mamna Vayya</insert> is its name. From the knees to the thighs is twelve times ten thousand and one thousand and four parasangs in height, and likewise for the left <insert>Maghabo Hariz</insert> is its name. The thigh of the right is <insert>Sheshfusat Parsab</insert> by name, and the left is <insert>Tafgat Chazazya</insert> by name. From the thighs to the neck is twenty-four thousand times ten thousand parasangs. And the loins <insert>Motanyahu Atmanah Yedidya</insert> is the name. And upon His heart are written seventy names:

# The Clouds, YH HU VH, MM NNN, HUU YH, Desired, Strong, AI ZA, Tze'ah, AEA, QQQ, Qishro, Pure, Mighty, YA

<insert>The neck</insert> is thirteen thousand times ten thousand and four hundred and eight parasangs in the height of the neck—<insert>Sananyahu Ubhatikan</insert> is the name. The circle of the head is three thousand times ten thousand and thirty-three and a third parasangs—that which the mouth cannot speak and the ear cannot hear—<insert>Atar Hodarya</insert> and <insert>Atasyah Atatyah</insert> are its names. The beard is ten thousand and one thousand and five hundred parasangs—<insert>Hadrak Samya</insert> is its name. And the appearance of the face is like the appearance of torches, like the likeness of spirit and in the form of a soul. No creature is able to mention His body, which is like **Tarshish** <note>A precious gem, often identified as chrysolite or beryl</note>; this is the elevation of His radiance from within the darkness, and clouds and thick darkness surround Him. All the Princes of the Countenance and the Seraphim are like small gnats compared to Him. We have no measure in our hands, only the names revealed to us—<insert>HaChotaf Lagbatya</insert> is the name, <insert>Abrang</insert> and <insert>Tatfayya</insert> are the names. His tongue [reaches] from one end of the world to the other, as it is said, "He declares His words to Jacob" (Psalm 147:19). And it is said: anyone who does not know how to seal this verse is in error, as it says, "He declares His words to Jacob"—<insert>Assagdayahu Ve'iza</insert> is the name. <margin>The black</margin> of His right eye is ten thousand and one thousand and five hundred parasangs, and likewise for the left. And likewise for the right <insert>Odarya Atatyos</insert> is its name and Chashar is its name, **Rachmiel**. And upon <insert>Shehuel Metat Grufmatzya</insert> is its name, and the sparks that go out from Him provide light to all creatures.

<margin>The white</margin> of His right eye is twenty-two times ten thousand and two parasangs, and likewise for the left, and its name is <insert>Bazaqucha</insert>. From His right shoulder to His left shoulder is sixteen thousand times ten thousand parasangs. The right shoulder is named <insert>Metatgarya'a An-gan</insert> and the name of the left is <insert>Tatmahnata</insert>, and He has one name: <insert>Shelomo Yagniel</insert>. From His right arm to His left arm is twelve times ten thousand parasangs. His arms are doubled. The right arm is named <insert>Gevrahozya Abbovay</insert> and the left is named <insert>Metatnechatzayahahu</insert>. The fingers of His hands are fifteen thousand times ten thousand parasangs for each and every finger, and likewise for the left. Those of the right are <insert>Tatmatzmin Gagmoth Gagshamash</insert> and those of the left are <insert>Titz Maq Tatmat Agmatz Agmat</insert> and roses, and thus you count from the thumb onward. The palms of His hands are four thousand times ten thousand parasangs, and likewise for the left. The right is <insert>Chazvya Atagri</insert> and the left is <insert>Ashhahozych</insert>. The toes of His feet on the right are ten thousand times ten thousand parasangs for each and every toe, and likewise for the left. Those of the right are <insert>Atarmin Adarmin Barmanas</insert> <insert>Bartahmim Ve'achuz</insert> and those of the left are <insert>Zavyayn Kovayn Hatmat Ahazu</insert>. As with the hands, you count them as you go. Therefore the God is called Great, Mighty, and Awesome, as it is said: "For the Lord your God is God of gods and Lord of lords, the great, the mighty, and the awesome God" (Deuteronomy 10:17).

# The Book of Raziel

Who is this King of Glory? The Lord, strong and mighty, the Lord, mighty in battle. Lift up your heads, O gates; be lifted up, you ancient doors, that the King of Glory may come in. Who is He, this King of Glory? The Lord of Hosts—He is the King of Glory. Selah. <note>original: Psalms 24:8-10</note>

**You** are the Lord alone. You made the heavens, the highest heavens and all their host, the earth and all that is upon it, the seas and all that is in them, and You give life to them all; the host of heaven bows down to You. □ You are the Lord God who chose Abram and brought him out of Ur of the Chaldeans and gave him the name Abraham. □ You found his heart faithful before You, and made a covenant with him to give the land of the Canaanites, Hittites, Amorites, Perizzites, Jebusites, and Girgashites—to give it to his offspring; and You have kept Your promise, for You are righteous. * Yours, O Lord, is the greatness and the power, the glory, the victory, and the majesty, for everything in the heavens and on earth is Yours. Yours, O Lord, is the kingdom, and You are exalted as head over all. You rule over everything; in Your hand is power and might to make great and to give strength to all. And now, our God, we thank You and praise Your glorious name. May Your glorious name be blessed and exalted above all blessing and praise. He preserves kindness for thousands, forgiving iniquity, rebellion, and sin, and He cleanses. * O Lord God, Almighty <term>Shaddai</term> of Hosts, Yah, Ah-Heh, Yah * Yah, I Shall Be What I Shall Be <note>original: אהיה אשר אהיה (Ehyeh Asher Ehyeh); the divine name revealed to Moses at the burning bush</note>, Jehovah, Jehovah, God, merciful and gracious, slow to anger and abounding in kindness and truth. Let Your power be great, I pray, and show me mercy; and Your anger, Your wrath, Your fury, and Your indignation—distance them from me, [So-and-so] <note>original: פלוני (Peloni); a traditional placeholder for the user to insert their own name</note>, Your servant, son of [So-and-so] Your handmaid, and from all that is mine, and from all my household, from Your dwelling, from Your light, and from Your inheritance. May Your mercy, O Lord, be upon us, and may You have pity and compassion on us and on Your treasured people. Let everyone say: "The Lord is God!" And You shall cast down before You those who dwell in the heavens. * He who dwells in the highest heavens, who resides in [So-and-so], who rides upon the clouds <term>Arabot</term> <note>original: ערבות (Arabot); the seventh and highest heaven in Jewish cosmology</note>, who sits in the lofty habitation * who dwells in the celestial residence, who sits in the skies, who sits upon the Throne of Mercy.

In the seventh firmament, all is light sevenfold, and from its radiance and brightness all the dwellings are illuminated. In it stands the Throne of Glory, set upon four Creatures of Glory <term>Chayyot</term> <note>original: חיות (Chayyot); the 'Holy Living Creatures' described in Ezekiel's vision</note>. Within it are the treasures of life and the treasures of souls. There is no searching or end to the great light within it, and from the abundance of its light, the earth is illuminated. Angels are held by pillars of light, and their radiance is like the light of brilliance and flame; their eyes are like sparks of lightning. Their station is upon the lights before the One who sits on the high and lofty throne, for He is alone and there is no stranger with Him, and there is nothing besides Him. Before Him flow rivers of fire. From His majesty, the mighty ones <term>Elim</term> <note>original: אלים (Elim); here referring to angelic beings or 'god-like' powers</note> are afraid, and from His roar, the pillars tremble, and from His voice, the foundations of the thresholds shake. His hosts stand before Him, yet they cannot gaze upon the likeness of His face, for He is hidden from all, and they are not able to see Him. They tremble, for His likeness is hidden from everything, yet the likeness of everything is not hidden from Him. He reveals deep things out of darkness and brings the shadow of death into light. He knows what is in the secrets of the gloom, for light dwells with Him. He wraps Himself in light as a garment and sits upon the Throne of Light. Around Him are eyes of light and generations. The wheels <term>Ophanim</term> <note>original: אופנים (Ophanim); a class of angels associated with the wheels of the Divine Chariot</note> are exalted, and some of them fly; they have six wings. With their wings they cover their faces, and they cast their faces downward; the four of them have faces, but they do not lift their faces upward because of the dread and awe of Him. They stand in their ranks and groups before Him like a river of purity, wrapped in garments of white fire, and they respond with a strong and steady voice: "Holy, Holy, Holy <note>original: ק' ק' ק' (Q. Q. Q.); abbreviation for Qadosh, Qadosh, Qadosh</note> is the Lord of Hosts; the whole earth is full of His glory!" He is the place of all works; He is the place of the whole world; He is the place of the Garden of Eden. He existed even before the heavens and the earth. He is alone, and no one is with Him, and there is nothing besides Him. * Praise to the Creator in His dwelling, and in all the dwellings of His fear, for by the breath of His mouth they were carved, and by His flame they were hewn. * He is one and has no second; He has no substitute and there is no one like Him. There is none besides Him and none without Him. And He commanded: I shall not be nullified. He is the living King of the Kings of Kings, the Holy One, Blessed be He, who reigns over all the kings of the earth and is exalted above all the angels of the heavens. He searches hearts before they are even formed, and knows thoughts before they exist. The Lord is His name; blessed is His precious glory forever and for all eternity, for ever and ever, to the end of time, for there is none but Him. * There is no God besides Him. With His voice, He makes the earth tremble, and He moves mountains and shatters mountains in His anger. He stirs the sea with His power and paces the pillars of the world with His utterance. He carries everything in His arm and is hidden from the eyes of all living things. He sits on a throne of great glory, His holy kingdom. He paces the pillars of the world and moves through the entire world, yet He is not seen. He knows all secrets and knows what is in the darkness. He turns the shadow of death into morning and makes the night shine like the day. All secrets are revealed before Him like the sun, and nothing is hidden from Him. He is the Holy King who walks on the wings of the wind. He rules over all worlds, and there is no king but Him. The sun, the moon, all the stars, and all the constellations bow down to Him. He is the one revealed in the Garden of Eden, and He is the one who plants the Tree of Life in His garden. Blessed be His name in every generation, and blessed in the dwellings on high. He is ancient, and with Him are ancient things and righteousness. Blessed is the Glory from His place, and blessed is He in the beauty of His preciousness. He fills and understands knowledge for His creatures, to search out and to know the fear of His name.

Blessed be His name forever and ever * and blessed for all eternity.

<meta>This page continues the mystical revelation of the Book of Raziel, shifting from the physical dimensions of the Divine to the role of the supreme angel Metatron and the liturgical praises sung in the heavenly courts. # The Book of Raziel

The Holy One, blessed be He, is praised three times every single day by Metatron. The Holy One, blessed be He, bestowed some of His own radiance and splendor upon the ministering angel Metatron, the Prince of the Presence <term>Sar HaPanim; the high-ranking angel who stands directly before God</term>. It is written that he is the Great Prince over all other princes and over all ministering angels. They stand before him, while he stands high above in the heights, serving before the Master of the World <note>original: Ar’m, likely an abbreviation for Adon Olam</note>, who is a consuming fire. A throne of fire is his place. This is the same Metatron, the Prince of the Presence, who was written of with the single letter through which heaven and earth were created, and he was sealed with the ring of **I Am That I Am** <note>original: Ehyeh Asher Ehyeh</note. He is written with seven letters, and with seventy-four letters, and with seventy names, and with seven sanctities. He was placed over six of their names and engraved upon twelve stones. He was written with seven voices, six upon the heights of six, and placed within the innermost chambers, the secrets of secrets, and the wonders of wonders for our teacher Moses. The Holy One, blessed be He, did not give permission to use this [the Name] to Adam the first man, nor to Shem the son of Noah, nor to Abraham, Isaac, or Jacob—but to Moses alone. As it is said: "Behold, I send an angel before you." The Holy One, blessed be He, warned Moses to be careful regarding him, as it is said: "Pay attention to him and listen to his voice; do not rebel against him, for he will not forgive your rebellion, since My Name is in him" <note>Exodus 23:20-21</note>. And Moses said before the Holy One, blessed be He: "If Your Presence does not go with us, do not bring us up from here." The angels who are with him come and circle before the Throne of Glory from one side, and the Living Creatures <term>Hayyot; a high order of angels</term> from the other side. The Divine Presence <term>Shekhinah</term> is upon the Throne of Glory in the center. One Living Creature rises above the Seraphim and descends upon the dwelling of Metatron, saying with a great voice, a "still small voice": "This is the Throne of Glory!" Immediately, they are silenced. The Wheels <term>Ophanim; another order of angelic beings</term> become still, and the angels, the Watchers, and the Holy Ones are hushed and tremble at the word of the Lord. The Living Creatures cast their faces to the ground. Metatron then brings "silent fire" and places it in the ears of the Living Creatures so that they do not hear the voice of the Glory of the Holy One, blessed be He, nor the Explicit Name <term>Shem HaMephorash; the secret, articulated name of God</term>. At that moment, Metatron mentions the Explicit Name. This is how he calls to the Holy One, blessed be He, by His name—the Living, Pure, Holy, Mighty, Awesome, Strong, Powerful, Valiant, Honored, and Terrible: **Adirdu Adirki Dach-chayi YHVH I Am That I Am Hach-chayi Ziya Hakah Heh Heh Hu Havah Vahu Heh Heh Hi Hu Heheh Yehi Hi Hi Yiheyeh YHVH, the Life of the Worlds.** "This is My name forever, and this is My memorial from generation to generation." This is explained in the language of purity: **Yaho Heh-yiv Hayah Hi Yah Yah Havahi Hayah Ve-yihyu Yehu He-chayi Vay-chah Yehi Hi He-hi Hayi Hu YHVH Yehu He-yahu Hi YHVH.**

# The Book of Raziel

<meta>catchword: He</meta>

The Holy One, blessed be He, is praised three times every single day through Metatron <note>The highest archangel in Jewish mysticism, often described as the celestial scribe or the "Prince of the Presence"</note>. The Holy One, blessed be He, placed some of His own brilliance and splendor upon the ministering angels through Metatron, the Prince of the Face <note>original: "Sar ha-Panim"; a title for an angel who stands directly before the Divine</note>, for it is written that he is the Great Prince over all princes and over all ministering angels. They stand before him, and he stands high above in the heights, serving before the Consuming Fire <note>A metaphor for God, based on Deuteronomy 4:24</note>. A throne of fire is there; this is Metatron, the Prince of the Face, whose name is written with the single letter through which heaven and earth were created, and he is sealed with the ring of "I Am That I Am" <note>original: "Ehyeh Asher Ehyeh"; the name God revealed to Moses at the burning bush</note>. He is written with seven letters, and with seven letters, and with twenty-four letters, and with seventy names, and with seven sanctities. He was granted six of their names, engraved upon twelve stones, written with seven voices in six upon the height of six. He was placed in the innermost chambers, in the secret of secrets and the wonder of wonders, for Moses our teacher. The Holy One, blessed be He, did not give permission to use this—not to Adam the first man, nor to Shem the son of Noah, nor to Abraham, Isaac, or Jacob—but to Moses alone. As it is said: "Behold, I send an angel before you," and the Holy One, blessed be He, warned Moses to be careful with him, as it is said: "Be careful before him and obey his voice; do not rebel against him, for he will not forgive your transgression, for My Name is within him." <note>Quoting Exodus 23:20-21</note> And Moses said before the Holy One, blessed be He: "If Your presence does not go with us, do not bring us up from here." The angels who are with him come and surround the Throne of Glory; they are on one side, and the Living Creatures <note>original: "Chayyot"; the four-winged beings from Ezekiel's vision</note> are on the other side, and the Divine Presence is upon the Divine Presence, the Glory is in the middle, and one Living Creature rises above the Seraphim <note>High-ranking angels associated with fire and purification</note> and descends upon the dwelling of Metatron, saying with a great voice, a voice of thin silence: "This is the Throne of Glory." Immediately the Wheels <note>original: "Ophanim"; celestial beings described as multi-eyed wheels</note> become silent, the angels, the guards, the watchers, and the holy ones fall silent. They feel the rushing of the River of Fire, and the Living Creatures turn their faces toward the earth. Metatron brings a "silent fire" <note>original: "Esh Hacharsit"</note> and places it in the ears of the Living Creatures so they do not hear the sound of the Glory of the Holy One, blessed be He, nor the Explicit Name <note>The Tetragrammaton, the four-letter name of God</note>. Metatron mentions it at that moment, and thus he calls to the Holy One, blessed be He, by His name—the Living, Pure, Holy, Mighty, Splendid, Strong, Powerful, Sovereign, Courageous, Honored, and Awesome. Mighty is He: A-H-H-Y, D-H-Y, Y-H-W-H, I Am That I Am, the Living, Y-W-A, H-W-H, W-H, W-H, H-W-H, W-H-W, H-H, H-H, H-Y-A, H-W-A, H-H, Y-H, Y-H-Y, H-Y, H-Y, Y-H-W-H, Y-H-W-H, Life of the Worlds. "This is My name forever, and this is My memorial from generation to generation." <note>Quoting Exodus 3:15</note> And its meaning in the tongue of purity is: Y-H-W, H-H-Y-W, H-Y-H, H-Y, Y-H, Y-H, H-W-H-Y, H-Y-H, and they shall be, Y-H-W the Living, and Y-H-H, Y-H-Y, H-Y, H-H-Y-W, H-Y-Y, H-W, Y-H-W-H, Y-H-W, H-Y-H-W, H-Y, Y-H-W-H.

# The Book of Raziel

The praises shall be hallowed with their full voice; they shall be lauded in every combination. You shall be magnified forever and sanctified for all eternity, **Metatron <note>original: "Metatron YHVH"</note>, Lord** God of Israel, King over all the worlds, Master of all deeds, Wise in all secrets, and Ruler over all spirits—the one God, Prince of the worlds and Master of all actions, the unique King who is victorious for all eternity, Selah.

> **May** His name be blessed forever and for all eternity. Who is like You, the King called **Metatron <note>original: "Metatron YHVH"</note>**, God of Israel, Lord of Lords? May the Throne of Your Glory be praised. To You they shall give majesty and strength; in You shall dwell power and beauty. For You they shall be strengthened; for You they shall be emboldened. For You they shall renew; for You they shall sing; for You they shall rejoice; for You they shall bless; for You they shall praise; for You they shall exalt; for You they shall glorify; for You they shall uplift; for You they shall magnify; for You they shall honor; for You they shall sing music; for You they shall profess unity; for You they shall shine; for You they shall laud; for You they shall crown; for You they shall grant victory; for You they shall extol; for You they shall exult; for You they shall sanctify; for You they shall praise; for You they shall sing; for You they shall make melody. To You, **Metatron**; to You, **the Lord** God of Israel and the splendor of the Seraphim <note>The "Burning Ones," a high order of angels.</note>. To You <unclear>Yekhi-Orbat</unclear>. To You, **Metatron**, to You, **the Lord** God of Israel, Your servants shall offer crowns and sing a new song, for Your Kingship is forever. Everything is under Your dominion, and over all is Your rule. One unique sustainer for all eternity, **Metatron <note>original: "Metatron YHVH"</note>**, God of Israel. Blessed are You, [Lord], King over all secrets and Master of all hidden things. And when the Holy One, Blessed be He, calls to the Youth <note>term: Na'ar (Youth). A common title for the angel Metatron, signifying his role as a celestial servant or high priest.</note>, this is how He calls him: **Yaho-Bi-El**. This is **Yah** and **Yehi**; this is **Ha-Re** and **Hu-Bar**, and the nicknames <unclear>A-H-H D-Y-A A-Ch-V-H N-V-Y</unclear> **the Lord** from the flickering of fire. The heavens of Arabot <note>The seventh and highest heaven in Jewish cosmology.</note>... [These are] the name of the Youth, who does not learn of death or guilt. His appearance from his loins downward and from his loins upward are similar to one another. The name of the Youth is like the name of his Master <note>A famous Talmudic and Kabbalistic concept based on Exodus 23:21: "for My name is in him."</note>, for it is said, "For My name is within him." The name of the Holy One, Blessed be He, consists of seventy-two letters, and the name of the **Youth** consists of 72 letters. When he ascends, it is 27 letters, and when he descends, it is 27 letters. "It is the glory of God to conceal a matter" <note>A quote from Proverbs 25:2.</note> so that you are not cast out from the world. Beyond this, you have no permission to speak, for so it is written in the Book of Ben Sira: "In that which is too wonderful for you, do not seek; in that which is concealed from you, do not investigate; in that which you know, reflect; and you have no business with hidden things." And we learn one-on-one, as our Sages of blessed memory taught: "One may not expound upon the laws of forbidden sexual relations before three people, nor the Work of Creation <note>term: Ma'aseh Bereshit. The mystical study of the origins of the universe.</note> before two, nor the Work of the Chariot <note>term: Ma'aseh Merkabah. The most advanced form of early Jewish mysticism, involving visions of the Divine Throne-Chariot.</note> even to one, unless he is wise and understands from his own knowledge; [then] one may transmit to him the 'chapter headings.'"

**The Lord** shall enlighten my eyes to understand what was said regarding "The Beginning." Because of this, [the light] goes out from the letter *Yod* found in the AL-BaM <note>gloss: a system of Hebrew letter substitution/cipher.</note> and rises to the *Yod* found in the AT-BaSh... this is the "Face." It goes out from *Yod* and *Pe* in the AT-BaSh and from the *Lamed* in the AL-BaM and rises... this is the Explicit Name. Blessed are You, Lord our God, King of the Universe, who has sanctified us with His commandments and commanded us regarding the words of the Holy Name. Rabbi Akiva said: Since I explained this descent and ascent to the Children of Israel, they established for me three blessings in the Heavenly Court and the Earthly Court. And for anyone who studies the Book of the Work of Creation, I have established a blessing for him every single day in the Heavenly Court and the Earthly Court—and in a house where they study it, how many more blessings shall there be! Furthermore, I love him and make him fruitful.

**And anyone** who wishes to become wise every day should take this book and read it from beginning to end. And the house in which this book is kept shall not suffer fire, nor injury, nor destruction, nor lack. For the Living Creatures <note>The "Chayot," the angels carrying the Divine Chariot.</note> asked Moses our Teacher, peace be upon him: "What is the Name that serves above?"

**A Dream Inquiry**, tested and proven: Wash your hands until pure, and rub the left hand with lily water. Write upon it as follows: "You, holy angels of <unclear>A-K</unclear>, whose names are <unclear>S-M-Y-K-N</unclear>, come and tell me my entire question clearly, just as you told Joseph the interpretation of the dream. And now, if you are to do kindness and truth, tell me: **A-A-A S-S-S** victory, victory, victory." Then lie on your right side—though it is better to be on the left—and you shall see and be amazed.

> **A Dream Inquiry**, tested and proven: Fast for three days in purity, humility, and awe. Wear clean white garments and cleanse yourself from all ritual impurity. Purify yourself for three days—Monday, Tuesday, and Wednesday—and do not eat until Thursday. On Thursday night, light a candle in your house and lie on the earth in the middle of your house. Your face should be toward the light of the candle. State your question and request and adjure [the angel]. "I adjure you by the Explicit Name, who dwells in the thick clouds of the high heavens, that you shall come this night with a good eye and a peaceful heart. Do not come to me except with love, and speak my question to me—that which I seek from you—and interpret my question for me just as Gabriel interpreted Pharaoh's dream for Joseph, and his words were truth. So may you come to me this night in truth and interpret my question and request by your name, for your name is the Prince **Metatron**. [I adjure you] by the Explicit Name who sits upon the wheels of the Chariot, by the name of the Living and Enduring One who dwells in the Arabot heaven—*Yah* is His name, exult before Him! By the name of the Great, Mighty, and Awesome God, who is beloved, exchanged, and faithful. I have adjured you, Prince **Metatron**, by 'I Am That I Am' <note>The name revealed to Moses at the burning bush.</note>, who was revealed to Moses son of Amram from within the bush. Blessed are You, [Lord], teach me Your statutes. By the Name that is expressed in seventy languages, and the one who ascends in seventy languages and ascends in seventy names. I adjure you by the Name that makes the world tremble; at His name the legions of the upper and lower worlds shudder, and from His wrath the earth quakes and the nations cannot endure His indignation. 'Blessed be the glory of the Lord from His place,' and 'Blessed be the Name of His glorious kingdom forever and ever.'"

> **I have adjured** you, Prince **Metatron**, by the name of the God of Israel, the God of Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob; by the name of the angels who stand before the Great God. And these are the seven angels: Michael, Gabriel, Raphael, Haniel, Zuriel, Tariel, Uziel.

# The Book of Raziel

->YHVH, the Lord, He, the One Who Is, the One Who Was, He of the Worlds. This is My name forever, and this is My memorial from generation to generation. <note>original: "זה שמי לעולם וזה זכרי לדר דר"</note> Blessed be the Name of the glory of His kingdom forever and ever. <note>original abbreviation: "בשכמל"ו"</note> YH, H-HYV, HYH, HY, YH, HH, HV<-

->Blessed be the Name of the glory of His kingdom forever and ever.<-

As His name, so is His greatness; and as His greatness, so is His praise; and as His praise, so is His kingdom; and as His kingdom, so is His holiness; and as His holiness, so is His beauty; and as His beauty, so is the length of His years; and as the length of His years, so is His patience; and as His patience, so is the abundance of His kindness; and as the abundance of His kindness, so is His strength; and in His strength, so is His vengeance; and as His vengeance, so is His greatness; and as His greatness, so is the abundance of His mercy; and in the abundance of His mercy, so is His goodness; and His goodness and His mercy are over all His works.

**To You it is fitting** <note>original: "Lekha Na'eh"</note> to clarify His name, the name of greatness, and strength, and the kingdom, and the beauty, and the victory, and the majesty—for all that is in the heavens and the earth is Your name. From it comes the son of the name of Your glory. From of old, Your name has been in glory forever and ever. The upper beings shall bless Your name, and the glorified of Your name, and the sanctified of Your name. Your name is lauded and praised, and Your name is exalted above every blessing and praise. For Your name is in You, and You are in Your name, for Your name was established before all things, and Your name is established for all eternity. From Your name, the legions of fire tremble; the legions of flame glorify Your name; the Seraphim <note>The "Burning Ones," high-ranking angels.</note> praise Your name; the Holy Creatures <note>original: "Chayot," the angels carrying the Divine Throne.</note> sanctify Your name; and the Wheels <note>original: "Ophanim," a class of celestial beings.</note> purify Your name. For Your name created all creatures, yet Your name they have not envisioned. From Your name, the sea flees backward, and all its breakers and waves bless Your name. All the inhabitants of the heights and every single dwelling-place fear and glorify it. The heavens and the earth and their inhabitants, the deep and the abyss, the parched land and the shadow of death, man and beast and creeping thing, valleys and hills, seas and rivers of fire, hail, snow, and smoke, the great wind, the angels of fire, and the angels of water—all shall tremble from Your name. And everything in whose nostrils is the breath of the spirit of life shall give glory to Your name.

And I, David your servant, son of Zlata <note>This is a personal insertion by the individual using the book, identifying himself by his mother's name, as is traditional in prayers for mercy or healing.</note>, who am but dust and ashes, of a broken heart and a lowly spirit, a maggot and a worm compared to all who pass in the field—I have come to lay my supplication before You and my prayer to find grace in Your eyes. Grant favor, kindness, charity, and mercy before the Throne of Your kingdom’s glory, for You are close to all who call upon You and available to all who seek You. Holy and awesome, for You are full of mercy; for You are pure and upright, gracious and compassionate; for You are pure, a good God who forgives. Because You are pure, please fulfill my prayer, my request, and my plea for the sake of Your glory and my prayer. 

[I,] David son of Zlata, Your servant, [ask for] the gates of prayer, and the gates of repentance, and the gates of understanding, the gates of wisdom, and the gates of knowledge, the gates of charity, the gates of mercy, the gates of the fear of heaven, the gates of blessing, the gates of sustenance and livelihood. And may You write me in the Book of Life with the righteous, and give me my portion in Your Torah and my lot for a good life, for the sake of the glory of Your great, mighty, and awesome Name—the Explicit Name <note>original: "HaMeforash," referring to the Tetragrammaton.</note>, the noble, the valiant, the strong, the powerful, the wondrous, the holy, the pure, and the honored. Holy, holy, holy is the Lord of Hosts; the whole earth is full of His glory!

**That Your angels,** Your hosts, shall give to You, shall honor You, shall embolden You, shall crown You, shall walk for You, shall ring out for You, and shall glorify You. They shall sanctify You and exalt You. They shall rejoice for You and open with song and the voice of praise: song and melody, blessing, praise and laud, acclaim, thanksgiving, freedom, victory, music, meditation, rejoicing, shouting, gladness and joy, pleasantness, humility and majesty, beauty and tranquility, trust, goodness, love, desire and comfort, grace and kindness for beauty of form, light, splendor, merit, radiance, will and a crown, brightness, light and branch and bough, a fragrant mixture of light and myrrh, strength, height and preciousness, strong dominion and courage, exaltedness and pride, holiness, purity, and cleanness, power and might and valor, greatness, majesty, and kingdom, and glory and beauty, a crown of majesty and a diadem of awesomeness. 

For His name is pleasant and His memory is sweet; His love is for Him and His radiance is fitting for Him; His glory is desired by Him. His ministers sing pleasantly before Him, and the power of His wonders is told by the King of kings, the God of gods, and the Lord of lords. He is crowned with the bonds of royalty, surrounded by the branches of the hidden treasuries of the lights of power. His appearance is a refuge, and His fragments shatter the mighty ones. His portion is a precious fortress. Let every sorrowful one rejoice in His word! The princes shall sing with joy, and His words shall flow in the heavens. Those who sing and go forth are precious in flames of fire, and they grant freedom to those who seek Him in tranquility. For You are the established King, beloved and desired and pure, who is exalted above all the proud; who meditates upon freedom; the glorious one who rises above the mighty; the exalted over the awesome ones; the beauty of those who clothe themselves in praise; the kin of the joyful; the holiness of the holy; and accessible to all who hope in His name. He is good in all His ways, upright in all His deeds, and pleasant in all His attributes. Pure in counsel and in knowledge, clear in understanding and in action, Judge of every soul, Witness to every matter, and Arbiter of every word. Valiant in wisdom with every secret of radiance, in purity and in all holiness.

The True King, the Unique One, the King Who Lives and Endures forever. The King who puts to death and brings to life. The King who forms every blow and creates every healing. The King who forms every blessing and prepares every good thing. The King who feeds and sustains all who perform His works, and provides for all His creatures. The King who is high above every lowly person, and for every valiant one, He is strong. He is the King, high and lifted up, exalted and wondrous, precious and honored, beloved and upright, strong and valiant, supreme and humble, holy and pure, righteous and just, faithful, pious, and powerful. Great and mighty, then, is the Eternal One who sits upon a high and lofty throne. His strength is glorious, precious, and awesome, for He is included in all the bonds of majesty and established upon all the pillars of beauty in the chambers of the palace of pride. He gazes into their hidden places and sees into the depths; He is in every place. There is no turning back His words, nor delaying His will and His desire. There is no changing Him, and there is no place to flee from Him, and no opening to hide from Him. He shall reign forever! He shall reign on His throne for generation to generation. A King merciful and gracious, forgiving and pardoning, who revolves and passes over [sin]. # The Book of Raziel

The praises shall be lauded with every voice; You shall be acclaimed in all grandeurs. You shall be magnified forever, and You shall be sanctified for all eternity, Metatron <note>Metatron is a high-ranking angel in Jewish mysticism, often described as the "Prince of the Countenance" or the heavenly scribe.</note> YHVH, God of Israel, King over all worlds, Master of all deeds, Wise in all secrets, and Ruler over all spirits. He is the One God, the Prince of the worlds, and the Master of all deeds; the unique King who is victorious for all eternity, Selah.

**May the Name** be blessed forever and for all eternity. Who is like You, the King called Metatron-YHVH, the God of Israel, the Lord of Lords? May the throne of Your glory be praised. To You they shall give grandeur and strength; for You, strength and beauty shall dwell. For You, they shall grow strong; for You, they shall take courage. For You, they shall renew; for You, they shall sing. For You, they shall rejoice; for You, they shall bless. For You, they shall praise; for You, they shall exalt. For You, they shall glorify; for You, they shall lift up. For You, they shall magnify; for You, they shall honor. For You, they shall speak; for You, they shall increase. For You, they shall triumph; for You, they shall acclaim. For You, they shall crown; for You, they shall prevail. For You, they shall extol; for You, they shall exult. For You, they shall sanctify; for You, they shall laud. For You, they shall sing; for You, they shall make pleasant music. To You, *Tetrosy* <note>original: "טטרוסי" - a mystical permutation or name of power</note>; to You, O Lord God of Israel, the Seraphim <note>A high order of angels described as fiery beings.</note> shall give honor. For You, He shall kindle the lights; to You, *Tetrosy* YHVH God of Israel, Your ministers shall offer crowns. They shall sing a new song to You and crown You King forever. For everything is within Your dominion, and over all is Your sovereignty. There is one Ancient One <note>original: "vatiq", meaning ancient or venerable</note> for all eternity: *Tetros* YHVH God of Israel. Blessed be the King over all secrets and the Master of all hidden things.

And when the Holy One, Blessed be He, calls him "the Youth" <note>Metatron is frequently called "Na'ar" (Youth or Servant) in Kabbalistic literature.</note>, this is how He calls him: this is *Bariel*, and this is *D...* <note>The text here lists cryptic letter permutations and secret names of the angel</note> and his nickname is *A-H-H D-Y-A A-H-R N-V-Y* YHVH Metatron. The foundations of the firmament, the majesty of *Ruviel*—this is the name of the Youth. He who does not learn... <note>The text becomes highly elliptical/coded here regarding the dangers of using these names</note> and whoever makes use of the Crown <note>meaning the mystical names of God</note> shall pass away. From the appearance of his loins and downward, they resemble one another; and from the appearance of his loins and upward, they resemble one another. The name of the Youth is as the name of his Master, as it is said: "For My name is within him" <note>Exodus 23:21, traditionally interpreted as referring to Metatron whose name shares the numerical value (314) with Shaddai (Almighty).</note>. The name of the Holy One, Blessed be He, consists of seventy-two letters, and the name of the Youth consists of seventy-two letters. When he ascends, he has 27 letters, and when he descends, he has 27 letters. It is the glory of God to conceal a matter, so that you are not driven from the world. [He is] the King of the world. From here on, you have no permission to speak, for so it is written in the Book of Ben Sira:

-> "In that which is too wonderful for you, do not seek; in that which is hidden from you, do not inquire. In that which you know, reflect; and you have no business with hidden things." <note>A famous quote from the apocryphal Wisdom of Sirach 3:21–22, often cited in the Talmud to limit mystical speculation.</note> <-

And it was repeated in the teachings of our Sages, of blessed memory: "One may not expound upon forbidden sexual relations before three, nor the Work of Creation <note>Ma'aseh Bereshit</note> before two, nor the Work of the Chariot <note>Ma'aseh Merkavah - the highest form of Jewish mysticism</note> even before one, unless he is wise and understands by his own knowledge; then they may transmit to him the chapter headings."

**The Name** shall enlighten my eyes to understand what was said at the "Beginning" <note>Bereshit</note>. For this reason, it emerges from the *Yod* that is in *Elohim* and ascends to the *Yod* that is in *Elohim*... <note>The scribe describes a complex process of extracting and permuting letters from the Divine Name to find the "Explicit Name" or Shem HaMeforash.</note> This is the Explicit Name. Blessed be the Lord, King of the Universe. 

Regarding the words of the Holy Name, Rabbi Akiva said: Once it was explained, it descended and ascended for the Children of Israel. They established for me three blessings in the Heavenly Court and in the earthly court. And for anyone who studies the order of the Work of Creation, I have established a blessing for him every single day in the Heavenly Court and the earthly court. In a house where these things are studied, how many more blessings there shall be! Moreover, I love him and make him fruitful.

**And whoever** wishes to grow wise every day should take this book and read it from beginning to end. And the house in which this book is kept shall not suffer fire, nor wreckage, nor destruction, nor lack. This is what the Living Creatures <note>The 'Hayyot' - the highest order of angels supporting the Divine Throne.</note> asked of Moses our Teacher regarding the Name that serves on high.

**A Dream Inquiry** <note>Sha'alat Halom - a ritual to receive answers to specific questions through a dream.</note>, tested and proven: Wash your hands until clean, and rub your left hand with lily water. Write upon it as follows: "You holy angels, *D-A-Q* <note>An angelic name/formula</note> are your names; come and tell me my entire question clearly, just as you told Joseph the interpretation of the dream. And now, if you are performing kindness and truth, tell me: *A-A-A S-S-S* Victory, Victory, Victory." Then lie down on your right side—though it is better to be on the left—and you shall see and be amazed.

**A Dream Inquiry**, tested and proven: Fast for three days in purity, humility, and awe. Wear clean white garments and cleanse yourself of all ritual impurity. Purify yourself for three days—Monday, Tuesday, and Wednesday—and do not eat until Thursday. On Thursday night, light a lamp in your house and lie on the ground in the middle of your house. Your face should be toward the light of the lamp. Speak your question and your request, and adjure him:

"I adjure you by the Explicit Name, Who dwells in the thick clouds of the highest heavens, that you shall come this night with a good eye and a peaceful heart. Do not come to me except with love, and speak my question to me—that which I ask of you. Solve my question for me just as Gabriel solved for Joseph the dream of Pharaoh, and his words were truth. So may you come to me this night in truth, and solve my question and my request in your name; for your name is the Prince Metatron. By the Explicit Name Who sits upon the wheels of the Chariot; by the name of the Living and Enduring One Who dwells in the highest heaven <note>Arabot</note>, whose name is *Y-H*, and [be] joyful before Him. By the name of the God, the Great, the Mighty, and the Awesome, Who is beloved, feared, and faithful. And I adjure you, Prince Metatron, by *I Am That I Am* <note>Ehyeh Asher Ehyeh - the name revealed at the Burning Bush.</note>, which was revealed to Moses son of Amram from within the bush. Blessed are You, O Lord, teach me Your statutes. By the name expressed in seventy languages, the most excellent of seventy languages, and the most excellent of seventy names. And I adjure you by the name that makes the world tremble; at His name the legions of the upper and lower beings shudder, and from His wrath the earth quakes and nations cannot contain His indignation. Blessed be the glory of the Lord from His place, and Blessed be the Name of the glory of His kingdom forever and ever."

**I have adjured** you, Prince Metatron, by the name of the God of Israel, the God of Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob; by the name of the angels who stand before the Great God. These are the seven angels: Michael, Gabriel, Raphael, Hanael, Tzuriel, Tariel, Azriel...

<summary>This page contains mystical praises of the angel Metatron, emphasizing his status as the bearer of the Divine Name. It also provides warnings against forbidden mystical speculation and detailed instructions for performing "dream inquiries" to gain hidden knowledge through angelic invocation.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the liturgical exaltation of the Divine from the previous page, transitioning from the specific mention of Metatron into a series of mystical permutations of the Divine Name and a lengthy formal prayer of praise and personal petition.</meta>

# The Book of Raziel

YHVH, He is, He was, the Life of the worlds. "This is My name forever, and this is My memorial from generation to generation" <note>Exodus 3:15</note>. Blessed be the name of the glory of His kingdom forever and ever. May it be: He who is, who was, who shall be; He, Yah, Yah, He, He, Hu, Hi, Havah.
He, Havah, and they shall be; Yahu, He-Hi, and-Hi, Havah; Yah, Yehi, Hu, He-Hiyu, Hayi, Hu, YHVH, Yahu, He-Hu, Hi, Havah. <note>These strings of letters are mystical permutations of the Tetragrammaton (the four-letter name of God, YHVH), intended for meditation or vocalization in prayer.</note>

->Blessed be the name of the glory of His kingdom forever and ever.<-

As His name, so is His greatness; and as His greatness, so is His praise. And in His praise, so is His kingdom; and as His kingdom, so is His holiness. And as His holiness, so is His beauty; and as His beauty, so is the length of His years. And as the length of His years, so is His patience; and as His patience, so is the abundance of His kindness. And the abundance of His kindness, so is His might; and in His might, so is His vengeance. And in His vengeance, so is His greatness; and as His greatness, so is the abundance of His mercy. And in the abundance of His mercy, so is His goodness; and His goodness is His mercy upon all His works.

## To You it is Befitting
It is befitting for the glory of Your name: the greatness, the power, the kingdom, the beauty, the victory, and the majesty; for all in heaven and on earth is Your name. From Him, to the Son, <insert>the Name</insert> of Your glory; and from eternity, Your name is in glory forever and ever. The beings on high bless Your name; Your name is glorified, Your name is sanctified, Your name is praised, and Your name is exalted above every blessing and praise. For Your name is "Son," and in You is Your name; for before all things Your name was established, and for the eternity of eternities Your name is established. From Your name, the legions of fire tremble; and from the fear of Your name, the legions of flame [shudder]. The Seraphim <note>A high order of angels associated with fire and purification.</note> praise Your name; the Living Creatures <term>Chayyot: a class of celestial beings described in the vision of Ezekiel</term> sanctify Your name; and the Wheels <term>Ophanim: angelic beings described as celestial wheels</term> purify Your name. For Your name created all creatures, and Your name they have not envisioned. From Your name, day and night slumber; and all the breakers and waves of the sea magnify Your name. All the inhabitants of the heights and every single dwelling place tremble and fear. Heaven and earth and their inhabitants, the deep and the abyss, the desert and the shadow of death, man and beast, mountains and hills and peaks, the seas and the rivers of fire, hail, snow and smoke, the great wind and the angels of fire and the angels of water—all quiver before Your name. And everything that has the breath of the spirit of life in its nostrils shall give glory to Your name.

And I, Dor Your servant, son of Zlata <note>This identifies the specific person for whom this prayer was written or who was using this manuscript.</note>, dust and ashes, proud of heart but lowly of spirit, a worm and a maggot, a passing shadow, like a flower of the field—I have come to pour out my supplications before You, and my prayer is to find favor in Your eyes: favor, kindness, charity, and mercy before the throne of the glory of Your kingdom. For You are close to all who call upon You, and found by all who seek You. You are Holy and Awesome, for You are full of mercy; for You are grace and uprightness, gracious and merciful. For You are pure, a God who is good and forgiving; because You are pure, act now upon my prayer, my request, and my petition from before the throne of Your glory. Open for me—Dor son of Zlata, Your servant—the gates of prayer, the gates of repentance, the gates of understanding, the gates of wisdom, the gates of knowledge, the gates of righteousness, the gates of mercy, the gates of the fear of heaven, the gates of blessing, the gates of sustenance and livelihood. Write me in the Book of Life with the righteous, and give me my portion in Your Torah and my lot for a good life, for the sake of the glory of Your name—the Great, the Mighty, the Awesome, the Glorified, the Magnificent, the Courageous, the Strong, the Wondrous, the Holy, the Hidden, and the Honored. Holy, Holy, Holy is the Lord of Hosts; the whole earth is full of His glory!

## Which Your Angels
Your hosts shall give to You, they shall honor You, they shall strengthen You, they shall crown You; to You and for You they shall ring out; for You they shall glorify, for You they shall sanctify, and for You they shall exalt. For You they shall rejoice and break forth in song and acclamation of praise; song and music, blessing, praise, and laudation; acclamation of praise, thanksgiving, freedom, eternity, melody, meditation, joy, exultation, gladness and mirth, singing and pleasantness, humility and pride, and strength, truth, charity, uprightness, and habit; greatness, beauty and power, and rejoicing, high wonders and salvation, rest, a dwelling place, comfort, peace, quiet, tranquility, and secure ease, trust, goodness, love, compassion and consolation; grace and kindness for beauty, form, light, desire and comfort; radiance, merit, brightness, will, and a crown of splendor; light and a branch and a crown of splendor; a compounded spice, a luminary of light, and a watchful city; a daughter of height, preciousness, strength, dominion, and courage; a high lifting and brilliance, holiness, purity, and cleanliness; power, might, valor, greatness, pride and kingship, and majesty and splendor; a diadem of pride and a crown of awesomeness.

His name is pleasant and His memory is sweet; splendor belongs to Him and His majesty is fitting for Him. His glory is desired by Him, and His ministers make music before His presence. There is the strength of His wonders, telling of the King of kings of kings, the God of gods, and the Lord of lords. He is entwined in the bonds of kingship, surrounded by the turbans of the hidden stores of might. In the eyes of His majesty, so is the heaven; and in His splendor, He appeared from on high. From His mouth the depths were split, and from His light the proud skies were sparked. His form is a refuge, and the mighty are shattered and leap; the precious ones are driven by His portion, and all who are sad rejoice at His word. The grasses sing in His joy, and His words are in the heavens like mountains; the precious ones go out in flames of fire, and riddles are given to those who seek them in tranquility in their places.

He is a beloved King, desired and pure, who raises Himself above all the proud. He is the Beautiful One who is glorified above the prince; the Splendid One who is exalted above the mighty; the Power and the Exalted above the awesome ones; the Beauty of the kings of praise, the Joy of those close to Him, the Sanctity of His holy ones. He is found by all who hope for His name. He is good in all His ways, upright in all His deeds, and pleasant in all His attributes. He is pure in counsel and knowledge, clear in understanding and action. He is the Judge for every soul and a Witness for every matter, a Judge for every word. He is the Mighty One in wisdom, in every single secret, in purity and in all holiness. He is the King of truth and Unique, the King who lives and endures forever, the King who brings death and gives life, the King who forms every blow and creates every healing. He is the King who forms every blessing and understands every good thing. He is the King who is pure in all His deeds and sustains all His creatures. He is the King who is like a father to every lowly person and to every courageous one—He is strong. He is the King who is high and lifted up, exalted and wondrous, precious and honored, unique, existing, strong and courageous, supreme and humble, holy and pure, righteous and upright, faithful, pious and powerful, great and mighty. Then, the Enduring One who sits upon a throne, high and lifted up, strong, magnificent, precious, and awesome; for He is paved over all the bonds of pride and established upon all the pillars of beauty in the chambers of the palace of pride. He gazes into hidden places and sees into the depths. He is in every place, and there is no turning back His words, nor delaying His will or His desire. There is no changing and no rising to flee from Him, and no secret place to hide from Him. He shall reign forever! He shall reign on His throne for generation after generation. He is a King, merciful and gracious, forgiving and pardoning, moving and passing [sins] away.

May You be glorified in every song; may You be beautified in every pleasantness. May You be exalted in His palace of pride; may You be uplifted in every beauty. May You be made high in all deeds; may You be proud above all creatures. May You be honored upon the throne of Your glory; may You be made precious upon the instrument of Your... <note>The text likely refers to the "mercy-seat" or "throne."</note> May You be blessed with all the blessings; may You be praised with all the praises.

<summary>This page contains a series of mystical permutations of the Divine Name followed by two major liturgical poems ("Lecha Na'eh" and "Asher Mal'achecha") that exalt God's attributes and request the opening of spiritual gates for the petitioner, Dor son of Zlata.</summary>

--- Page 99 ---
<meta>This page continues the transition from purely liturgical praise into the "practical Kabbalah" (magical) applications of the Book of Raziel. The previous page ended with a glorification of the Divine Name; this page applies those names and angelic hierarchies to specific human needs such as love, influence over royalty, illusions of fire, and miraculous travel.</meta>

# The Book of Raziel

<margin>If you seek</margin> to turn the heart of a woman so she will not listen to anyone but you: take the sweat of your brow and face in a new glass vessel. Take one copper plate and write upon it the name *Kasiternan* <note>original: "קסיט"ר"נן"</note>, who is the Name of the Officer, along with the names of the [following] angels. Cast [the sweat] onto it and say, "By the knowledge of your face, I adjure you, angels of knowledge <note>an alternate reading suggests "angels of the appointed time"</note>, that you shall turn the heart of [Name] daughter of [Mother's Name] or [Name] son of [Mother's Name], that she/he shall do nothing without me, and her/his heart shall hasten to me." Take the vial and hide it in the place where she passes. This should be written on a Thursday at the fourth hour of the day, specifically on a Thursday that is the last one of that month. These are the angels and the Officer with them; the Officer is *Palmiya* <note>original: "פלמ"י"א"</note>, and these are his servants: *Aburiah, Azmoriah, Remaniah, Amnehar, Afnoch, Patvaza, Tuviel, Galiel, Ofori, Gamti, Orniel, Maniel, Uvamtu, Sartan, Tzavaktani, Bamartias, Serfit, Karsuwin, Amaf, Pakhar, Hastir, Satriel, Alism, Halsiel, Suraspi, Karchotuch, Malkiel, Achsaf, Amiel, Partam, Gadiel, Skaviel, Kochav*. These are the angels who serve upon the plate; and these are the angels who entice the heart of the King, the mind of the great, and the hearts of all people.

<margin>And if you desire</margin> to bring the King, or if you seek to show his image to your friend or companion through these wondrous things mentioned in this holy and honored book: mention the names of these holy angels and the name of the Officer, and you shall succeed. These are the names of the angels who serve *Dalkiel* <note>name derived from the Hebrew word for "burning" or "kindling"</note>, who stands in the third dwelling and rules over the flames of fire: *Nurah, Aviel, Malkiah, Chuliel, Chariel, Shalchiel, Chaviel, Tzuriel, Bamchiel, Akriel, Sasniel, Bananiel, Tumniel, Amaliel, Talmataf, Shotniel, Arfiel, Agniel, Mashriel, Amgagnau*. Say "thus and so shall you do," and they will do it; you shall succeed, provided it is done in ritual purity.

<margin>And if you desire</margin> to fill the house with smoke and fire that blazes but does not burn anything: take *Agad Agri* <note>possibly a corrupted name for a botanical or mineral substance</note> or *Paryon* and place it upon coals of fire. Its smoke will rise within the house. Mention the name of the Officer and these angels as the smoke rises, and say: "I adjure you, angels wrapped in fire, by Him who is entirely fire, whose seat is upon a throne of fire, whose ministers are a consuming fire, whose camp is fire, and who has ministers of fire before Him; in His Great Name I adjure you to show me this great miracle, and let me not be afraid." When you finish speaking, you will see the house full of fire. If you wish to extinguish it, say: "I adjure you, O mighty angels, to extinguish this great fire, that it shall not remain for even one hour in the house." When you finish, the fire will subside and be extinguished quickly within the hour, leaving no ash or anything behind. Only perform this in purity, and you shall succeed.

<margin>These</margin> are the names of the angels who serve in the sixth camp under *Paskad*: *Aviel, Arbiel, Trifone, Pichvam, Pastamar, Langiel, Karoniran, Shuvtan, Saviran, Azuel, Paniel, Tarmiel, Chamamiel, Tzachmiel, Bimamem, Geronit, Gorantal, Baruva, Armar, Harmor, Zavnum, Chasmiel, Nazuriel, Panion, Karmiel, Kafleya, Armiel, Armon, Afusael, Safriel, Kataniel, Shvaviel, Armenius, Tofimus, Patzatziel, Chatafiel, Parsumin, Nachliel*.

<margin>And these</margin> are the mighty angels girded with power and strength to run from place to place and to bring a person who is in a distant land back to his home, flying in about one hour like a bird; or to bring back a runaway slave. Take four copper plates and write upon each one of them the person's name, his mother's name, the name *Pafkar*, and the names of the angels serving before him. Say: "I hand over to you, angels of might, [Name] son of [Mother's Name], that you shall not delay him in any place where he dwells. Turn his heart, whether in a city, a province, at sea, or on dry land; whether at food or at drink. Make him fly like a bird and bring him even against his will. Do not let him tarry by day or by night." Place [the plates] in the four corners of the house.
