--- Page 100 ---
<meta>This page continues the instructional portion of the manuscript, transitioning from love magic into the high-level adjuration of celestial beings. It focuses on the Prince Metatron and the seasonal angels, culminating in the presentation of the "Explicit Name" of 72 triplets, a central element of Kabbalistic practice. This follows the previous page's focus on influencing others through angelic intervention.</meta>

-># The Book of Raziel<-

<margin>I adjure</margin> I adjure you by Your name and Your truth; Your name is Gracious and Merciful; Your name is "Blessed be the Name of His glorious kingdom forever and ever" <note>original: בשכ"מלו, an abbreviation for *Baruch shem kevod malkhuto l'olam va'ed*</note>.

<margin>First, Nuriel</margin> Upon you, the Prince Metatron <note>Metatron is often described as the highest of angels, the celestial scribe</note>, in the name of the Princes appointed over the four seasons <term>tekufot|the four solar turning points of the year: the equinoxes and solstices</term> of the year:

Tzadkiel, second Uziel, Samael; and third Zachariel, Samniel; fourth Gabriel, Raphael. These are the Princes who rule in every season of that year.

<margin>I adjure</margin> I adjure you, the Prince Metatron, in the name of the Blessed One—whose name is blessed and exalted above all blessing and praise—for Your name is within You and You are within Your name. For before all things, Your name was established, and for all eternity Your name shall endure. At Your name, the legions of the upper and lower worlds tremble, glorifying Your name. All the hosts on high glorify Your name, though they have not seen Your name. At Your name, all things tremble, and the sea flees backward from the fear of Your name, while all its breakers and waves exalt and glorify Your name. The earth trembles when You look upon it, and its pillars shake and fear Your name. All things have been Your name from of old; the inhabitants of the world fear and tremble from the dread of Your name. The dwellers of the abyss, the place of destruction, the wilderness, and the shadow of death shake at Your name. The Garden of Eden and all Your pious ones sing to Your name, and every creature that has the breath of life in its nostrils shall, with the sweetness of its tongue, give glory to Your name. Grant glory to Your name! And I, who am but dust and ashes, a maggot and a worm, broken-hearted and humble in spirit—a passing shadow, like a flower of the field—I have come to pour out my supplication before You and to seek mercy from before You and before Your Throne of Glory. For You are close to all who call upon You and available to those who seek You. Please, fulfill my request through Your favor, hasten my petition through Your mercy, and perform my desire, for You have created me.

<margin>And after</margin> After all this, speak your desire. Your face should be directed downward. "And He, being merciful, forgives iniquity and does not destroy; He frequently turns away His anger and does not stir up all His wrath" <note>Psalm 78:38</note>. "And the Lord descended in a cloud and took of the spirit that was upon him and placed it upon the seventy elders; and it came to pass that when the spirit rested upon them, they prophesied, but they did not continue" <note>Numbers 11:25</note>.

<margin>And passed</margin> "And the Lord passed before his face and called..." etc. "Keeping mercy for thousands..." etc. "And You shall forgive our iniquity..." etc. "For with You is forgiveness..." etc. "O Lord hear, O Lord forgive, O Lord attend..." etc. "And Jacob went out from Beersheba..." etc. "And he lighted upon a certain place and tarried there..." etc. "And he dreamed, and behold a ladder set up..." etc. "And behold, the Lord stood above it... and your seed shall be as the dust of the earth, and you shall spread abroad..." etc. "And behold, I am with you and will guard you wherever you go..." etc. <note>These are citations of Exodus 34:6-7, Psalm 130:4, Daniel 9:19, and Genesis 28:10-15. The practitioner is expected to know the full verses.</note> You must say all these verses with deep intention <term>kavanah|focused meditative state</term>, while your face is directed downward, and you will see your desire fulfilled.

<margin>This is the Explicit Name</margin> This is the Explicit Name <term>Shem HaMephorash|the hidden, articulated name of God</term> which Moses, our teacher (peace be upon him), mentioned at the Burning Bush. Anyone who mentions it against a Prince, he will flee; against fire, it will be extinguished; against illness, it will be healed; and against a "possession" <note>original: הרווית, likely referring to a spirit or demonic infestation</note>, it will be released. If a person remembers a part of it and writes it against an enemy, he will die; and [if written] for a ruler, he will love him. However, do not read it unless you are pure and clean from all ritual impurity, etc. Whoever reads it and is not pure and clean will die—take heed! And thus you must say: "I decree upon you, Haniel, Chasdiel, and Tzadkiel, by this Name, that you do for me such and such." It is the Name that emerges from the three verses: *Vayisa* ("And he moved"), *Vayavo* ("And he came"), and *Vayet* ("And he stretched") <note>Exodus 14:19-21. Each verse contains 72 letters</note>. One interweaves them with one another: the beginning, the end, and the beginning. This is the honorable and awesome Name, and it is explained as seventy-two names, which are these:

VHU YLY SYT ALM MHSh LLH AKA KHTh HZY ALD LAV HH'A YZL MBA HRY HQM LAV KLY LVV PHL
NLK YY MLA ChHV NThH HAA YRT ShAH RYY AVM LKB VShR YHV LHCh KVQ MND ANY Ch'AM VRH'A YYZ
HHH MYK VVL YLH SAL ARY ASShL MYH VHV DNY HChSh AMM NNA NYTh MBA PVY NMM YYL HRCh MTzR
VMB YHH ANV MChY DMB MNQ AY'A ChBV RAH YBM HYY MVM

Blessed be the Name of His glorious kingdom forever and ever.

<image-desc>
A sequence of magical or amuletic diagrams consisting of eight triangular structures arranged in two horizontal rows. Each triangle contains words from the Song of Songs (Chapter 7) or related liturgical/kabbalistic texts, written in a diminishing "step-down" pattern (removing the initial letter in each successive line).

Top Row (Left to Right):
1. A large triangle for the word "כארגמן" (Like Purple), reducing down to "ן".
2. A dual-triangle structure: the left part for "ראשך" (Your head) reducing to "ד"; the right part for "ודלת" (And the hair) reducing to "ת".
3. A central triangle for the word "ככרמל" (Like Carmel) reducing to "ל".
4. A dual-triangle structure: the left part for "עליך" (Upon you) reducing to "ך"; the right part for "ראשך" (Your head) reducing to "ד".

Bottom Row (Left to Right):
1. A triangle for "מור" (Myrrh) reducing to "ר".
2. A triangle for "טום" (Purity/Tum) reducing to "ם".
3. A triangle for "טור" (Mountain/Row) reducing to "ר".
4. A complex triple-triangle structure: the right part for "מלך" (King) reducing to "ד"; the middle for "אסור" (Bound) reducing to "ר"; the left for "ברהטים" (In the tresses) reducing down to "ם".

<summary>This page contains a powerful adjuration of the angel Metatron and the seasonal rulers, followed by instructions for using the 72-letter Name of God (Shem HaMephorash). It includes strict warnings about ritual purity and provides geometric amulets based on verses from the Song of Songs.</summary>

--- Page 101 ---
<meta>This page continues the instructional portion of the Book of Raziel, shifting from general angelic adjurations to specific practical rituals (theurgy). It focuses heavily on "The Opening of the Heart" (Ptihat HaLev), a category of rituals designed to improve memory, understanding, and the retention of Torah study. These rituals often involve consuming "encoded" foods—items like wine, eggs, or cakes inscribed with holy names.</meta>

# The Book of Raziel

On the New Moon of Nisan <note>The first month of the Hebrew calendar, occurring in the spring</note>, say over a cup of old wine seven times these names: Phatiel, Raphael, Anael, Yahu, Yahu, Tarphiel, Yehoel; and afterward, drink it.

<margin>Another method</margin>
Say forty times: "How I love Your Torah! It is my meditation all the day" <note>Psalm 119:97</note> and so on. Afterward, write it upon an etrog <term>etrog|a citron fruit used in Jewish ritual</term> or an apple. Then, return to saying it as mentioned above more than three times, and afterward eat it. This is the law of its writing.

<margin>Another method</margin>
I adjure you, Galtzio, the Prince of the Heights who provides the times of the Torah; and I adjure you, Yefifiya, the Prince of the Face <note>Yefifiya is the angelic tutor of Moses</note>; I adjure Ganzgal, the Prince of the Torah, that you shall open the heart of [Name] son of [Mother's Name] to the Torah, to wisdom, and to understanding, in the name of Yah, Yah, Yah, Yah, Lord of Hosts, Amen, Amen, Amen, Selah.

<margin>Another method</margin>
Take a virgin egg <note>An egg laid on a hen's first day of laying</note> and boil it in fire and peel it. Afterward, write these letters upon it: *PY PY WT PY PY YWT HYL PY PY YWT ABWT HYL TZWR YShW TATYT* <note>These appear to be mystical permutations or abbreviations of divine names</note>. Holy, Holy, Amen, Amen, Amen, Selah.

<margin>Another method</margin>
In the name and the number of the scribe, *Abad Pas-pas-el Amsar* the Great: Open my heart, David son of Zlata, to learn Torah; and all that I learn, may I not forget from now and forever, Amen, Amen, Amen, Selah.

<margin>Another method</margin>
The primary time for performing this is on the New Moon of Sivan and the Eve of Shavuot <note>The holiday celebrating the giving of the Torah</note>, even though one does not eat it until the next day. However, one who engages in this on every New Moon will not lose out. It must be done with a clean body and heart, and with an intention toward Heaven. This is the procedure for its preparation: take barley flour, and if barley is not found, take wheat flour—though barley is better. Knead it in purity and say: "For the sake of [Name], I engage in this. May it be the will before our God in Heaven that I succeed in this 'Opening of the Heart' which I am preparing for [Name] son of [Mother's Name], so that his heart may be open to the study of Torah and to all things, and that he may not forget anything he learns or knows, by the decree of the Watchers <term>Watchers|a class of high-ranking angels</term> and the word of the Holy Ones, requested in the name of Yah, the Lord of Hosts." One should knead the flour well and make it into fine flour. From this fine flour, make a cake one handbreadth by one handbreadth, circular in shape. Take a stylus and make four square markings in the cake like this, in the middle of the cake, and write inside the square these three names. They should be written in a thick, clear script, one above the other; do not write them in a single line, nor in two, but in these three lines. On the back of the cake, corresponding to the square already made, make another square like this and make six markings, writing within them these eight names, one above the other, each name on its own in thick script. After writing all the names on both sides of the cake, bake the cake...

| Arimas |
| :---: |
| Abrimas |
| Armimas |

<margin>
Examine,
Establish,
and Do Good
</margin>
| Asiel |
| :---: |
| Ansiel |
| Anchiphiel |
| and Phatiel |
| and Phata |

...in an oven, and heat that oven with wood from grapevines. After it is baked, take a cup of red wine and take five
<margin>Examine, Establish, and Do Good</margin>
leaves of myrtle. Write upon them the eight names written above in the square; write each name on a single myrtle leaf separately. Erase the writing into the red wine <note>by dipping the leaves in the wine until the ink dissolves</note> and place the leaves themselves into the cup of wine. One should not drink until saying seven times: "Ada bar Pappa, Rafram bar Pappa, Rami bar Pappa, Yakir bar Pappa, Surhav bar Pappa, Rahu bar Pappa, Hama bar Pappa, Achi bar Pappa, Nachman bar Pappa, Mari bar Pappa <note>These are the ten sons of the Talmudic sage Rav Pappa; reciting their names is a traditional Jewish folk-remedy for memory</note>. Blessed are You, O Lord, teach me Your statutes. My soul is parched <note>or 'yearning'</note>, and from the beautiful ones <note>referring to the names or angels</note> they shall rouse me. You are good and do good. Blessed are You, O Lord, Who is good and does good. I adjure you, Putah <note>The Angel of Forgetfulness</note>, Prince of Oblivion, that you depart from me, from my foolish heart, and cast it upon the high mountains, in the name of these holy names: Arimas, Abrimas, Armimas,
->Asiel, Ansiel, Anchiphiel, Phatiel, and Phata.<-
And Noah found favor in the eyes of the Lord" <note>Genesis 6:8</note>. Say this six more times, and afterward:
->Eat that cake. [Blessed be the Lord forever, Amen]<-

---

<margin>Amulet</margin>
Good, tested, and proven for the Evil Eye, for an evil spirit, for sickness, for imprisonment, for the sword, for the "Opening of the Heart," for preaching Torah in public, for all kinds of illnesses and calamities, and to prevent one from losing his wealth. In the name of Shaddai <term>Shaddai|Almighty, a name of God</term> who created the heavens and the earth; and in the name of the Angel Raphael, who is appointed over the month; and for you: Samamel, Haganel, Vengashorsh, Kendorum, Gadmah, Kamiel, Saariel, Abrir, Gurir—the ministers over the summer solstice <note>Tekufat Tammuz</note>; and for the Prince of Peace, Oranir, the angel of the year and the zodiac. In the name of the Lord, God of Israel, who dwells among the Cherubim, the Great, Mighty, and Awesome God, the Lord of Hosts is His name; and in Your name, O Merciful God; and in the name of Adiriron, the God who is a faithful healer, in whose hand is the entire assembly of the worlds below and above. In the name of the Lord, make me successful in the writing of this amulet, to be written in the name of [Name] son of [Mother's Name], to guard him in all his 248 limbs from imprisonment and from the double-edged sword. May they help him, save him, rescue him, and deliver him from an evil man, from an evil tongue, and from a harsh litigant—whether he is a member of the Covenant <note>a Jew</note> or not a member of the Covenant. And all who rise against him to do him harm—whether by their deeds, their tongue, their advice, or their thoughts—subdue them, humble them, overthrow them, make them corpses, humble and shake them, break and make them corpses without rising. May all who seek his harm be shamed, and all who desire his misfortune be disgraced. Save him and rescue him from every "evil eye" <note>transliterated as *bishoff* or *bi-shuf*</note> and all types of calamities, from poverty, from base men, from a strange death, from all types of the "Evil Inclination," and from all types of male spirits and illnesses.

<margin>By permission</margin>
In the Name, the honored and awesome, I adjure you, **Ashmedai** <note>Also known as Asmodeus, the king of demons in Jewish and Near Eastern folklore</note>, king of the spirits, and all your cohort; and **Agrat bat Mahlat** <note>A powerful female demon often described as a queen of the "spirits of the air" or succubi</note>, and all your cohort, that you shall not harm, nor frighten, nor terrify so-and-so, son of so-and-so <note>The practitioner would insert the name of the client and their mother here</note>. Rather, assist him and save him from every trouble and distress, and from every evil occurrence, and from every illness that enters the 248 limbs <note>Traditional Jewish anatomy counts 248 bones/limbs and 365 sinews, totaling the 613 parts of the body</note>, in the name of the "Four Keys" of *Y-V-H-N*: *Phat-Chai-Yo-Yo-H-N-S-N-V-Yn*. "May the Lord bless you and keep you; may the Lord make His face shine upon you and be gracious to you; *Pipsasayim* <note>A mystical name derived from the letters of the Priestly Blessing</note>; may the Lord lift up His countenance toward you and give you peace" <note>Numbers 6:24-26</note>. I adjure you, holy and pure King, that you shall uproot and cut off every fever—hot or cold—and every demon and destroyer, and every prohibition, knot, and bond, and all sorceries and all witchcrafts from this so-and-so, son of so-and-so, in the name of *Dihon Diaon She-Hu Shemot Mifsha Bishmi*, in the name of *Sh-L* engraved upon the Crown: *A-A-A S-S-S*.

<margin>Every person</margin>
who wishes to write an amulet must first write the angels appointed over the *Tekufah* <term>Tekufah|One of the four seasons of the solar year, marking the equinoxes and solstices</term> as follows:
The Season of Nisan <note>Spring</note>: Samael, Anzael, and Ganeshrish; and the name of their ministers is Or-Panir.
The Season of Tammuz <note>Summer</note>: Kadmiel, Tzadkiel, and Abaniel; and the name of their ministers is Abel Akhem.
The Season of Tishrei <note>Autumn</note>: Barkiel, Ormariel, and Gabriel; and the name of their ministers is Albri-Or.
The Season of Tevet <note>Winter</note>: Gabriel, Uriel, and Barkiel; and the name of their ministers is Raviel.
The seasons according to another version: Nisan: Uriel; Iyar: Amriel; Sivan: Tzofniel; Tammuz: Tiel; Av: Barkiel; Elul: Paniel; Tishrei: Tzuriel; Cheshvan: Keviel; Kislev: Adniel; Tevet: Tzofiel; Shevat: Yeriel; Adar: Sumiel; and for Adar also Sandalphon <note>Sandalphon is a prominent archangel in Kabbalah associated with prayers</note>.

<margin>Day 1</margin>
Sunday: Ariel; Monday: Aspasmiel; Tuesday: Ariel; Wednesday: Hakaviel; Thursday: Yehoiel; Friday: Hadriel; Saturday: Israel.
The Season of Tishrei: Galitzur and Hadriel. The Season of Tevet: Badzal and Samamiel. The Season of Nisan: Samiel and Anzael. The Season of Tammuz: Namtziel and Tzadkiel.

<margin>The Book of Mazalot</margin>
## The Book of the Zodiac Signs

Nisan: its sign is Aries <note>The Ram</note> and Uriel is the angel.
Iyar: its sign is Taurus <note>The Bull</note> and the angel is Lehatiel.
Sivan: its sign is Gemini <note>The Twins</note> and the angel is Paniel.
Tammuz: its sign is Cancer <note>The Crab</note> and the angel is Zuriel.
Av: its sign is Leo <note>The Lion</note> and the angel is Barkiel.
Elul: its sign is Virgo <note>The Maiden</note> and the angel is Haniel.
Tishrei: its sign is Libra <note>The Scales</note> and the angel is Tzuriel.
Cheshvan: its sign is Scorpio <note>The Scorpion</note> and the angel is Gabriel.
Kislev: its sign is Sagittarius <note>The Archer</note> and the angel is Meroniel.
Tevet: its sign is Capricorn <note>The Goat</note> and the angel is Sheniel.
Shevat: its sign is Aquarius <note>The Water-bearer</note> and the angel is Gabriel.
Adar: its sign is Pisces <note>The Fish</note> and the angel is Rumiel.

<margin>Day 1</margin>
Sunday: its star is the Sun and the angel is Uriel.
Monday: its star is the Moon and the angel is Samael.
Tuesday: its star is Mars and the angel is Viruel.
Wednesday: its star is Mercury and the angel is Lehaviel.
Thursday: its star is Jupiter and Viruel is the angel.
Friday: its star is Venus and the angel is Ashiel.
Saturday: its star is Saturn and the angel is Ashriel (another version: Israel).

<margin>Season</margin>
Nisan: Samael. Tammuz: Namtziel and Tzadkiel. Tishrei: Yokmiel and Yarmut. Tevet: Barkiel and Samamiel.
The Angels: *T-A-Q* <note>Aries, Leo, Sagittarius</note> were created from fire and are male; their service is in the East. *Sh-B-G* <note>Taurus, Virgo, Capricorn</note> were created from earth and are male; their service is in the South. *Ch-M-D* <note>Gemini, Libra, Aquarius</note> were created from air and are male; their service is in the West. *S-A-R* <note>Cancer, Scorpio, Pisces</note> were created from water and are female; their service is in the North.
The Sun is in Leo by day. Venus is in Taurus by day and Libra by night. Mercury is in Gemini by day and Virgo by night. The Moon is in Cancer by day. Saturn is in Capricorn by day and Aquarius by night. Jupiter is in Sagittarius by day and Pisces by night. Mars is in the "horns of Aries" by day and the "sting of Scorpio" by night. Of the twelve zodiac signs: *T-A-Q* and *Ch-M-D* are male; *Sh-B-G* and *S-A-R* are female. And a male child, if born in a female sign, will not live; and a female child, if born in a male sign, she also will not live <note>This reflects a medieval belief in the necessity of gender harmony between the person and their celestial sign</note>.

---

<margin>And these</margin>
are the hours of the days of the week that are suitable for writing amulets: Sunday: 7th hour; Monday: 5th hour; Tuesday: 1st hour; Wednesday: 2nd hour; Thursday: 4th hour; Friday: 5th and 10th hours. The mnemonic sign for these hours is *Z-H A-B-D H-V*.

<margin>And these</margin>
are the days of the month which are suitable for writing amulets: 1st: all day; 2nd: morning; 3rd: nothing; 4th: all day; 5th: morning; 6th: nothing; 7th: morning; 8th: morning; 9th: nothing; 10th: nothing; 11th: morning; 12th: all day; 13th: nothing; 14th: morning; 15th: nothing; 16th: morning; 17th: evening; 18th: nothing; 19th: nothing; 20th: morning; 21st: morning; 22nd: all day; 23rd: nothing; 24th: morning; 25th: all day; 26th: nothing; 27th: morning; 28th: all day; 29th: nothing; 30th: morning.

---

<margin>And so</margin>
<margin>Opening of the heart</margin>
The one writing the amulet should say: "In the name of the One who created heaven and earth, I write this amulet for so-and-so son of so-and-so, and in the name of the angel appointed over the hour of such-and-such day." This is what Moses our teacher (peace be upon him) did for Joshua: write it upon the leaves of an etrog <term>etrog|a citron fruit</term>, dissolve it in old wine, and drink it on the New Moon. And these are the names: *RD TBA YD TIHV BVTA AAA NS*.

For the protection of a woman in labor and her child, and against sorcery and the evil eye, and for the hour of birth so that no demon or evil occurrence may hold power over her or the child: "A Song of Ascents" <note>original: *Shir Ha-Ma'alot*, Psalm 121</note>, etc. The Holy Names that emerge from "A Song of Ascents": "I will lift up my eyes" <note>original: *Esa Einai*</note>. The initial letters equal 72 <note>The number 72 refers to the *Shem Ha-Mephorash*, a potent 72-fold name of God</note>; this is the Name of the Lord <note>original: *Y-H-V-H*</note> when spelled out with the letter *Yod*. Likewise, the final letters of "my eyes to the mountains, from whence" <note>original: *Einai el he-harim me-ayin*</note> equal 130; this refers to the Lord "who rides upon the clouds" <note>The Hebrew word for "cloud" (*Av*) has the value 72, and the word for "rides" combined with it relates to the value 130</note>. Also, the final letters of "the mountains, from whence shall come" <note>original: *he-harim me-ayin yavo*</note> signify the names of the Lord <note>original: *Adonai* and *Y-H-V-H*</note> interwoven. "My help comes from the Lord, the Maker" <note>Psalm 121:2</note>—the final letters equal *Adonai*. "Heaven and earth"—the final letters equal 130. "Behold, He who keeps Israel shall neither slumber..."—the initial letters are the Name of the Lord spelled out with the letter *Aleph*. This is the Name. "The sun shall not strike you by day"—the three *Yods* come from the 72-letter Name, which is entirely mercy, to awaken the mercies of the "Higher Adam" <note>original: *Adam Elyon*, a mystical configuration of the Divine</note>. "The sun shall not strike you"—the initial letters are the Name of the Lord spelled out with the letter *Aleph*. Every letter after the Name of the Lord is an acronym for "Maker of Heaven and Earth." This Name is very good for protection. Every person should recite this "Song of Ascents" and meditate on this Name every night before sleep.

And afterwards, "And he passed over the ford of Jabbok" <note>Genesis 32:23. The word *Yabbok* (J-B-Q) has the numerical value of 112, which represents the combination of the names *Y-H-V-H*, *Adonai*, and *Ehyeh*</note>. "And he passed" <note>original: *Vayavor*</note> relates to the 72 Names, for it contains the 216 letters <note>3 letters per name x 72 names = 216</note>. *Yabbok* represents the names *Adonai*, *Y-H-V-H*, and *Ehyeh* interwoven. And afterwards, recite the 72-letter Name as you find it on page 24, column 2. Then the verse "May the Lord bless you..." <note>The Priestly Blessing, Numbers 6:24</note> and the second verse "May the Lord answer you in the day of trouble..." <note>Psalm 20:2</note>. From this Psalm, the name *Yabbok* emerges four times: "In the day of trouble" <note>original: *Be-yom tzarah*</note>, "May He strengthen you from Zion" <note>original: *yisgabcha... kodesh*</note>, "with the saving strength" <note>original: *bigvurot yesha*</note>, and "In the day we call" <note>original: *be-yom korenu*</note>. These four acronyms for *Yabbok* are the three names *Adonai*, *Y-H-V-H*, and *Ehyeh* interwoven, and these four powers are a remedy <note>term: *Segulah* — a spiritual charm or remedy</note> for every single trouble, to be saved from it, and specifically for a woman in labor.

And afterwards: "Behold, the Lord rides upon a swift cloud" <note>Isaiah 19:1</note>. The names emerging from this verse are very good for protection, so that no sorcery may cause harm. "Behold" <note>original: *Hineh*</note> has the numerical value of "Vessel" <note>original: *Keli*</note>; this is a name from the 72-letter Name to break every sorcery, for no sorcery can pass through except by way of a "vessel." The 72-letter Name breaks all the "husks" <note>term: *Kelipot* — kabbalistic term for forces of evil or impurity that "shell" the divine light</note>, as is known. And afterwards, recite the verse "For Your salvation I hope, O Lord" <note>Genesis 49:18</note>. Every three letters is a name in its own right. Then "Blessed be the Name of His glorious kingdom forever and ever."

Then: "Joseph is a fruitful bough, a fruitful bough by a well; his branches run over the wall" <note>Genesis 49:22, a classic verse used against the Evil Eye</note>. By the name *Y-H-V-H* spelled out... <note>The text lists series of letter permutations: *M-Y-K, Sh-R, N-Y-T, A-Sh-L, G-M-S, R-H-A*</note>. These names are very good against the Evil Eye and emerge from the verse "A fruitful bough" <note>original: *Ben Porat*</note>. The proof for this is that the acronym *A-Sh-L* <note>meaning "Tamarisk tree"</note> equals the numerical value of "Evil Eye" <note>original: *Ayin Ra*</note>, and likewise *G-M-S R-H-A* equals "The Evil Eye."

Then the seventy names of angels—these are very good for all forms of protection, as is known. And afterwards, these names and their illustrations as Adam the First Man saw and engraved them; they are very good for the protection of the woman in labor and the child:

Inside the hexagram is the command "Go out, you and all the people at your feet" followed by protective letter permutations (Q-V-P, Q-P-V, V-Q-P, etc.). Between the points of the star are acronyms from the Ana Be-Koach prayer (Heal us, accept our prayer, etc.). The hexagram is surrounded by circular borders containing the names of the four rivers of Paradise: Pishon, Gihon, Hiddekel, and Euphrates. The top arc of the circle reads "Adam and Eve—Lilith be gone!—The first." On either side of the main circle are names of protective angels including Shamriel (Guardian of God), Chasdiel (Kindness of God), Sinui, and Sansanui (the legendary angels who protect against Lilith).</image-desc>

For a woman having difficulty giving birth: Take a piece of kosher deer parchment and write upon it the name: *QVP QPV VQP VPQ PQV PVQ*. Tie it upon her navel and whisper into her right ear: "Go out, you and all the people at your feet" <note>Exodus 11:8</note>. Tested and proven.

[By the name of] the Princes of the Torah and in the name of *V-P-T Z-A-Y B-T-A R-B-B Tz-R-R Y-H*... <note>Further mystical acronyms follow</note> ...that you shall open the heart of **Leib son of Sarah** to learn the Torah, the Prophets, and the Writings <note>The Tanakh or Hebrew Bible</note>, and the Jerusalem Talmud, and the Babylonian Talmud, and the Midrash, and the *Tosafot*, and the *Sifra*, and the *Sifrei* <note>These are the foundational texts of Rabbinic law and lore</note>, and the depths of the Torah and its secrets. May his heart be like a flowing spring, and may the Lord be with him, and may his intellect and all the orders of wisdom and all words of Torah be [with him]. Guard him, save him, rescue him, and deliver him from all types of sorcery, and from the "Evil Eye" <note>original: *chaphita*</note>, and from all types of misfortune, and from all types of suffering, and from the double-edged sword, and from an evil man, and from an evil tongue, and from a harsh litigant—whether he is a "son of the covenant" <term>a fellow Jew</term> or whether he is not a "son of the covenant." And all those who rise against him for evil, whether in action or in thought—subdue them and humble them, strike them and smite them, and break without any healing <note>literal: "without the rising of a limb"</note> all those who seek his harm. Let those who desire his ruin be put to shame. Save him and rescue him from all types of misfortune, and from poverty, and from worthless men <note>original: *anshei beli-ya'al*</note>, and from an unnatural death, and from all types of the "Evil Inclination" <note>original: *Yetzer HaRa*</note>, and from evil diseases, and empower a "good heart" within him over an "evil heart." And let his fear be upon all creatures like the fear of a predator over a lion. In the name of: *Y-H-V-H, Y-H-V-H, Y-H-V-H, Y-H-V-H, H-V-Y-H, H-V-Y-H...* <note>Various permutations of the Tetragrammaton follow</note>. In the name of *A-B-G Y-T-Tz, K-R-A S-T-N* <note>The 42-letter name continues, including a plea to "tear away the Adversary" (Satan)</note>... and in the name of the 72 names... and in the name of *Anaktam Pastam Paspasim Dionsim* <note>A common mystical name found in the Priestly Blessing</note>. "May the Lord bless you and keep you. May the Lord make His face shine upon you and be gracious to you. May the Lord lift up His countenance upon you and give you peace." <note>The Priestly Blessing from Numbers 6:24-26</note>

Hear, O Israel, [the one who] dwells in the secret place of the Most High <note>The text here begins to interweave the *Shema* (Deuteronomy 6:4) with Psalm 91, a psalm of protection</note>, the Lord is our God, in the shadow of the Almighty he shall abide. [The Lord is One]. I will say of the Lord, He is my refuge, my God in whom I trust, [And you shall love] with all your heart, and with all your soul, and with all your might. For He shall deliver you from the snare of the fowler and from the noisome pestilence. These words which I command you today shall be upon your heart. He shall cover you with His pinions, and under His wings you shall find refuge; His truth is a shield and a buckler. You shall teach them diligently to your children and speak of them when you sit in your house, when you walk by the way, when you lie down, and when you rise. You shall not be afraid of the terror by night, nor of the arrow that flies by day. Bind them as a sign upon your hand and they shall be as frontlets between your eyes. A thousand shall fall at your side and ten thousand at your right hand, but it shall not come near to you, **Leib son of Sarah**. Write them upon the doorposts of your house and upon your gates. No destroyer shall approach; the Lord shall guard him from all spirits, destroyers, night-demons, and evil ghosts, and from all fears. In the name of the Lord, the God of gods and Lord of lords, the Great God... Blessed are You, O Lord.

[This is done] by the power of this Psalm and the power of these names: "Bless the Lord, O my soul. O Lord my God, You are very great; You are clothed in glory and majesty, wrapped in light as with a garment, stretching out the heavens like a curtain. He lays the beams of His upper chambers in the waters; He makes the clouds His chariot; He walks upon the wings of the wind. He makes the winds His messengers, flaming fire His ministers. He established the earth on its foundations, so that it shall never be shaken. You covered it with the deep as with a garment; the waters stood above the mountains. At Your rebuke they fled; at the sound of Your thunder they hurried away. They went up the mountains, they went down the valleys to the place that You established for them. You set a boundary that they may not pass over, so that they may not return to cover the earth. He sends forth springs into the valleys; they flow between the mountains. They give drink to every beast of the field; the wild donkeys quench their thirst. Beside them the birds of the heavens dwell; they sing among the branches. He waters the mountains from His upper chambers; the earth is satisfied with the fruit of Your works. He causes grass to grow for the cattle, and vegetation for the service of man, to bring forth bread from the earth, and wine that gladdens the heart of man, to make his face shine with oil, and bread which sustains the heart of man. The trees of the Lord are satisfied, the cedars of Lebanon which He planted, where the birds make their nests; as for the stork, the fir trees are her house. The high mountains are for the wild goats; the rocks are a refuge for the hyraxes. He made the moon for the seasons; the sun knows its setting. You make darkness, and it is night, in which all the beasts of the forest creep. The young lions roar after their prey and seek their food from God. When the sun rises, they gather themselves together and lie down in their dens. Man goes out to his work and to his labor until the evening. O Lord, how manifold are Your works! In wisdom You have made them all; the earth is full of Your creatures. This is the sea, great and broad, where there are creeping things without number, living creatures both small and great. There the ships go, and Leviathan, which You formed to play in it. They all look to You, to give them their food in its season. You give it to them, they gather it; You open Your hand, they are satisfied with good. You hide Your face, they are troubled; You take away their breath, they die and return to their dust. You send forth Your spirit, they are created, and You renew the face of the ground. May the glory of the Lord endure forever; may the Lord rejoice in His works. He looks on the earth, and it trembles; He touches the mountains, and they smoke. I will sing to the Lord as long as I live; I will sing praise to my God while I have my being. Let my meditation be pleasing to Him; as for me, I will rejoice in the Lord. Let sinners be consumed from the earth, and let the wicked be no more. Bless the Lord, O my soul. Hallelujah." <note>This is the full text of Psalm 104, often used in Jewish liturgy and folk-magic to represent the harmony of nature and divine providence</note>.

# The Book of Raziel

<margin>**Tear Satan**</margin> And he said: I adjure you, Nahaliel, Amoniel, Tapzaphiya, Atzatztiyah, Narayiel, by this Name that brings forth truth from a flowing spring before me:
The letters of the phrase "Small Gate" <note>original: "Sha’ar Katan"</note> exist above, and through it, Satan enters to accuse Israel. Immediately, the "Holy Living Creatures" <term>Chayot HaKodesh; the highest order of angels in Ezekiel's vision</term> see him and strike their wings against the gate so that he cannot come before the Divine Presence to accuse Israel. They mention the merit of Jacob, who is called "small," as it is said: "How can Jacob stand? For he is small" <note>Amos 7:5</note>. And of this, it is said: "Righteousness and justice are the foundation of Your throne; kindness and truth precede Your face" <note>Psalm 89:15</note>. The final letters of this verse spell "Small" <note>original: "Katan"</note>, and everything is accomplished through this Name.

<margin>**Against the Dry**</margin> "Tear Satan" <note>A phrase formed by the second line of the 42-letter Name of God</note>—it is good for one whose mind is troubled, whether by an evil spirit or by a demon or damaging force. One writes this Name and hangs it around their neck, and they shall be healed.
And why are lashes given below as thirty-nine <note>standard judicial lashes in Jewish law</note> and above as sixty? Because it is said: "Lo, all these things God works, twice and three times with a man" <note>Job 33:29</note>. When a person is thirteen years old <term>the age of Bar Mitzvah</term>, they become liable for punishment below, and if they are lashed three times thirteen, it reaches the count of thirty-nine. But above <term>in the Heavenly Court</term>, they do not punish until a person is twenty years old. Three times twenty is sixty. The word "Gardener" <note>original: "Negad," which are the letters in the margin</note> has the numerical value <term>Gematria</term> of "Nourisher" <note>original: "Zon"</note>, which is good to mention for livelihood, sustenance, and support.

<margin>**B-T-R Tz-T-G**</margin> Some read this as "Peter," meaning "to release," as in "The beginning of strife is like releasing water" <note>Proverbs 17:14</note>, and some read "Katan" <note>Small</note> as "Kera" <note>Tear</note>, certainly so for adjuration, or they read "Tear Satan, Peter Tz-T-G." But for other adjurations, they read it with the letter *Shin*. The numerical value of the word "Betar" <note>B-T-R</note> is the same as the word "These" <note>original: "Eleh"</note> when its letters are spelled out fully <term>Milui; a kabbalistic method of calculating letters by their names</term>. The value of "Tz-T-G" is equal to "Snows" <note>original: "Talgot"</note>, for when prayer ascends above, it is like what is said: "Looking through the windows, peering through the 
<margin>**lattices •**</margin> lattices." The word "Looking" <note>original: "Mashgiach"</note> is an acronym for Metatron, the Great Prince. He opens his window—and there are thirteen windows in the Throne of Glory—and he opens them all so that the prayers of Israel may enter. Everything is done through this Name.

<margin>**B-T-R Tz-T-G**</margin> In the Atbash cipher <term>a substitution cipher where the first letter is replaced by the last, second by second-to-last, etc.</term>, these letters become "Sh-G-N H-A-R," teaching that the Primordial Light that the Holy One, Blessed be He, created, He set aside for the righteous in the future and hid it. It is the Garden of Eden <note>Gan Eden</note>, as it is said: "And God called to the lights." The word "Light" <note>Or</note> has the numerical value of "To the Garden of Eden," and it is accessed through this Name.

<margin>**Ch-K-D T-N-A**</margin> The numerical value of this is "Re’em" <note>a powerful biblical horned animal</note>, for it is said: "And his horns are the horns of a wild ox; with them he shall gore the nations" <note>Deuteronomy 33:17</note>. "Re’em" <note>R-A-M</note> also stands for Raphael, Uriel, and Michael; they are the camps of the Divine Presence, and they are destined to help the Messiah. This is what is meant by the verse: "He had rays [horns] coming out of His hand; and there was the hiding of His power" <note>Habakkuk 3:4</note>. These letters also contain the Name "Y-H-H." Regarding Uriel, it means "The face of Uriel shall not be seen," as it says: "You shall see my back, but my face shall not be seen."

<margin>**Ch-K-D T-N-A**</margin> The numerical value is "Gazriel," who is an angel. One adjures this angel, Gazriel, to nullify a bad decree <note>gezeirah ra’ah</note> that was decreed. With this Name, one requires great purity and ritual immersion <term>Mikvah</term>.

<margin>**Y-G-L P-Z-K**</margin> The numerical value is "Like a Cherub" <note>Ke-Cherub</note>. This Name is engraved upon a Cherub above, and every single day the Holy One, Blessed be He, rides upon it, as it is said: "And He rode upon a Cherub and did fly; and He soared upon the wings of the wind" <note>Psalm 18:11</note>. He sees eighteen thousand worlds that are behind the East, and eighteen thousand behind the West, and eighteen thousand behind the North—these are seventy-two worlds, corresponding to the value of "Kindness" <note>Chesed = 72</note>, as it is said: "I said, the world shall be built with kindness" <note>Psalm 89:3</note>. These worlds number in the thousands of myriads, and regarding this, it is said: "It was eighteen thousand [measures] round about; and the name of the city from that day shall be, 'The Lord is there'" <note>Ezekiel 48:35</note>. This means eighteen thousand on each of the four sides. No angel is with Him except for the Angel of the Presence <term>Metatron</term>; he rides with Him in the clouds of glory, as it is said: "Walking before him" <note>original: "Holech Nechcho"</note>, the letters of which spell "Enoch" <note>who became Metatron</note>. At that time, He shows His glory to all the inhabitants of the upper world, and all the heavenly hosts sing a song and mention this Name.

<margin>**Y-G-L P-Z-K**</margin> When does this happen? At the time when the Holy One, Blessed be He, ascends the Throne of Glory for judgment. Immediately, all the heavenly hosts tremble, and when they see the Holy One, they take their crowns off their heads, fall from their horses, and pray and seek mercy for Israel. Immediately, He rises and sits upon the Throne of Mercy. Therefore, the Divine Presence resides on the Throne of Mercy. Thus, "Y-G-L P-Z-K" is upon the Throne of Glory. Understand all this well.

<margin>**Sh-K-V Tz-Y-T**</margin> The numerical value is "And upon the curtain" <note>original: "Ve-al ha-yeria"</note>. This teaches that this Name is engraved upon the curtain of the Throne. When the Evil Foe comes to accuse Israel, they immediately mention this Name, and immediately he is silenced <note>original: "Navu," likely meaning 'shuttered' or 'faded'</note> and the Evil Foe sees it and is not permitted to enter. Therefore, the value of "Sh-K-V" is "Over the Evil Inclination" <note>Ve-al Yetzer Ra</note>, meaning He is master over the evil impulse.

<margin>**Sh-K-V**</margin> The numerical value is "And upon Hadraniel." This teaches that the angel Hadraniel goes through everything; he takes the curtain spread over the Throne and immerses it in the River of Fire <term>Nehar Dinur</term> and spreads it over the Throne of Glory. Immediately, all the heavenly hosts gather to sing songs, and they too immerse themselves in the River of Fire three hundred and sixty-eight times, like "Gardens by a river." The word "By a river" <note>Al Nehar</note> has the value of 368. Then they take permission from our father Jacob, peace be upon him, and they say a song so that Jacob will not suffer from the voice of Gehenna <term>Purgatory/Hell</term>. Immediately they sing with this Name, for it is engraved upon their hearts.

<margin>**Sh-K-V Tz-Y-T**</margin> This is good for a person who wears sackcloth and puts ashes on their head; if they mention this Name and pray, their prayer is immediately heard and answered. We say we are not permitted to wear sackcloth unless we are answered like King Jehoram son of Ahab. They also said in the Midrash: "How beautiful is sackcloth," for as soon as the Holy One sees it, He immediately turns to the person. Understand this secret of why sackcloth <note>Sak</note> is so beautiful: the phrase "My desire is here" <note>Chishek Peh</note> contains the letters of "The sackcloth is beautiful" <note>Ha-Sak Yafeh</note>. You find this with Mordecai: "And he put on sackcloth and ashes and went out into the midst of the city" <note>Esther 4:1</note>, and similarly with the people of Nineveh: "And they put on sackcloth" <note>Jonah 3:5</note>. Understand this secret, for it is great.

---

Here ends the commentary on the 42-letter Name, and here ends the Book of the Spring of Wisdom, the traditions of the great Raziel, and all the books that the angel Raziel gave to Adam the First Man to learn from them all the springs of wisdom. May the Lord, in His mercy, lead us in the pleasantness of His eternity, and may our desire succeed by His hand and in the path of His wisdom. His ministers and the myriads of His hosts, troops of flashing fire, rivers of fire sit before Him; from His majesty the angels shrink, and from His voice the thresholds shake. He shall lead us forever. Blessed be the Lord forever, Amen and Amen.

->By the worker engaged in the holy craft in faithfulness, helped by David, son of my lord and father, the honorable<-
->Issachar Dov, of blessed memory; from Lesser Poland, from the capital city of Valkovy near the holy community of Lviv.<-
